{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"「我曾遇一位来自古国的旅人，\n 他说：两条庞大无躯的石腿\n 矗立荒漠之中。近旁沙地上，\n 半埋着一张破碎的石脸，那蹙眉、\n 皱唇与冷傲的蔑笑，\n 表明雕刻者深知那些激情，\n 它们至今犹存，铭刻在这无生命的石上，\n 那嘲弄它们的手，那滋养它们的心。\n 基座上浮现这些字句：\n '吾乃万王之王奥兹曼迪亚斯，\n  功业盖世，强者折服！'\n 此外荡然无存：庞然废墟之周，\n 无边无垠，荒凉寂寥，\n 平沙漫漫，遥接天际。」\n    -珀西·比希·雪莱，《奥兹曼迪亚斯》。1818。","current_english":"“I met a traveller from an antique land\n Who said: Two vast and trunkless legs of stone\n Stand in the desert. Near them on the sand,\n Half sunk, a shatter'd visage lies, whose frown\n And wrinkled lip and sneer of cold command\n Tell that its sculptor well those passions read\n Which yet survive, stamp'd on these lifeless things,\n The hand that mock'd them and the heart that fed.\n And on the pedestal these words appear:\n ‘My name is Ozymandias, king of kings:\n Look on my works, ye Mighty, and despair!’\n Nothing beside remains: round the decay\n Of that colossal wreck, boundless and bare,\n The lone and level sands stretch far away.”\n    -Percy Bysshe Shelley, “Ozymandias”. 1818.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Dungeon features","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:100","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:95","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"a71868de7ed913b936a53b6f91a366bd7b6126e61500a6bef417094e586d718f","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:a granite statue","key":"a granite statue","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"a granite statue","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Dungeon features","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"「当你长久凝视深渊，深渊也在凝视你。」\n    -弗里德里希·尼采，《善恶的彼岸》，格言146。1886。\n\n「在我们那儿，」爱丽丝仍然微喘着说，「只要你跑得很快、跑很久，一般总会\n到达另一个地方。」「多么慢吞吞的国家！」红皇后说。「你看，在这里，你得\n拼命跑，才能待在原地。你若想到别的地方，至少得跑得比这样快两倍！」\n    -刘易斯·卡罗尔，《爱丽丝镜中奇遇记》，\n第二章“活花的花园”。1871。","current_english":"“And if you gaze for long into an abyss, the abyss gazes also into you.”\n    -Friedrich Nietzsche, _Beyond Good and Evil_ , Aphorism 146. 1886.\n\n“Well, in our country,” said Alice, still panting a little, “you'd generally\n get to somewhere else — if you ran very fast for a long time, as we've been\n doing.” “A slow sort of country!” said the Queen. “Now here, you see, it takes\n all the running you can do, to keep in the same place. If you want to get\n somewhere else, you must run at least twice as fast as that!”\n    -Lewis Carroll, _Through the Looking-Glass, and What Alice Found There_,\nch. 2 “The Garden of Live Flowers”. 1871.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Dungeon features","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:118","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:113","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"440947c7b6ba8287cfec98da99a3d55ed527400e7b39679ae22c5d1c1898e32b","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:a one-way gate to the infinite horrors of the abyss","key":"a one-way gate to the infinite horrors of the abyss","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"a one-way gate to the infinite horrors of the abyss","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Dungeon features","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"「我沉思鱼的神秘，它们奇异而无思虑的美，如何——天真而邪恶地——彼此\n捕食，吞噬弱小者，既不愤怒，也不呼喊，也不变色。在那里，水的领域中，\n对它们而言也是大地和天空，大鱼上下游弋，衰老而不枯朽，享受永恒的成长\n而无臃肿之柔弱。那是一个没有道德的世界，没有选择的世界，一个进食、产卵、\n壮大的世界。我羡慕大鱼，也羡慕（在其他更小的池塘里）那些小鱼，游弋闪烁，\n金光熠熠。\n\n 人们谈论'人心中的野兽'，谈论'丛林法则'。他们何不（我这么思忖着，\n漫步在如天坛琉璃瓦般白蓝相间的云天下），何不谈谈'人心中之鱼'？谈谈\n'海洋法则'？那片海，据说我们正是从中而来……？」\n    -Avram Davidson，《大衮》，1959。","current_english":"“I mused upon the mystery of fish, their strange and mindless beauty, how—\n innocently evil—they prey upon each other, devouring the weaker and smaller\n without rage or shout or change of countenance. There, in the realm of water,\n which is also earth and air to them, the great fish passed up and down,\n growing old without aging and enjoying eternal growth without the softness of\n obesity. It was a world without morality, a world without choices, a world of\n eating and spawning and growing great. I envied the great fish, and (in other,\n smaller ponds) the lesser fish, darting and flashing and sparkling gold.\n\n They speak of ‘the beast in man,’ and of ‘the law of the jungle.’ Might they\n not (so I reflected, strolling underneath a sky of clouds as blue and as white\n as the tiles and marble of the Altar of Heaven), might they not better speak\n of ‘the fish in man’? And of ‘the law of the sea’? The sea, from which they\n say we came...?”\n    -Avram Davidson, “Dagon”, 1959.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Dungeon features","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:131","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:124","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"5d758f06e2530b3979688db33fef1e2c1c285e3d1807c8793610e483eb2fb5d3","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:the open sea","key":"the open sea","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"the open sea","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Dungeon features","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"「他看见一个巨大的洞穴，拱顶高度与成人身材相当，凿于原生岩石之中，由\n岩石顶部气孔与圆窗透入的光线照亮。他本以为这贼窝中只有一片黑暗，却不料\n满室堆放着各种货物的包裹，从地面到穹顶，塞满骆驼驮载的丝绸、锦缎、刺绣\n织品，以及层层叠叠五彩斑斓的地毯；此外他还看到不计其数的金币银币，有的\n堆在地上，有的装在皮革袋囊之中。看到这些货物与钱财如此丰盛，阿里巴巴心\n中断定，这地方绝非短短数年，而是经历许多世代，盗贼们才将抢掠所得储藏于\n此。」\n    -《一千零一夜》。Richard F. Burton英译，1885。","current_english":"“He saw a large cavern and a vaulted [roof], in height equalling the stature\n of a full-grown man and it was hewn in the live stone and lighted up with\n light that came through air-holes and bullseyes in the upper surface of the\n rock which formed the roof. He had expected to find naught save outer gloom in\n this robbers' den, and he was surprised to see the whole room filled with\n bales of all manner stuffs, and heaped up from sole to ceiling with\n camel-loads of silks and brocades and embroidered cloths and mounds on mounds\n of vari-colored carpetings; besides which he espied coins golden and silvern\n without measure or account, some piled upon the ground and others bound in\n leathern bags and sacks. Seeing these goods and moneys in such abundance, Ali\n Baba determined in his mind that not during a few years only but for many\n generations thieves must have stored their gains and spoils in this place.”\n    -_The Arabian Nights_. trans. Sir Richard F. Burton, 1885.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Dungeon features","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:149","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:138","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"3f002c0ca354044d6f6fa717048613c6faa6b78136c429e5f425f03f14c50458","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:a portal to a secret trove of treasure","key":"a portal to a secret trove of treasure","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"a portal to a secret trove of treasure","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Dungeon features","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"「那个洞此刻从讲台边算起直径约有三米——不过它的轮廓像脉动的活物般微微\n收缩膨胀。透过它，我们看见形状翻涌变幻，随色彩与光芒不断生灭。紫色的树\n从粉红色的天空中倒着生长，变成黑色的花，花又变成巨型黄虫羽状的舌头，在\n震颤爆裂的沙漠上笨拙蠕动。仿佛这个世界还没决定自己要成为什么。只需看这\n景象片刻，我就头痛欲裂，若长久注视，人必定发疯。」\n    -Laurence Yep，《龙战》。1992。","current_english":"“The hole now stood some three meters in diameter beginning from the dais -\n though its outline shrank and dilated slightly like a pulsing, living thing.\n Through it we could see shapes seething and changing constantly with the\n colors and the light. Purple trees grew upside down from a pink sky, changing\n into black flowers and the flowers into feathery tongues of giant yellow worms\n lumbering over a shaking, exploding desert. It was as if the world couldn't\n make up its mind what to be yet. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"「我不知法律是否正确，\n  亦不知法律是否错误；\n 我们这些狱中人只知，\n  那高墙坚固无比；\n 每一日都如一年般漫长，\n  那年中的每一天都漫长如年。」\n    -奥斯卡·王尔德，《雷丁监狱之歌》。1898。","current_english":"“I know not whether Laws be right,\n   Or whether Laws be wrong;\n All that we know who lie in gaol\n   Is that the wall is strong;\n And that each day is like a year,\n   A year whose days are long.”\n    -Oscar Wilde, “The Ballad of Reading Gaol”. 1898.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Dungeon features","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:177","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:158","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"83a8fcf43ac87661aef771ecc28a412f1ca7d87d292f9c66b477d2d6dd72fe74","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:a rock wall","key":"a rock wall","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"a rock wall","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Dungeon features","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}

{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"「重力，对基督徒不过是小小不便，对教皇、异教徒和绝望者却是恐惧之源。\n对老鼠或任何更小的动物而言，它几乎不构成危险。你可以把一只老鼠扔进\n一千码深的矿井；只要地面还算柔软，它落到底只会受到轻微震击，然后走开。\n老鼠会被摔死，人会被摔碎，马则会溅开。」\n    -J.B.S.霍尔丹，《论合适的体型》。1926年3月。","current_english":"“Gravity, a mere nuisance to Christian, was a terror to Pope, Pagan, and\n Despair. A rat is killed, a man is broken, a\n horse splashes.”\n    -J.B.S. Haldane, “On Being The Right Size”. March 1926.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Dungeon features","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:196","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:175","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"5ae90a25dea0b5adf82f92cacdf0ff46cd689c6e27909cc1660772af568c4fe2","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:a shaft","key":"a shaft","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":"「重力，对基督徒不过是小小不便，对教皇、异教徒和绝望者却是恐惧之源。\n对小鼠或任何更小的动物而言，它几乎不构成危险。你可以把一只小鼠扔进\n一千码深的矿井；只要地面还算柔软，它落到底只会受到轻微震击，然后走开。\n大鼠会被摔死，人会被摔碎，马则会溅开。」\n    -J.B.S.霍尔丹，《论合适的体型》。1926年3月。","reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["保留现译；会继续留下已由英文正文或当前术语直接证明的偏差。"],"resolved_key":"a shaft","reviewer_rationale":"修复 mouse/rat 的物种对照：前两处 mouse 为“小鼠”，结句 rat 为“大鼠”；原译把三处都译成“老鼠”，抹去了引文用体型形成的递进。","section":"Dungeon features","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"adjust"}

{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"「我喜欢树，因为它们比其他生灵更安于自己的生存方式。」\n    -薇拉·凯瑟，《啊，拓荒者！》。1913。","current_english":"“I like trees because they seem more resigned to the way they have to live than\n other things do.”\n    -Willa Cather, _O Pioneers!_. 1913.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Dungeon features","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:251","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:227","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"2b125eb3882954675405967d8a8dfd3c2f252935684d913d4d4196b0e3587d60","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:a tree","key":"a tree","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"a tree","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Dungeon features","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"「在外域地带，你永远不知道什么时候你的骨髓会预订一趟异国假期却忘了\n捎上你。在紫色内月下思考方形念头，可能把你身体每三个细胞重新定义成\n一个动词。有会吃掉脚的球鞋，会吃掉眼珠的双焦镜，会吃掉小肠的草莓\n酸奶。在更远更远的地方，有那么些比光更古老的波长，能爬进你的过去，\n吃掉你出生的那一天。\n\n 当这些事发生在那些非自然的枝生者、那些滑稽可笑的游客身上时——他们\n从灰色的遗忘中闪进闪出——没有任何存在会眨眼或蠕动一下。像她这样的\n东西，或者蕨，或者板条迷宫的失败者，对大多数实体来说不过是些_怪虫子_，\n最好算是个古怪的奇观，更常见的是不受欢迎的寄生虫。现实有着无限的\n层次，而她仍然，以最糟糕的方式，觉得自己从未离开过家。」\n    -Jonathan Wojcik，《可怕医院》。2021。","current_english":"“Out here in the zones, you never even knew when your bone marrow might book an\n exotic vacation and neglect to invite you along. Thinking square thoughts under\n a violet endomoon could redefine every third cell in your body as a verb. There\n were sneakers that ate feet, bifocals that ate eyeballs, strawberry yogurt that\n ate small intestines. Farther out there, _so_ much farther, there were\n wavelengths of something ancestral to light that could crawl into your past and\n eat the day you were born.\n\n Nobody so much as batted an eye or quivered a flange when these things happened\n to the unnaturally branchinated, the puzzling and comical tourists that blipped\n in and out from the forgettable grey. Things like herself, or Fern, or the\n plank maze loser were little more than _weird bugs_ to most entities, a quaint\n curiosity at best and more often an unwanted parasite. Infinite layers of\n reality, and it still felt, in the worst way, like she'd never left home.”\n    -Jonathan Wojcik, _Awful Hospital_. 2021.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Dungeon branches","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:262","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:237","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"f7b2342d53fb958e28552e81a95901dac330e528aa3721d27190e27dde644a2d","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:abyss","key":"abyss","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"abyss","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Dungeon branches","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"「在盐矿里\n 我看见这个盐瓶\n 里的盐。\n 我知道你不信，\n 但在那里，\n 它在歌唱，\n 盐在歌唱，盐矿\n 的皮肤\n 在用塞满泥土的\n 嘴歌唱。\n 当我听到\n 盐的声音，\n 我独自\n 在空旷的\n 沙漠中\n 颤抖。\n 安托法加斯塔附近，\n 整片\n 盐碱平原\n 用它\n 龟裂的\n 嗓音\n 嘶吼出\n 一首悲悯的\n 歌。」\n    -巴勃罗·聂鲁达，《盐颂》，1957。\n     Philip Levine英译，2013年出版","current_english":"“In the salt mines\n I saw the salt\n in this shaker.\n I know you won’t believe me,\n but there\n it sings,\n the salt sings, the skin\n of the salt mines\n sings\n with a mouth choking\n on dirt.\n Alone\n when I heard\n the voice\n of salt,\n I trembled\n in the empty\n desert.\n Near Antafagusta\n the whole\n salted plain\n shouts out\n in its\n cracked\n voice\n a pitiful\n song.”\n    -Pablo Neruda, “Ode to Salt”, 1957.\n     trans. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"「精灵是奇妙的。他们引发奇妙。\n 精灵是不可思议的。他们制造不可思议。\n 精灵是奇幻的。他们创造奇幻。\n 精灵是魅惑的。他们投射魅惑。\n 精灵是迷人的。他们编织迷咒。\n 精灵是骇人的。他们孕育骇惧。\n 词语这玩意，意义可以像蛇一样扭曲，你若想找蛇，就到改变了意义的\n 词语背后去寻。\n 从没有人说过精灵_善良_。\n 精灵是_坏的_。」\n    -特里·普拉切特，《老爷与夫人》。1992。","current_english":"“Elves are wonderful. They provoke wonder.\n Elves are marvelous. They cause marvels.\n Elves are fantastic. They create fantasies.\n Elves are glamorous. They project glamour.\n Elves are enchanting. They weave enchantment.\n Elves are terrific. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"「你装了十六吨，得到了什么\n 又老了一天，债更深了一层\n 圣彼得别召唤我，我去不了\n 我的灵魂已抵押给公司商店。」\n    -Merle Travis，《十六吨》。1946。","current_english":"“You load sixteen tons, what do you get\n Another day older and deeper in debt\n Saint Peter don't you call me 'cause I can't go\n I owe my soul to the company store.”\n    -Merle Travis, “Sixteen Tons”. 1946.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Dungeon branches","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:326","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:296","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"35cbf7d5bc67efbc90e37e2f1c34ee65121dd55aef0f36e9d9034739aafb1b75","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:orcish mines","key":"orcish mines","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"orcish mines","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Dungeon branches","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"「我常想起那个古老的比喻，说我们都是广阔海洋中的孤岛。尤其是这些日子，\n如今一切都比从前更艰难，世界比我们曾经想象的更严酷。\n\n 我们都像岛屿，那位哲人说。也许这是真的。然而我忍不住想起一句更古老\n的箴言，刻在某个洞穴的壁上，出自一位早已被遗忘的先知之手：最终，大海\n会吞没一切。\n\n 那些古老的字句如浪潮般冲入我的脑海，将我从睡梦中唤醒，让我充满自己\n也无法完全理解的情感。我们像岛屿。这意味着我们彼此相连吗？我们共享\n同一个起源？还是仅仅共享沉没的共同命运？」\n    -《大海将吞没一切》。2012。","current_english":"“I often think about that old metaphor, the one that says we are all islands on\n a wide sea. Especially these days, now that things are more difficult than\n before and the world appears to be harsher than we once imagined it to be.\n\n We are all like islands, the philosopher said. Perhaps it's true. Yet I cannot\n help but remember an older saying scratched on a cave wall somewhere by a\n long- forgotten prophet: In the end the sea will claim everything.\n\n The ancient words crash into my mind like waves, waking me from sleep, filling\n me with feelings I cannot fully understand. We are like islands. Does it mean\n we are connected? Do we share a common origin? Or just the common fate of\n sinking?”\n    -“The Sea Will Claim Everything”. 2012.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Dungeon branches","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:334","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:304","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"2ce921e048b87531e18b523e5f5358a95c23d2a5d6797718eb45350b895eb80d","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:shoals","key":"shoals","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"shoals","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Dungeon branches","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"「地狱中有一片凄寂孤绝之地，\n 远离一切寻常的阴郁：\n 一片平坦、悲伤的国土，阴影遍布，\n 其孤寂境况，我诗句永难尽述。」\n    -爱德华·阿灵顿·罗宾逊，《至高》。1897。","current_english":"“There is a drear and lonely tract of hell\n From all the common gloom removed afar:\n A flat, sad land it is, where shadows are,\n Whose lorn estate my verse may never tell.”\n    -Edward Arlington Robinson, “Supremacy”. 1897.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Dungeon branches","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:350","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:318","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"4606a93a5874163ec977ec6839ba0df2e7dc79c3b991abfc290aa1a553263a99","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:tartarus","key":"tartarus","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"tartarus","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Dungeon branches","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"「我听见有大声音从殿中出来，向那七位天使说：『你们去，把盛神大怒的\n七碗倒在地上。』」\n    -圣经和合本，启示录 16:1。","current_english":"“And I heard a great voice out of the temple saying to the seven angels, Go\n your ways, and pour out the vials of the wrath of God upon the earth.”\n    -KJV Bible, Revelations 16:1.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Dungeon branches","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:358","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:326","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"ac4478bd7e45e91e9b3d90c27f5929fc6780d8ab53fc69046224387e79b6550e","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:temple","key":"temple","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"temple","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Dungeon branches","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"「每个人心底深处，都有一座坟墓和一间地牢，尽管上方的灯火、音乐和狂欢\n可能使我们忘记它们的存在，忘记它们隐藏的被埋者和囚徒。但有时，而且往往\n在午夜时分，那些黑暗的容器会猛然敞开。在这样的时刻，当心灵只有被动的\n感受力而无主动的力量；当想象只是一面镜子，赋予一切念头以生动，却没有\n选择或掌控它们的能力——此时愿你祈祷，让你的悲伤沉睡，让悔恨的兄弟会\n不要挣断锁链。」\n    -纳撒尼尔·霍桑，《萦绕之心》。1835。","current_english":"“In the depths of every heart, there is a tomb and a dungeon, though the\n lights, the music, and revelry above may cause us to forget their existence,\n and the buried ones, or prisoners whom they hide. But sometimes, and oftenest\n at midnight, those dark receptacles are flung wide open. In an hour like this,\n when the mind has a passive sensibility, but no active strength; when the\n imagination is a mirror, imparting vividness to all ideas, without the power\n of selecting or controlling them; then pray that your grieves may slumber, and\n the brotherhood of remorse not break their chain.”\n    -Nathaniel Hawthorne, “The Haunted Mind”. 1835.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Dungeon branches","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:364","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:332","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"9d1da2851fef8bc9910d3c19d47d8e187e9961e84b9bb6bbcbcb277d73ce9773","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:tomb","key":"tomb","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"tomb","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Dungeon branches","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}

{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"尽管其外形笨拙，这法术却普遍被认为是阿利斯泰尔最受欢迎的作品；在地表\n图书馆中可以找到无数变体，根据用途定制，从地板蜡到蔬菜汤无所不包。然而，\n在地牢深处，大多数法师更青睐经典版本在战斗中的实用价值。","current_english":"Despite its unwieldy appearance, it is widely considered Alistair's most popular\nwork; countless variations of this spell can be found in surface libraries,\ncustomised to prepare everything from floor polish to vegetable soup. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"「请记住，我所记录的并非疯人的幻象。太阳在天上照耀的真实，绝比不上我此刻\n所断言之事为真。某种奇迹或许造就了它，然而发现的过程却清晰可循且合乎情理。\n经过无数个日夜难以置信的辛劳和疲惫，我成功发现了生命与生机的根源；不仅如此，\n我本人也得以将生机赋予无生命之物。」\n    -玛丽·雪莱，《弗兰肯斯坦，或现代的普罗米修斯》，\n     第一卷第三章。1818（第一版）","current_english":"“Remember, I am not recording the vision of a madman. The sun does not more\n certainly shine in the heavens, than that which I now affirm is true. Some\n miracle might have produced it, yet the stages of the discovery were distinct\n and probable. After days and nights of incredible labour and fatigue, I\n succeeded in discovering the cause of generation and life; nay, more, I became\n myself capable of bestowing animation upon lifeless matter.”\n    -Mary Shelley, _Frankenstein, or the Modern Prometheus_,\n     Vol. I, Chapter 3. 1818 (1st Ed.)","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Spells and abilities","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:393","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:357","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"a1c75eac57c526f357938e0abbb1246ce8f6b914afb41c1d4eb81c495de43cc4","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:animate dead spell","key":"animate dead spell","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"animate dead spell","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Spells and abilities","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}

{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"「这就是龙去的地方。\n 它们躺着……\n 不是死了，也不是睡着了。不是在等待，因为等待意味着期待。\n 或许我们要找的词是……\n ……蛰伏。\n 尽管它们占据的空间不同于寻常空间，但它们\n   密密匝匝地挤在一起。没有一寸之地不为一只爪、\n   一枚爪尖、一片鳞、一截尾尖所占据，效果便如同\n   那些视觉幻象画，你的眼球最终会意识到每一条龙之间\n   的空间，实际上，是另一条龙。\n 它们会让你想到一罐沙丁鱼——如果你想象的沙丁鱼\n   又大又有鳞又骄傲又傲慢的话。\n 而且想必，在某个地方，有一把钥匙。」\n    -特里·普拉切特，《卫兵！卫兵！》。1990。","current_english":"“This is where the dragons went.\n They lie...\n Not dead, not asleep. Not waiting, because waiting implies expectation.\n Possibly the word we're looking for here is...\n ...dormant.\n And although the space they occupy isn't like normal space, nevertheless\n   they are packed in tightly. Not a cubic inch there but is filled by a claw,\n   a talon, a scale, the tip of a tail, so the effect is like one of those\n   trick drawings and your eyeballs eventually realise that the space between\n   each dragon is, in fact, another dragon.\n They could put you in mind of a can of sardines, if you thought sardines\n   were huge and scaly and proud and arrogant.\n And presumably, somewhere, there's the key.”\n    -Terry Pratchett, “Guards! Guards!”. 1990.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Spells and abilities","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:493","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:449","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"8f959f8a5f2e8b64c0f4ad3fd200af2bd6b6c37dee9c91ba13dbe63b013a27f1","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:dragon's call spell","key":"dragon's call spell","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"dragon's call spell","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Spells and abilities","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"决斗，名词。一种正式的仪式，旨在为两个仇敌之和解铺垫前奏。要圆满完成\n需要很高的技巧；如果执行得笨拙，有时会导致最出人意料且可悲的后果。很久\n很久以前，有一个人在决斗中丧了命。\n    -安布罗斯·比尔斯，《魔鬼辞典》，1906。","current_english":"DUEL, n. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"「那时房内充满男女，非利士人的众首领也都在那里。房的平顶上约有三千男女\n观看参孙戏耍。\n\n 参孙求告耶和华说：'主耶和华啊，求你眷念我。神啊，求你赐我这一次的\n力量，使我在非利士人身上报那剜我双眼的仇。'\n\n 参孙就抱住托房的那两根柱子：左手抱一根，右手抱一根，\n\n 说：'我情愿与非利士人同死！'就尽力屈身，房子倒塌，压住首领和房内的\n众人。这样，参孙死时所杀的人比活着所杀的还多。」\n    -圣经和合本，士师记 16:27-30。","current_english":"“Now the house was full of men and women; and all the lords of the Philistines\n were there; and there were upon the roof about three thousand men and women,\n that beheld while Samson made sport.\n\n And Samson called unto the LORD, and said, O Lord GOD, remember me, I pray\n thee, and strengthen me, I pray thee, only this once, O God, that I may be at\n once avenged of the Philistines for my two eyes.\n\n And Samson took hold of the two middle pillars upon which the house stood, and\n on which it was borne up, of the one with his right hand, and of the other\n with his left.\n\n And Samson said, Let me die with the Philistines. And he bowed himself with\n all his might; and the house fell upon the lords, and upon all the people that\n were therein. So the dead which he slew at his death were more than they which\n he slew in his life.”\n    -KJV Bible, Judges 16:27-30.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Spells and abilities","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:557","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:511","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"15c3b45faa721a2970a745858ca50399a559bc631c9c65fea077b51feb4e2319","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:lee's rapid deconstruction spell","key":"lee's rapid deconstruction spell","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"lee's rapid deconstruction spell","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Spells and abilities","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"「如同强者一向知晓的那样，\n 总有小窍门可循；\n 因为作为好人，你永远不会那么狡猾，\n 一支永远百发百中的魔法飞箭！」\n\n    -ASP，《变形记 I-III》。2008\n","current_english":"“Wie die Mächtigen es schon seit jeher wissen,\n Es gibt immer einen Kniff;\n Denn als Guter bist du niemals so gerissen,\n Ein Zauberpfeil der immer trifft!\n\n [As the powerful have known all along,\n There is always a trick;\n Because as a good person, you'll never be so cunning,\n A magic arrow that always hits!]”\n    -ASP, \"Verwandlungen I-III\". 2008\n","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Spells and abilities","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:577","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:525","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"6f09db3911a307b009a9c80c92f05b4c37c6e517d23d5be6c9be9661cde80962","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:magic dart spell","key":"magic dart spell","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"magic dart spell","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Spells and abilities","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"「'我们下去，在那里变乱他们的口音，使他们的言语彼此不通。'于是耶和华\n使他们从那里分散在全地上；他们就停工，不造那城了。」\n    -圣经和合本，创世记 11:7-8。","current_english":"“Go to, let us go down, and there confound their language, that they may not\n understand one another's speech. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"虽然最初是为工业建设而设计，但有进取心的模仿者很快发现了大量其他问题，\n都可以通过将一个物体极快地移进另一个物体来解决。","current_english":"While originally designed for use in industrial construction, enterprising\ncopycats were quick to discover a multitude of other problems that could be\nsolved by moving one object into another, very, very quickly.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Spells and abilities","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:599","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:542","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"7a7761958821c8691a94102d842cf5aba5a74756c191fbb5a4d6d69c3fbf2bb9","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:maxwell's portable piledriver spell","key":"maxwell's portable piledriver spell","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"maxwell's portable piledriver spell","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Spells and abilities","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"「多年以来，印度的霍乱显示出日益强烈的蔓延与迁移倾向。这种瘟疫产生于\n恒河三角洲的温热沼泽，随着那繁茂无用、人迹罕至的原始荒岛丛林的瘴疠之气\n而升腾——在那竹林中蹲伏着猛虎——它持续而异常猛烈地在整个印度斯坦肆虐，\n向东蔓延至中国，向西传入阿富汗和波斯，并沿着商队往来的主干道，将恐怖一直\n带到阿斯特拉罕，甚至莫斯科。」\n    -托马斯·曼，《死于威尼斯》，1911。","current_english":"“Seit mehreren Jahren schon hatte die indische Cholera eine verstärkte\n Neigung zur Ausbreitung und Wanderung an den Tag gelegt. Erzeugt aus\n den warmen Moraesten des Ganges-Deltas, aufgestiegen mit dem\n mephitischen Odem jener üppig-untauglichen, von Menschen gemiedenen\n Urwelt- und Inselwildnis, in deren Bambusdickichten der Tiger kauert,\n hatte die Seuche in ganz Hindustan andauernd und ungewöhnlich heftig\n gewütet, hatte östlich nach China, westlich nach Afghanistan und\n Persien übergegriffen und, den Hauptstraßen des Karawanenverkehrs\n folgend, ihre Schrecken bis Astrachan, ja selbst bis Moskau getragen.”\n    -Thomas Mann, _Der Tod in Venedig_, 1911.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Spells and abilities","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:605","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:547","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"771a91792f878b719b8982c9b78e9dc865c107fd3619f0d74e7af4f28b03425a","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:mephitic cloud spell","key":"mephitic cloud spell","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"mephitic cloud spell","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Spells and abilities","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"「在全书论述过程中，每种元素都获得了充裕时间来称颂自己的美德和对人类的\n功用，但当轮到水星时，他宣布自己主宰着之前出现的所有存在。水星是母亲，\n也是父亲。他赋予其他金属光彩，使它们为人类所用。没有水星，其他金属便\n毫无（使用）价值。也许坐上王座反而会将他限制在固定的主权位置上。他统治\n着，但他必须保持流动。」\n    -Karen Pinkus，《炼金术的水星：暧昧理论》，2009","current_english":"“During the course of the treatise, each element is allowed time to tout its\n own virtues and usefulness to mankind, but when it comes time for Mercury,\n he announces that he presides over all of the beings that have come before\n him. Mercury is mother and father. He gives other metals their splendor and\n makes them useful to humanity. Without Mercury, the other metals would have no\n (use) value. Perhaps a seat on the throne would restrict him to a fixed\n position of sovereignty. He reigns, but he must be mobile.”\n    -Karen Pinkus, _Alchemical Mercury: A Theory of Ambivalance_, 2009","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Spells and abilities","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:618","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:556","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"6289867c0d79158b0d8767f9c60e2fcb5056b19cc4446925b465c78c728719c4","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:mercury arrow spell","key":"mercury arrow spell","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":"「在全书论述过程中，每种元素都获得了充裕时间来称颂自己的美德和对人类的\n功用，但当轮到汞时，他宣布自己主宰着之前出现的所有存在。汞是母亲，\n也是父亲。他赋予其他金属光彩，使它们为人类所用。没有汞，其他金属便\n毫无（使用）价值。也许坐上王座反而会将他限制在固定的主权位置上。他统治\n着，但他必须保持流动。」\n    -Karen Pinkus，《炼金术之汞：矛盾性理论》，2009","reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["保留现译；会继续留下已由英文正文或当前术语直接证明的偏差。"],"resolved_key":"mercury arrow spell","reviewer_rationale":"炼金语境中的 Mercury 是元素汞而非行星水星；全段及书名同步改为“汞”，保留拟人代词和流动性论述。","section":"Spells and abilities","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"adjust"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"长期以来，学者们认为那锤击不过是遮掩法术背后复杂结构强化魔法的戏剧性\n表演，然而每次尝试从咒语中移除锤击都会导致法术失效。这一现象的含义至今\n仍是激烈争论的话题。","current_english":"Scholars long considered the hammer mere theatrics to mask the complex\nstructural enhancement magics which must underpin this spell, and yet every\nattempt to remove it from the incantation has caused the spell to lose its\neffectiveness. The implications of this remain hotly debated.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Spells and abilities","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:629","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:565","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"55eec237b0e0fd2184d8777d5eac480f6d93895032556a8911ebadbef414c47e","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:nazja's percussive tempering spell","key":"nazja's percussive tempering spell","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"nazja's percussive tempering spell","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Spells and abilities","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}

{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"「任何姿势保持太久都会钙化。\n 任何信念相信太久都会变成教条。\n 任何表情维持太久都会硬化成面具。\n 那么，_真正的_我们究竟是\n 那坚硬的外壳，\n 还是藏在底下柔软而不安的自我？」\n    -Dorothy Gambrell，《猫和女孩》，2011。","current_english":"“Any pose held too long calcifies.\n A belief believed too long becomes dogma.\n Any expression held long enough will harden into a mask.\n And who is the _real_ us\n that hard exterior\n or the soft uncertain self we keep beneath?”\n    -Dorothy Gambrell, _Cat and Girl_, 2011.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Spells and abilities","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:641","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:576","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"f83de9d798c81b78f3456836348e8e69f4eda94de33ab73c9beb2872c99cb6be","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:petrify spell","key":"petrify spell","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"petrify spell","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Spells and abilities","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"「我在一间厅堂中看见一支指示方向的箭，我想，这个无害的符号曾经是一件\n铁器，一支不可逃避的致命弹丸，曾刺穿人和狮子的血肉，曾在温泉关遮蔽太阳，\n曾给了哈拉尔·西古尔德森六英尺永远属于他的英格兰土地。」\n    -豪尔赫·路易斯·博尔赫斯，《变异》。1960。\n     Mildred Boyle英译","current_english":"“I saw in a hall an arrow pointing the way and I thought that this inoffensive\n symbol had once been a thing of iron, an inescapable and fatal projectile that\n pierced the flesh of men and lions and clouded the sun at Thermopylae and gave\n Harald Sigurdarson six feet of English earth forever.”\n    -Jorge Luis Borges, _Mutations_. 1960.\n     trans. Mildred Boyle","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Spells and abilities","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:651","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:586","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"20356735af60ccbbf64959ad5afe52b9fdb6e283947e3ebca2a4e16f26ca914a","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:poison arrow spell","key":"poison arrow spell","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"poison arrow spell","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Spells and abilities","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"「其中详述了与我那被诅咒的出身相关的一切；那一连串令人作呕、导致我诞生\n的情境被完整呈现；我所憎恶、令人嫌恶的形貌被以最细致的笔墨描摹，那语言\n描绘了你的恐惧，却将我的恐惧铭刻得不可磨灭。我读着读着，心生厌恶。\n'可恨啊，我获得生命的那一天！'我在痛苦中喊道。'被诅咒的创造者！你为何\n造出如此丑陋的怪物，连你自己都厌恶地背过身去？上帝以悲悯之心，按自己的\n形象造人，使人美丽动人；而我的外形却是你那形象的污秽翻版，因相似而更显\n可怖。撒旦尚有同伴，有同党魔鬼来钦佩和鼓舞他；而我却孤身一人，被所有人\n憎恶。'」\n    -玛丽·雪莱，《弗兰肯斯坦，或现代的普罗米修斯》，\n     第二卷第七章。1818（第一版）","current_english":"“Every thing is related in them which bears reference to my accursed origin;\n the whole detail of that series of disgusting circumstances which produced it\n is set in view; the minutest description of my odious and loathsome person is\n given, in language which painted your own horrors, and rendered mine\n ineffaceable. I sickened as I read. ‘Hateful day when I received life!’ I\n exclaimed in agony. ‘Cursed creator! Why did you form a monster so hideous that\n even you turned from me in disgust? God in pity made man beautiful and\n alluring, after his own image; but my form is a filthy type of yours, more\n horrid from its very resemblance. Satan had his companions, fellow-devils, to\n admire and encourage him; but I am solitary and detested.’”\n    -Mary Shelley, _Frankenstein, or the Modern Prometheus_,\n     Vol. II, Chapter 7. 1818 (1st Ed.)","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Spells and abilities","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:660","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:594","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"9223b5d3c32c3573ffb518986e9d2c1b75bcd209bf328d3d29e5f9ea8663b04c","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:sacrifice love ability","key":"sacrifice love ability","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":"「其中详述了与我那被诅咒的出身相关的一切；那一连串令人作呕、导致我诞生\n的情境被完整呈现；我所憎恶、令人嫌恶的形貌被以最细致的笔墨描摹，那语言\n描绘出你自己的恐怖，也令我的恐怖变得不可磨灭。我读着读着，心生厌恶。\n'可恨啊，我获得生命的那一天！'我在痛苦中喊道。'被诅咒的创造者！你为何\n造出如此丑陋的怪物，连你自己都厌恶地背过身去？上帝以悲悯之心，按自己的\n形象造人，使人美丽动人；而我的外形却是你那形象的污秽翻版，因相似而更显\n可怖。撒旦尚有同伴，有同党魔鬼来钦佩和鼓舞他；而我却孤身一人，被所有人\n憎恶。'」\n    -玛丽·雪莱，《弗兰肯斯坦，或现代的普罗米修斯》，\n     第二卷第七章。1818（第一版）","reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["保留现译；会继续留下已由英文正文或当前术语直接证明的偏差。"],"resolved_key":"sacrifice love ability","reviewer_rationale":"horrors 在此指造物与创造者各自造成的恐怖，而非双方的“恐惧”；恢复 painted/rendered 的对象和对照。","section":"Spells and abilities","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"adjust"}

{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"「鲁马克：一开始是轻微发烧和喉咙干燥。当病毒侵入红细胞，受害者开始头晕，\n皮肤出现瘙痒皮疹，然后毒液攻击中枢神经系统，剧烈肌肉痉挛，紧接着是\n不可避免的口水直流。\n [奥维尔随着鲁马克的描述逐一出现了以上所有症状]\n 鲁马克：到这一步，整个消化系统崩溃，伴随着无法控制的肠胃胀气。\n [奥维尔开始无法控制地放屁]\n 鲁马克：直到最后，那可怜的家伙化为一团颤抖的、只剩渣滓的果冻状废物。」\n    -《空前绝后满天飞》，1980。","current_english":"“Rumack: It starts with a slight fever and dryness of the throat. When the\n virus penetrates the red blood cells, the victim becomes dizzy, begins to\n experience an itchy rash, then the poison goes to work on the central nervous\n system, severe muscle spasms followed by the inevitable drooling.\n [Oveur does all of the above as Rumack describes each one]\n Rumack: At this point, the entire digestive system collapses accompanied by\n uncontrollable flatulence.\n [Oveur begins to fart uncontrollably]\n Rumack: Until finally, the poor bastard is reduced to a quivering wasted piece\n of jelly.”\n    -Airplane! 1980.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Spells and abilities","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:686","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:615","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"c67ed04b379b6645aea19d621a60beb8572b0c0ea18300914fc845f69e63c73d","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:slimify ability","key":"slimify ability","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"slimify ability","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Spells and abilities","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}

{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"他杀了高贵的穆乔基维斯，\n用它的皮做了手套，\n毛面朝里做内衬，\n皮面朝外做外层。\n他为了把暖面放里面，\n就把内层皮面翻外面；\n他为了把凉面放外面，\n就把暖面毛面翻里面。\n这就是为什么他把毛面放里面，\n为什么他把皮面放外面，\n为什么他把里外翻了个遍。\"\n    ——佚名，载于威尔斯编《戏仿诗选》，第120页。1904年。","current_english":"“He killed the noble Mudjokivis.\n Of the skin he made him mittens,\n Made them with the fur side inside,\n Made them with the skin side outside.\n He, to get the warm side inside,\n Put the inside skin side outside;\n He, to get the cold side outside,\n Put the warm side fur side inside.\n That's why he put the fur side inside,\n Why {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"（退尔持弩登场）\n威廉·退尔：\n我珍贵的宝贝——我最贵重的财富——\n我要给你立一个标记，悲痛的哀泣\n永远无法穿透——但你却要刺穿它——\n而你，我忠实的弩弦，多少次\n在游戏中忠实地为我效劳，\n在这危急万分的时刻，不要抛弃我——\n只这一次别辜负我，我的好弦，\n你曾多少次为利箭插上翅膀：——\n因为倘若你这次从我手中徒劳飞去，\n我便没有第二支可继你之后。\"\n    ——弗里德里希·席勒，《威廉·退尔》，第四幕第三场。1804年。\n    西奥多·马丁爵士英译，1898年。","current_english":"“(Tell enters with his crossbow)\n W. TELL:\n My precious jewel now, —my chiefest treasure—\n A mark I'll set thee, which the cry of grief\n Could never penetrate,—but thou shalt pierce it,—\n And thou, my trusty bowstring, that so oft\n For sport has served me faithfully and well,\n Desert me not in this dread hour of need,—\n Only be true this once, my own good cord,\n That hast so often wing'd the biting shaft:—\n For shouldst thou fly successless from my hand,\n I have no second to send after thee.”\n    -Friedrich Schiller, _Wilhelm Tell_, IV, iii. 1804.\n     trans. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"共和国总是在边境维持七八千正规军，以防备鞑靼人的入侵。国王并不供养这些军队，他只支付海杜克兵、塞梅尔兵和耶尼切里兵的薪饷。前述第一种士兵身着蓝衣，缀有大纽扣和锡片，头戴毡帽。他们配备火枪和长柄斧，据说那是一种极好的武器。\"\n    ——约翰·平克顿，《世界最佳最有趣航海与旅行记总汇，其中多篇首次译为英文。以新体例编纂》。1808年。","current_english":"“The republic always maintains seven or eight thousand regular troops on the\n frontiers, to prevent the incursions of the Tartars. The King does not\n maintain these troops; he only pays the Heydukes, the Semelles, and the\n Janizaries. The first-mentioned are dressed in blue, with large buttons and\n plates of tin, and have bonnets made of felt upon their heads. They have\n firelocks, and the bardiche, which they say is a very good weapon.”\n    -John Pinkerton, _A General Collection of the Best and Most Interesting\n     Voyages and Travels in all parts of the World, many of which are now first\n     translated into English. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"在卡里亚的钱币上——确实是相当晚期的——双刃斧竖立在其长柱状手柄上，挂着两条垂带，极像一尊崇拜偶像的模样。\"\n    ——阿瑟·约翰·埃文斯爵士，\"迈锡尼的树与柱崇拜及其地中海关联\"，《希腊研究杂志》第二十一卷，第109页。1901年。","current_english":"“On Carian coins, indeed of quite late date, the labrys, set up on its long\n pillar-like handle, with two dependent fillets, has much the appearance of a\n cult image.”\n    -Sir Arthur John Evans, “Mycenaean tree and pillar cult and its\n     Mediterranean relations,” _Journal of Hellenic Studies_ XXI, p. 109. 1901.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Items","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:896","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:808","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"6b5aac8c6570ac9f4620231c4281eaa8e90a43d3cfed54c39b46d511705460ce","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:battleaxe","key":"battleaxe","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"battleaxe","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Items","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"棕榈叶上，莎草卷中；\n可塑黏土与皮革卷轴，\n人记录了他的思想；岁月流逝，\n看哪！印刷机终于问世！\"\n    ——约翰·格林利夫·惠蒂埃，《图书馆》，第四节。","current_english":"“On leaf of palm, on sedge-wrought roll;\n On plastic clay and leathern scroll,\n Man wrote his thoughts; the ages passed,\n And lo! the Press was found at last!”\n    -John Greenleaf Whittier, _The Library_, st. 4.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Items","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:904","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:813","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"00d540d71296b39b13d8cfb0cb423af03dbe2efbb1df6d7b4e1df9c64a7fa33b","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:book","key":"book","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"book","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Items","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"那武器从二三十码外掷出，以惊人的速度在空中旋转，击中对手右臂后，竟反弹至不下七八十码之外，留下一道可怖的瘀伤，激起全场赞叹。\"\n    ——《悉尼公报与新南威尔士广告人报》，1804年12月23日。","current_english":"“The weapon, thrown at 20 or 30 yards distance, twirled round in the air with\n astonishing velocity, and alighting on the right arm of one of his opponents,\n actually rebounded to a distance not less than 70 or 80 yards, leaving a\n horrible contusion behind, and exciting universal admiration.”\n    -The Sydney Gazette and New South Wales Advertiser, 23 December 1804","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Items","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:912","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:821","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"8f68653b6aa72fb28c2b56096ae27bc9db56ba9f8f3550e8460f8ad622bd3b16","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:boomerang","key":"boomerang","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"boomerang","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Items","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"武器啊，形体优美，赤裸而苍白！\n从母腹中拔出的头颅！\n木质的肉与金属的骨！只有独肢，只有独唇！\n烈火锻成的灰蓝叶片！从一粒小种子生出的柄！\n栖息于草丛中与草叶上，\n为人所倚，亦为人倚靠。\"\n    ——沃尔特·惠特曼，《阔刃斧之歌》，第1-6行。1867年。","current_english":"“Weapon, shapely, naked, wan!\n Head from the mother's bowels drawn!\n Wooded flesh and metal bone! limb only one, and lip only one!\n Gray-blue leaf by red-heat grown! helve produced from a little seed sown!\n Resting the grass amid and upon,\n To be lean'd, and to lean on.”\n    -Walt Whitman, _Song of the Broad-Axe_, l. 1-6. 1867.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Items","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:920","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:826","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"199d04de710d6894663db85e7d4ce89b552710d7f641ca2771836d6c09ee8cbc","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:broad axe","key":"broad axe","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"broad axe","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Items","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"任谁去夸耀战场上的勇气吧，\n我从盾牌中寻得的庇护却少得可怜。\n自然之法则，非荣誉之法则，我们必须遵从：\n正因如此，我扔掉了无用的盾牌，\n凭借谨慎的逃跑和机敏，从坟墓中\n保住了勇敢无法保全的生命。\n更好的小圆盾，我很快就能再得；\n可谁又能再得一条性命？\"\n    ——阿尔基洛科斯，公元前7世纪。\n    威廉·H·古德温英译，1878年。","current_english":"“Let who will boast their courage in the field,\n I find but little safety from my shield.\n Nature's, not honour's, law we must obey:\n This made me cast my useless shield away,\n And by a prudent flight and cunning save\n A life, which valour could not, from the grave.\n A better buckler I can soon regain;\n But who can get another life again?”\n    -Archilochos. 7th cent. B.C.\n     trans. William H. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"贝尔吾妻，你为何争吵不休？\n今是今，昔是昔：\n纵寻遍天涯海角，\n你也分不清村夫与绅士：\n他们身着黑、绿、黄与蓝，\n远超出自己的身份。\n此生我定要试上一回；\n因为我定要为自己添一件新斗篷。\"\n    ——佚名，\"旧斗篷\"。16世纪。","current_english":"“O Bell my wife, why dost thou flyte?\n   Now is now, and then was then:\n Seek now all the world throughout,\n   Thou kens not clowns from gentlemen:\n They are clad in black, green, yellow and blue,\n   So far above their own degree.\n Once in my life I'll take a view;\n   For I'll have a new cloak about me.”\n    -Anonymous, “The Old Cloak”. 16th Century.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Items","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:954","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:860","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"99d3c2d7e4985e168bdd3ba6d977f117219861887ef59573e6debc75eb229e4a","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:cloak","key":"cloak","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"cloak","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Items","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}

{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"他拔出匕首，如此锋利，\n如此锋利而精巧，\n刺入那棕发新娘的胸膛，\n她倒地死在他脚边。\n\n'等等我，亲爱的安妮特，'他说，\n'等等，吾爱，'他喊道；\n然后将匕首刺入自己的心脏，\n倒在她身旁死去。\"\n    ——英格兰传统民谣，\"托马斯勋爵与美丽的安妮特\"，约1650年。","current_english":"“He drew his dagger, that was sae sharp,\n   That was sae sharp and meet,\n And drave it into the nut-browne bride,\n   That fell deid at his feit.\n\n ‘Now stay for me, dear Annet,’ he sed,\n   ‘Now stay, my dear,’ he cry'd;\n Then strake the dagger untill his heart,\n   And fell deid by her side.”\n    -English traditional ballad, “Lord Thomas and Fair Annet”, circa 1650.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Items","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:975","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:877","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"78b535b0ab855ae292991ca8d5128b6f33a1daf08ab117c7c277a50ded0a6e7f","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:dagger","key":"dagger","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"dagger","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Items","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}

{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"听到这话，他惊跳起来，看到的——不是他的索菲亚——不，也不是为苏丹后宫精心打扮的切尔克斯美人。不；没穿长裙，只穿着一件颇为粗糙、也不甚干净的衬衣，上面还沾着些许当日劳作产生的芬芳气味，手里握着一把干草叉——莫莉·西格里姆走了过来。\"\n    ——亨利·菲尔丁，《弃儿汤姆·琼斯的历史》，第五卷第十章。1749年。","current_english":"“At these words he started up, and beheld—not his Sophia—no, nor a Circassian\n maid richly and elegantly attired for the grand Signior's seraglio. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"伴随着一声可怕的嚎叫，炎魔向前倒下，它的阴影坠入深渊，消失了。但就在坠落之际，它挥动了鞭子，鞭梢抽打着缠绕住巫师的膝盖，将他拖向边缘。他踉跄着坠落，徒劳地抓住石头，滑入了深渊。\"\n    ——J.R.R.托尔金，《魔戒同盟》，第二卷第五章\"卡扎督姆之桥\"。1954年。","current_english":"“With a terrible cry the Balrog fell forward, and its shadow plunged down and\n vanished. But even as it fell it swung its whip, and the thongs lashed and\n curled about the wizard's knees, dragging him to the brink. He staggered, and\n fell, grasped vainly at the stone, and slid into the abyss.”\n    -J.R.R. Tolkien, _The Fellowship of the Ring_. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"'哈！哈！哈！'他的两个手下笑道，'看那些诺曼恶棍看到他们勇猛的骑士被我们勇敢的同胞用连枷砸碎脑壳时，该有多么屈辱。'\"\n    ——《骑士传奇；或，洪水与战场之危》。1830年。","current_english":"“‘Ah! ah! ah!’ laughed his two men, ‘how the Norman villains will be humbled\nwhen they see their doughty knight's skull beaten in by our brave countryman.’”\n    -_Tales of Chivalry; or, Perils by Flood and Field_. 1830.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Items","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:1021","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:912","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"536310d61f02a2338cb31a8ed48a25ec4e3392db998d4ba4802ece14a1c2ee12","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:dire flail","key":"dire flail","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"dire flail","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Items","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
