{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"她跳着，被迫跳着——在黑夜中跳着。鞋子带着她越过荆棘和灌木；她把自己刮得鲜血淋漓；她跳过荒野，来到一座孤独的小屋前。她知道刽子手住在这里；她用指节敲着窗格，喊道——'出来，出来！我进不去，因为我必须跳舞！'\n\n刽子手说——'你大概不知道我是谁吧？我用斧头砍掉坏人的脑袋，你听听我的斧头如何作响！'\n\n'不要砍我的头，'卡伦说，'因为那样我就无法为我的罪忏悔了。但请砍掉我穿着红鞋的脚吧？'\n\n于是她忏悔了自己所有的罪，刽子手用红鞋砍掉了她的脚；但鞋子带着那双小脚跳走了，越过田野，跳进了幽深的森林。\n\n他给她削了一双木脚和一对拐杖，教她一首罪人们常唱的圣诗；她亲吻了那只握过斧头的手，然后穿过荒野离去了。\"\n    ——汉斯·克里斯蒂安·安徒生，\"红鞋\"，《新童话·第一卷·第三辑》。1845年。","current_english":"“She danced, and was compelled to dance—to dance in the dark night. The shoes\n carried her on over thorn and brier; she scratched herself till she bled; she\n danced away across the heath to a little lonely house. Here she knew the\n executioner dwelt; and she tapped with her fingers on the panes, and\n called,—‘Come out, come out! I cannot come in, for I must dance!’\n\n And the Executioner said,—‘You probably don't know who I am? I cut off the bad\n people's heads with my axe, and mark how my axe rings!’\n\n ‘Do not strike off my head,’ said Karen, ‘for if you do I cannot repent of my\n sin. But strike off my feet with the red shoes?’\n\n And then she confessed all her sin, and the Executioner cut off her feet with\n the red shoes; but the shoes danced away with the little feet over the fields\n and into the deep forest.\n\n And he cut her a pair of wooden feet, with crutches, and taught her a psalm,\n which the criminals always sing; and she kissed the hand that had held the\n axe, and went away across the heath.”\n    -Hans Christian Andersen, “The Red Shoes”, _Nye Eventyr. Første Bind.\n     Tredie Samling._. 1845.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Items","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:1065","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:949","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"07a7363ea68310d645924f6f8a855ff9d70b7d5eae92d6b6d0ef566cfd6579b0","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:executioner's axe","key":"executioner's axe","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":"\"她跳着，被迫跳着——在黑夜中跳着。鞋子带着她越过荆棘和灌木；她把自己刮得鲜血淋漓；她跳过荒野，来到一座孤独的小屋前。她知道刽子手住在这里；她用指节敲着窗格，喊道——'出来，出来！我进不去，因为我必须跳舞！'\n\n刽子手说——'你大概不知道我是谁吧？我用斧头砍掉坏人的脑袋，你听听我的斧头如何作响！'\n\n'不要砍我的头，'卡伦说，'因为那样我就无法为我的罪忏悔了。但请砍掉我穿着红鞋的脚吧？'\n\n于是她忏悔了自己所有的罪，刽子手连同红鞋一起砍掉了她的双脚；但鞋子带着那双小脚跳走了，越过田野，跳进了幽深的森林。\n\n他给她削了一双木脚和一对拐杖，教她一首罪人们常唱的圣诗；她亲吻了那只握过斧头的手，然后穿过荒野离去了。\"\n    ——汉斯·克里斯蒂安·安徒生，\"红鞋\"，《新童话·第一卷·第三辑》。1845年。","reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["保留现译；会继续留下已由英文正文或当前术语直接证明的偏差。"],"resolved_key":"executioner's axe","reviewer_rationale":"with the red shoes 表示脚连同红鞋一起被砍下，不是以红鞋为工具；恢复介词关系。","section":"Items","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"adjust"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"我见过那样的日子，用我锋利的弯刀\n我能让他们跳起来：可如今我老了，\n这些磨难把我毁了。\"\n    ——威廉·莎士比亚，《李尔王》，第五幕第三场。1608年。","current_english":"“I have seen the day, with my good biting falchion\n I would have made them skip: I am old now,\n And these same crosses spoil me.”\n    -William Shakespeare, _King Lear_, V, iii. 1608.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Items","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:1089","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:962","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"f418efb8c77186200993ccebd5135bd275a89a4545c972b4b5d429ae28e9b0ad","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:falchion","key":"falchion","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"falchion","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Items","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}

{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"即使付出了巨大的努力和惨重的损失，突破了这双重防御之后，[德意志人]仍然发现自己处于劣势；因为他们的盔甲几乎不足以与敌人那粗陋却可怕的武器平等对抗。波希米亚人装备着长柄铁连枷，以惊人的力量挥舞着。他们极少失手，而一旦命中，连枷便击穿铜盔、头颅与一切。\"\n    ——詹姆斯·A·怀利，《新教史》，第一卷第三册第十五章\"约翰·胡斯与胡斯战争\"。1878年。","current_english":"“Even after forcing their way, with great effort and loss, through this double\n defence, [the Germans] still found themselves at a disadvantage; for their\n armour scarce enabled them to contend on equal terms with the uncouth but\n formidable weapons of their adversaries. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"于是那巨人开始蓄势战斗，\n燃着轻蔑的愤怒与高傲的鄙夷，\n将他那可怕的巨棒高高举起，\n上面布满粗糙的节疤与多瘤的木纹，\n他以为第一击便能将对手杀死。\"\n    ——埃德蒙·斯宾塞，《仙后》，第一卷\"红十字骑士的传说\"，第八章第七节，第55-59行。1590年。","current_english":"“Therewith the gyant buckled him to fight,\n Inflamd with scornefull wrath and high disdaine,\n And lifting up his dreadful club on hight,\n All armed with ragged snubbes and knottie graine,\n Him thought at first encounter to have slaine.”\n    -Edmund Spenser, _The Faerie Queene_, Book I, “The Legend of the Knight of\n     the Red Crosse”, Canto VIII, stanza vii, l. 55-9. 1590.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Items","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:1117","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:983","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"3f9d9690939295652f150f7321dc902251782c69bfecaa8085f2c9502f6ff122","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:giant club","key":"giant club","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"giant club","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Items","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"鬼に金棒\n\"鬼持铁棒\"\n    ——表示力量压倒性的日本谚语","current_english":" oni-ni-kanabō\n “oni with a spiked iron club”\n    -Japanese proverb indicating overwhelming power","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Items","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:1127","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:992","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"5f768d425b4020e1fe751d15673be28ebc1ba324b5296af0307a8179dc06191c","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:giant spiked club","key":"giant spiked club","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"giant spiked club","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Items","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"欲知你短杖或半长矛、林地钩镰、花钺或长刃戟等有利且完美长度之武器的精确尺寸，你当直立，将杖竖直紧贴身侧，以左手握之，右手尽力向上伸展握住杖身，然后在此长度之上再留出双手握持之余地，以利实战中刺、击、格挡之便，此即合你身形之恰好长度。且注意，此等长度通常为八九英尺，虽不甚精准，却适用于一切人之身形而于实战中毫无妨碍，盖因在任何可移动双手、于战斗中随意伸缩武器之兵器上，最后方之手后多出一尺杖身亦无妨碍也。\"\n    ——乔治·西尔弗，《防御悖论》。1599年。","current_english":"“To know the perfect length of your ſhort ſtaffe, or half Pike, Forreſt bil,\n Partiſan or Gleue, or ſuch like weapons of vantage and perfect lengths, you\n ſhall ſtand vpright, holding the ſtaffe vpright cloſe by your body, with your\n left hãd, reaching with your right hand your ſtaffe as high as you can, and\n then allow to that length a ſpace to ſet both your hands, when you come to\n fight, wherein you may conueniently ſtrike, thruſt and ward, & that is the\n iuſt length according to you ſtature. And this note, that theſe lengths will\n commonly fall out to be eight or nine foot long, and will fit, although not\n iuſt, the ſtatures of all men, without any hindrance at all vnto them in their\n fight, becauſe in any weapon wherin the hands may be remoued, and at libertie,\n to make the weapon lõger or ſhorter in fight at his pleaſure, a foot of the\n ſtaffe behind the backmoſt hand doth no harme.”\n    -George Silver,_Paradoxes of Defence_.1599.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Items","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:1133","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:998","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"8a75269427ce981b68a56db81e6558d01dbff8e62388ed66c71d018ef3e1cf47","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:glaive","key":"glaive","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"glaive","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Items","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}

{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"正是在这里，萨莫奈人的使者们发现他正在灶角煮萝卜，便献给他一份黄金作礼物；但他将他们打发走了，并留下了这样的话：一个满足于这样一顿晚餐的人，不需要黄金；他认为征服那些拥有黄金的人，比拥有黄金本身更光荣。\"\n    ——普鲁塔克，\"马库斯·加图\"，《希腊罗马名人传》。公元75年。\n    约翰·德莱顿英译，1683年。","current_english":"“Here it was that the ambassadors of the Samnites, finding him boiling turnips\n in the chimney corner, offered him a present of gold; but he sent them away\n with this saying; that he, who was content with such a supper, had no need of\n gold; and that he thought it more honourable to conquer those who possessed\n the gold, than to possess the gold itself.”\n    -Plutarch, “Marcus Cato”, _Lives_. 75 AD.\n    trans. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"而迪尔米德啊，迪尔米德，他在战斗中丧生；\n他的头颅被挑在高高的戟上；\n他的旗帜被践踏：他毫无生还之机，\n即便主上帝亲自站在一旁！——\n哦，哀哉！\"\n    ——詹姆斯·克拉伦斯·曼根，《告别帕特里克·萨斯菲尔德，卢坎伯爵》。约1840年。","current_english":"“And Diarmid oh, Diarmid he perished in the strife;\n His head it was spiked upon a halberd high;\n His colours they were trampled: he had no chance of life\n If the Lord God Himself stood by!—\n  Och, ochone!”\n    -James Clarence Mangan , _A Farewell to Patrick Sarsfield, Earl of Lucan_.\n     ca. 1840.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Items","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:1174","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:1024","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"bc4a381a438d213261abb2be5fd55de2b6c9dc9f7cbde0e692b5f68c2ed2dc27","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:halberd","key":"halberd","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"halberd","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Items","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}

{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"丽兹·波登拿起一把斧头，\n给了她母亲四十下。\n当她看到自己的所作所为，\n又给了她父亲四十一下。\"\n    ——流行跳绳童谣，1893年后。","current_english":"“Lizzie Borden took an axe\n And gave her mother forty whacks.\n When she saw what she had done\n She gave her father forty-one.”\n    -A popular skipping-rope rhyme, after 1893.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Items","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:1193","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:1042","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"05d1045d7b28cbc8751810e4dd4083ca481c775518d5db7ce4ed7e5eea296bf9","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:hand axe","key":"hand axe","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"hand axe","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Items","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"我不以手瞄准；以手瞄准的人已忘记了他父亲的面容。\n我以眼瞄准。\n我不以手射击；以手射击的人已忘记了他父亲的面容。\n我以意志射击。\n我不以枪杀人；以枪杀人的人已忘记了他父亲的面容。\n我以心杀人。\"\n    ——斯蒂芬·金，《黑暗塔III：荒原》。","current_english":"“I do not aim with my hand; he who aims with his hand has forgotten the face of\n his father.\n I aim with my eye.\n I do not shoot with my hand; he who shoots with his hand has forgotten the face\n of his father.\n I shoot with my mind.\n I do not kill with my gun; he who kills with his gun has forgotten the face of\n his father.\n I kill with my heart.”\n    —Stephen King, “The Dark Tower III: The Waste Lands”","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Items","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:1201","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:1050","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"af4a5ab428a55d673e581e38f16279eb4f5c542221ba644c64437ada705d9992","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:hand cannon","key":"hand cannon","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"hand cannon","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Items","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"于是约书亚召集祭司，说：'抬起主之约柜，并指派七名祭司走在前面，每人手持一只公羊角。'\n\n当民众听到羊角的声音，他们尽力大声呼喊。突然，耶利哥的城墙倒塌了，以色列人径直冲入城中，夺取了它。他们用刀剑彻底毁灭了城中一切——男女老幼、牛羊驴畜。\"\n    ——《约书亚记》6:6,20-21，新生活译本。","current_english":"“So Joshua called together the priests and said, “Take up the Ark of the Lord's\n Covenant, and assign seven priests to walk in front of it, each carrying a\n ram's horn.\n\n When the people heard the sound of the rams’ horns, they shouted as loud as\n they could. Suddenly, the walls of Jericho collapsed, and the Israelites\n charged straight into the town and captured it. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"假设你发现你的兄弟和你的妻子在床上，用一支标枪刺穿两人，你将是正当的，他们将为他们的罪赎罪，并被接纳进入上帝的国度。\"\n    ——杨百翰，《讲道集》，3:247。1856年。","current_english":"“Suppose you found your brother in bed with your wife, and put a javelin\n through both of them, you would be justified, and they would atone for their\n sins, and be received into the kingdom of God.”\n    -Brigham Young, _Journal of Discourses_, 3:247. 1856.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Items","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:1237","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:1077","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"08de60857988b0dd0bf655738a77f5c5460215975182cbe9c0abae9aab0e89cc","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:javelin","key":"javelin","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"javelin","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Items","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}

{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"一件武器，落下时如\n雪花飘落草地的静谧；\n却执行自由人的意志，\n如闪电执行上帝的旨意。\"\n    ——约翰·皮尔庞特，\"选票\"。约1850年。","current_english":"“A weapon that comes down as still\n As snowflakes fall upon the sod;\n But executes a freeman's will,\n As lightning does the will of God.”\n    -John Pierpont, “The Ballot”. ca. 1850.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Items","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:1249","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:1087","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"37e7a6f492fc30c99913fe5f938422f20625404d09bbf86f6eff275ed0ef78e6","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:lajatang","key":"lajatang","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"lajatang","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Items","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}

{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"当我们与这支大军及其可怕的阔剑（马夸维特尔，以黑曜石制成）搏斗时，许多最强大的敌人似乎决定要俘获一匹马。他们发起猛攻，将手伸向一匹训练有素、既善游戏又善战斗的好母马。她的骑手佩德罗·德·莫隆是一位优秀的骑手；当他与另外三名骑手一同冲入敌阵时——他们受命一同冲锋以互相支援——有些人抓住了他的长矛使他无法使用，另一些人则用他们的阔剑（马夸维特尔）向他砍去，使他身负重伤。然后他们砍向他的母马，将马头从脖颈处砍下，仅剩皮肤相连。母马倒地而死，若非他的骑兵同伴们赶来救援，莫隆恐怕也会被杀。\"\n    ——贝尔纳尔·迪亚斯·德尔·卡斯蒂略，《征服新西班牙信史》。1623年。\n    J.M.科恩英译，1963年。","current_english":"“While we were at grips with this great army and their dreadful broadswords\n (maquahuitl [made of obsidian]), many of the most powerful among the enemy\n seem to have decided to capture a horse. They began with a furious attack, and\n laid hands on a good mare well trained both for sport and battle. Her rider,\n Pedro de Moron, was a fine horseman; and as he charged with three other\n horsemen into the enemy ranks—they had been instructed to charge together for\n mutual support—some of them seized his lance so he could not use it, and\n others slashed at him with their broadswords (maquahuitl), wounding him\n severely. Then they slashed at his mare, cutting her head at the neck so that\n it only hung by the skin. The mare fell dead, and if his mounted comrades had\n not come to Moron's rescue, he would probably have been killed also.”\n    -Bernal Díaz del Castillo, _The Conquest of New Spain_. 1623.\n     trans. J.M.Cohen, 1963.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Items","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:1274","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:1112","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"e32089d6d318bd51f0e532daea578ba39d7c0774a4ff7b75499dda3341b8e12c","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:long sword","key":"long sword","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"long sword","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Items","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}

{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"他那羽毛的华饰与闪耀的\n黄金之傲，在淬炼的剑锋前\n毫无用处；他的头盔——\n饰以羽冠，或雕满恐怖图案——\n也无济于事，当不列颠人的手\n将带刺的钉头锤刺入盔与颅；\n或以摧枯拉朽之势挥舞其铁质重球，\n所落之处，尽皆毁灭。\"\n    ——罗伯特·骚塞，《马多克》。1805年。","current_english":"“Little did then his pomp of plumes bestead\n The Azteca, or glittering pride of gold.\n Against the tempered sword; little his casque,\n Cay with its feathery coronal, or drest\n In graven terrors, when the Briton's hand\n Drove in through helm and head the spiked mace;\n Or swung its iron weights with shattering sway.\n Which, where they fell, destroyed.”\n    -Robert Southey, _Madoc_. 1805.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Items","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:1310","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:1136","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"b46aa93523f21eedee9148da521ac156ea4300f2d51483c84783b7d04513ee54","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:morningstar","key":"morningstar","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"morningstar","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Items","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}

{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"于是我依计给了她——\n一瓶安眠之药；那药果然\n如我所愿起了作用，因为它在她身上\n造成了死亡的假象：同时我写信给罗密欧\n让他赶在这可怕之夜来此，\n帮她离开这座借来的坟墓，\n因为那时药力应当正好消失。\"\n    ——威廉·莎士比亚，《罗密欧与朱丽叶》。","current_english":"“Then gave I her, — so tutor'd by my art, —\n A sleeping potion; which so took effect\n As I intended, for it wrought on her\n The form of death: meantime I writ to Romeo\n That he should hither come as this dire night,\n To help to take her from her borrow'd grave,\n Being the time the potion's force should cease.”\n    -William Shakespeare, _Romeo and Juliet_","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Items","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:1343","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:1164","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"c653f05e3e48bdf716f85faf35ac5b175c1a9e4b93fd344b11641dfa82f8a883","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:potion","key":"potion","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"potion","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Items","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}

{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"我永远不会忘记那个黎明，以及看到我双手变得如磨砂玻璃一般的奇异恐怖，看着它们随着白日推移变得越来越清晰、越来越薄，直到最后我能透过它们看到我房间里的病态混乱，尽管我闭着透明的眼睑。我的四肢变得玻璃般透明，骨骼和动脉逐渐消隐、消失，最后消失的是那些白色的小神经。我咬紧牙关，在那里坚持到了最后。最终只剩下死白的指尖，苍白而发白，还有我手指上某种酸液留下的褐色污渍。\"\n    ——H.G.威尔斯，《隐形人》，第二十章。1897年。","current_english":"“I shall never forget that dawn, and the strange horror of seeing that my hands\n had become as clouded glass, and watching them grow clearer and thinner as the\n day went by, until at last I could see the sickly disorder of my room through\n them, though I closed my transparent eyelids. My limbs became glassy, the bones\n and arteries faded, vanished, and the little white nerves went last. I gritted\n my teeth and stayed there to the end. At last only the dead tips of the\n fingernails remained, pallid and white, and the brown stain of some acid, upon\n my fingers.”\n    -H.G. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"但如果你说'威士忌'时指的是魔鬼的酿造、剧毒的\n祸害、血腥的怪物，它玷污纯真、废黜\n理智、摧毁家庭、制造苦难与贫穷，甚至\n从幼小孩童的口中夺走面包……\n那么我当然反对。\"\n    ——诺亚·S·斯威特二世，\"如果我说威士忌\"。1952年。","current_english":"“If when you say ‘whiskey’ you mean the devil's brew, the poison\n scourge, the bloody monster, that defiles innocence, dethrones\n reason, destroys the home, creates misery and poverty, yea,\n literally takes the bread from the mouths of little children...\n then certainly I am against it.”\n    -Noah S. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"于是罗宾解开他的腰带，\n放下他那把长弓；\n他拿起一根橡木棍，\n又硬又强又结实。\n'但让我量一量，'快活的罗宾说，\n'在我们动手之前；\n因为我不愿我的比你的长，\n那会被算作不公平。'\n'我不在乎长短，'勇敢的亚瑟答道，\n'我的棍是上好的橡木；\n八尺半长，能打倒一头小牛，\n我希望它也能打倒你。'\n于是罗宾再也按捺不住，\n他给了他一记重击，\n迅疾之间，血就淌了下来，\n还没到十点钟。\n他们转来转去，转来转去，\n像两头追逐的野猪，\n竭力想打残对方，\n大腿、手臂，或任何地方。\n一击换一击，他们急促地对打，\n持续了两小时有余；\n每次撞击，整座树林都回响，\n他们打得如此猛烈。\"\n    ——佚名，\"罗宾汉与制革匠\"。","current_english":"“Then Robin he unbuckled his belt,\n And laid down his bow so long;\n He took up a staff of another oak graff,\n That was both stiff and strong.\n ‘But let me measure,’ said jolly Robin,\n ‘Before we begin our fray;\n For I'll not have mine to be longer than thine,\n For that will be counted foul play.’\n ‘I pass not for length,’ bold Arthur replied,\n ‘My staff is of oak so free;\n Eight foot and a half it will knock down a calf,\n And I hope it will knock down thee.’\n Then Robin could no longer forbear,\n He gave him such a knock,\n Quickly and soon the blood came down,\n Before it was ten o'clock.\n About and about and about they went,\n Like two wild boars in a chase,\n Striving to aim each other to maim,\n Leg, arm, or any other place.\n And knock for knock they hastily dealt,\n Which held for two hours and more;\n That all the wood rang at every bang,\n They plied their work so sore.”\n    -Anonymous, “Robin Hood and the {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"有人说世界将毁于火；\n有人说毁于冰。\n从我尝过的欲望滋味来看\n我赞同那些倾向于火的人。\n但倘若它必须毁灭两次，\n我想我对仇恨了解得足够多\n以至于可以说，就毁灭而言，冰\n也同样出色\n且足矣。\"\n    ——罗伯特·弗罗斯特，\"火与冰\"。1920年。","current_english":"“Some say the world will end in fire;\n Some say in ice.\n From what I've tasted of desire\n I hold with those who favor fire.\n But if it had to perish twice,\n I think I know enough of hate\n To say that for destruction ice\n Is also great\n And would suffice.”\n    -Robert Frost, “Fire and Ice”. 1920.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Items","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:1492","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:1303","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"3a4986e71efdf75c0501f5a2b88052c8c02eda40097b833f613270dad003a3cf","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:ring of ice","key":"ring of ice","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"ring of ice","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Items","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}

{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"这三个人，沙得拉、米煞和亚伯尼歌，被捆着落入烈火燃烧的窑中。\n\n那时尼布甲尼撒王惊奇，急忙起来，对谋士说：我们捆起来扔在火里的不是三个人吗？他们回答王说：王啊，是的。\n\n王说：看哪，我见有四个人，并没有捆绑，在火中行走，也没有受伤；那第四个的相貌好像神子。\n\n于是尼布甲尼撒走近烈火燃烧的窑门，说：至高神的仆人沙得拉、米煞、亚伯尼歌，出来，上这里来吧。沙得拉、米煞、亚伯尼歌就从火中出来了。\n\n那些总督、巡抚、首领和王的谋士一同聚集来看这三个人，见火无力伤他们的身体，头发也没有烧焦，衣裳也没有变色，并没有火燎的气味。\"\n    ——圣经和合本，《但以理书》3:23-27。","current_english":"“And these three men, Shadrach, Meshach, and Abednego, fell down bound into the\n midst of the burning fiery furnace.\n\n Then Nebuchadnezzar the king was astonished, and rose up in haste, and spake,\n and said unto his counsellors, Did not we cast three men bound into the midst\n of the fire? They answered and said unto the king, True, O king.\n\n He answered and said, Lo, I see four men loose, walking in the midst of the\n fire, and they have no hurt; and the form of the fourth is like the Son of God.\n\n Then Nebuchadnezzar came near to the mouth of the burning fiery furnace, and\n spake, and said, Shadrach, Meshach, and Abednego, ye servants of the most high\n God, come forth, and come hither. Then Shadrach, Meshach, and Abednego, came\n forth of the midst of the fire.\n\n And the princes, governors, and captains, and the king’s counsellors, being\n gathered together, saw these men, upon whose bodies the fire had no power, nor\n was an hair of their head singed, neither were their coats changed, nor the\n smell of fire had passed on them.”\n    -KJV Bible, Daniel 3:23-27.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Items","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:1534","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:1346","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"7cfa9512a7b54bb4e50a5f5d2f342244436054fcb1208cfb0ebfe19e90752291","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:ring of protection from fire","key":"ring of protection from fire","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"ring of protection from fire","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Items","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}

{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"我确实是一位君王，因为我知道如何统治自己。\"\n    ——彼得罗·阿雷蒂诺，致阿戈斯蒂诺·里奇先生的信，1537年5月10日。引自\n    《阿雷蒂诺作品集，自意大利原文译为英文》，\n    萨缪尔·帕特南英译。1926年。","current_english":"“I am, indeed, a king, because I know how to rule myself.”\n    -Pietro Aretino, letter to Mr. Agostino Ricchi, May 10th, 1537. from\n      _The Works of Aretino, Translated into English from the original Italian_,\n      trans. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"博物馆和庞大的藏书室占据了整个底层——那里陈列着那些彼此矛盾、不可调和的书，它们不知怎地就是十九世纪的历史；那里还有来自尼沙普尔的弯刀，在其冰冷的弧形刀刃中，战斗的风与暴力似乎仍然生生不息。\"\n    ——豪尔赫·路易斯·博尔赫斯，《剑之形》。1953年。\n    安德鲁·赫尔利英译。","current_english":"“The museum-cabinet and huge library arrogated to themselves the entire lower\n floor — there were the controversial and incompatible books that are somehow\n the history of the nineteenth century; there were scimitars from Nishapur, in\n whose frozen crescents the wind and violence of battle seemed to be living\n on.”\n    -Jorge Luis Borges, _The Form of the Sword_. 1953.\n     trans. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"随每一阵激情飘荡，直到我的灵魂\n变成一面弦琴，任一切风来弹拨，\n难道就是为了这个，我才放弃了\n我那古老的智慧与严厉的自律？\n我想，我的生命是一张重写过的卷轴\n在某次稚气的假日里被胡乱涂鸦。\"\n    ——奥斯卡·王尔德，《唉》。1881年。","current_english":"“To drift with every passion till my soul\n Is a stringed lute on which all winds can play,\n Is it for this that I have given away\n Mine ancient wisdom, and austere control?\n Methinks my life is a twice-written scroll\n Scrawled over on some boyish holiday.”\n    -Oscar Wilde, _Helas_. 1881.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Items","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:1617","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:1413","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"a423816953f21ffa8422e389586436ee5d3bc34ac8283e823e18e6108b7dcc2e","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:scroll","key":"scroll","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"scroll","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Items","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"他已停止尝试思考。他只是反复念诵着他的祷文：\n\"我是一只动物，你看得出来。我不懂那些词，他们没教\n我那些词。我不知道怎么思考，那些混蛋不让我学\n怎么思考。但如果你真的是……全能的……全知的……那你自己\n想明白！看看我的心。我知道你需要的一切都在里面。\n一定在。我从来没有把我的灵魂出卖给任何人！它是我的，它是人的！你\n把我想要的东西拿走……我不可能想要什么\n坏东西！该死，我什么都想不出来，除了他的那些话……\n'人人幸福，免费，而且没有人会不满地离去！'\"\n    ——阿尔卡季和鲍里斯·斯特鲁加茨基，《路边野餐》。1972年。\n    安东尼娜·W·布伊斯英译，1977年。","current_english":"He had stopped trying to think. He just repeated his litany over and over:\n“I am an animal, you see that. I don’t have the words, they didn’t teach\nme the words. I don’t know how to think, the bastards didn’t let me learn\nhow to think. But if you really are... all-powerful... all-knowing... then you\nfigure it out! Look into my heart. I know that everything you need is in there.\nIt has to be. I never sold my soul to anyone! It’s mine, it’s human! You\ntake from me what it is I want... it just can’t be that I would want something\nbad! Damn it all, I can’t think of anything, except those words of his...\n‘HAPPINESS FOR EVERYBODY, FREE, AND NO ONE WILL GO AWAY UNSATISFIED!’”\n    -Arkady and Boris Strugatsky, “Roadside Picnic”. 1972.\n    trans. Antonina W. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"过去人们认为，改变世界的事件是像大型炸弹、疯子政客、巨大地震或大规模人口迁徙之类的东西，但现在人们已经认识到，这是一种非常过时的观点，持有这种观点的人完全与现代思想脱节。根据混沌理论，真正改变世界的，是那些微小的事物。一只蝴蝶在亚马逊丛林中扇动翅膀，随后一场风暴便肆虐了半个欧洲……\"\n    ——尼尔·盖曼与特里·普拉切特，《好兆头：女巫阿格妮丝·纳特精雅准确的预言》。1990年。","current_english":"“It used to be thought that the events that changed the world were things like\n big bombs, maniac politicians, huge earthquakes, or vast population movements,\n but it has now been realized that this is a very old-fashioned view held by\n people totally out of touch with modern thought. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"这个事物，就其本身，按它自身的构造，它究竟是什么？它的实质是什么？它的材料或功用是什么？它的形式或动力因是什么？它在这个世界上是为了什么，又能存在多久？你必须这样审察一切呈现在你面前的事物。\"\n    ——马可·奥勒留，《沉思录》，第八卷第十章。梅里克·卡索邦英译，1634年。","current_english":"“This, what is it in itself, and by itself, according to its proper\n constitution? What is the substance of it? What is the matter, or\n proper use? What is the form or efficient cause? What is it for in\n this world, and how long will it abide? Thus must thou examine all\n things, that present themselves unto thee.”\n    -Marcus Aurelius, _Marcus Aurelius Antoninus - His Meditations concerning\n     himselfe_, Book VIII, X. trans. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"'口袋地图是多么有用的东西啊！'我说道。\n'这是我们向贵国学到的另一件事，'迈因·赫尔说，'地图制作。不过我们比你们走得远得多。\n    你认为真正有用的最大的地图是多大比例尺？'\n'大约六英寸兑一英里吧。'\n'才六英寸！'迈因·赫尔叫道。'我们很快就做到了六码兑一英里。然后我们尝试了一百码兑一英里。\n    接着，最伟大的想法诞生了！我们真的制作了一幅国家的地图，比例尺为一英里兑一英里！'\n'你们用得很多吗？'我问道。\n'它还没有被展开过，'迈因·赫尔说：'农民们反对：他们说它会覆盖\n    整个国家，遮住阳光！所以我们现在把国家本身当作自己的地图，\n    我向你保证，效果也差不多。'\"\n    ——刘易斯·卡罗尔，《西尔维与布鲁诺终结篇》，第十一章。1895年。","current_english":"“‘What a useful thing a pocket-map is!’ I remarked.\n ‘That’s another thing we’ve learned from your Nation,’ said Mein Herr,\n    ‘map-making. But we’ve carried it much further than you. What do you\n    consider the largest map that would be really useful?’\n ‘About six inches to the mile.’\n ‘Only six inches!’ exclaimed Mein Herr. ‘We very soon got to six yards to the\n     mile. Then we tried a hundred yards to the mile. And then came the grandest\n     idea of all! We actually made a map of the country, on the scale of a mile\n     to the mile!’\n ‘Have you used it much?’ I enquired.\n ‘It has never been spread out, yet,’ said Mein Herr: ‘the farmers objected:\n     they said it would cover the whole country, and shut out the sunlight! So\n     we now use the country itself, as its own map, and I assure you it does\n     nearly as well.’”\n    -Lewis Carroll, _Sylvie and Bruno Concluded_, Chapter XI. 1895","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Items","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:1703","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:1486","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"345408358992965cb352cf045b8368533f576d01dde4024674386322ea2fa93a","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:scroll of revelation","key":"scroll of revelation","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"scroll of revelation","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Items","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"图书馆所在地的性质——一所谣传收容着可怜畸形者的疗养院——对毫无'正常人际交流'欲望的人成了一种诱惑。我记得有一个案例，一个在各方面都正常的男人，在言辞、推理和职业实践上，都无从挑剔，但唯有一点偏离了这种'正常'：他不可解释地需要将成千上万张大页纸上写满看似毫无意义的词语、近乎哀嚎之音的音节、无休无止的愤怒与痛苦的呼喊、残缺的句段，以及写给天知道是谁的哀求。\"\n    ——乔尔乔·德·马里亚，《都灵二十日》。1975年。\n    拉蒙·格拉佐夫英译，2016年。","current_english":"“The nature of the place where the Library was founded—a sanatorium rumored to\n harbor the pitiably deformed—had proven a lure to people with no desire at all\n for ‘regular human communication.’ I remember the case of a man who was normal\n in every respect, in words, in reasoning, in the practice of his profession,\n but strayed from this ‘normalcy’ in only one way: his inexplicable need to fill\n thousands of sheets of foolscap paper with seemingly meaningless words,\n phonemes close to wailing sounds, cries of fury and pain in relentless\n successions, fragments of sentences and pleas addressed to God-knows-whom.”\n    -Giorgio de Maria, _The Twenty Days of Turin_, 1975.\n     trans. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"在赤裸的光中我看见\n一万人，或许更多。\n人们在交谈却不说话，\n人们在聆听却不倾听；\n人们写着歌\n那些从未被声音分享的歌：\n没有人敢\n打扰那寂静\n之声。\"\n    ——西蒙与加芬克尔，《寂静之声》。1964年。","current_english":"“And in the naked light I saw\n   Ten thousand people, maybe more.\n People talking without speaking,\n   People hearing without listening;\n People writing songs\n   That voices never shared:\n No one dared\n   Disturb the sound\n   Of silence.”\n    -Simon & Garfunkel, _The Sound of Silence_. 1964.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Items","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:1734","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:1506","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"ac6d7a8cece8d4fafb555d60c16f6946f9cb0ef8d2e205cecc76e7bb34fb5626","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:scroll of silence","key":"scroll of silence","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"scroll of silence","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Items","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"当你荣耀地从天降临，在天空显现，从你那里派遣\n召唤的天使长，宣告你威严的审判庭。\"\n    ——约翰·弥尔顿，《失乐园》，第三卷。1667年。","current_english":"“When thou attended gloriously from heaven, Shalt in the sky appear, and from\n thee send Thy summoning archangels to proclaim Thy dread tribunal.”\n    -John Milton, _Paradise Lost_, Book III, 1667.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Items","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:1747","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:1519","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"8c74a1be7cc68bb89ae3b89d586c647dac9e372755aaa3621bb8fe500cc9d314","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:scroll of summoning","key":"scroll of summoning","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":"\"当你荣耀地从天降临，在天空显现，从你那里派遣\n传召众生的大天使，宣告你威严的审判庭。\"\n    ——约翰·弥尔顿，《失乐园》，第三卷。1667年。","reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["保留现译；会继续留下已由英文正文或当前术语直接证明的偏差。"],"resolved_key":"scroll of summoning","reviewer_rationale":"summoning archangels 是执行传召/召集的天使长，不是“被召唤的天使长”；恢复分词的主动功能。","section":"Items","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"adjust"}

{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"这是本能。如闪电击而不伤一般不合逻辑。每日谷物都必须收割。必须收割。为什么？嗯，就是必须，就是这样。他对着大手中的镰刀笑了笑。然后，吹着口哨，他把它带到那片成熟而等待的麦田，干了起来。他觉得自己有点疯了。见鬼，这本来就是一片很普通的麦田，不是吗？\"\n    ——雷·布拉德伯里，《镰刀》。1943年。","current_english":"“It was instinct. Illogical as lightning striking and not hurting. Each day the\n grain must be cut. It had to be cut. Why? Well, it just did, that was all. He\n laughed at the scythe in his big hands. Then, whistling, he took it out to the\n ripe and waiting field and did the work. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"或炸开那消逝英雄的高耸坟丘；\n在那遥远的孤寂海岸上他安眠着：\n他倒下了，倒下时万民哀悼；\n但如今，千千万万悲恸者中无人哭泣，\n也无人如传说所载，为那显圣的\n半神守夜。从那散乱的骸骨堆中\n移开那颗颅骨：那便是\n神灵曾居的庙堂吗？\n为何连蛆虫最终都鄙弃这破碎的居所！\n\n看那断裂的拱门，那残破的墙壁，\n那荒废的厅堂，那污秽的门廊：\n是的，这曾是野心的空中殿堂，\n思想的穹顶，灵魂的宫殿：\n透过每一对暗淡无光的空洞眼窝，\n看那智慧与才思的欢快密室，\n与那从不甘受控制的激情之群：\n一切圣贤哲人术士写就的篇章，\n可能住满这孤寂的高塔，修缮这座寓所？\"\n    ——拜伦勋爵，《恰尔德·哈罗德游记》，第二歌，第五节至第六节。","current_english":"“Or burst the vanish’d Hero’s lofty mound;\n Far on the solitary shore he sleeps:\n He fell, and falling nations mourn’d around;\n But now not one of saddening thousands weeps,\n Nor warlike worshipper his vigil keeps\n Where demi-gods appear’d, as records tell.\n Remove yon skull from out the scatter’d heaps:\n Is that a temple where a God may dwell?\n Why ev’n the worm at last disdains her shatter’d cell!\n\n Look on its broken arch, its ruin’d wall,\n Its chambers desolate, and portals foul:\n Yes, this was once Ambition’s airy hall,\n The dome of Thought, the palace of the Soul:\n Behold through each lack-lustre, eyeless hole,\n The gay recess of Wisdom and of Wit\n And Passion’s host, that never brook’d control:\n Can all saint, sage, or sophist ever writ,\n People this lonely tower, this tenement refit?”\n    -Lord Byron, _Childe Harold's Pilgrimage_, Canto the Second, Stanzas V-VI","decision_authority":"Issue #72 {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"芭蕉和尚示众云：'你有拄杖子，我与你拄杖子；你无拄杖子，我夺你拄杖子。'\n\n无门评曰：\n\n扶过断桥水，伴归无月村。若唤作拄杖，入地狱如箭。\"\n    ——无门慧开，《无门关》，第四十四则。1228年。\n    关毅峰英译。","current_english":"“Bashō Osho said to his disciples, ‘If you have a staff, I will give you a\n staff. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"'我是埃斯鲁艾德的埃德·阿拜德，即达努神族的善巫术之神，我又名鲁阿德·罗费萨和埃奥凯德·奥拉泰尔，这是我的三个名字。'\n\n他如此自报，背上背着他在与爱尔兰至尊王、基安之子卢格的战斗与格斗中倒下的儿子之一，克尔迈特·米尔贝尔。达格达求助于他的知识与学问，于是在克尔迈特的遗体周围放上了乳香、没药和药草，然后他背起克尔迈特，扛着他周游世界，来到了大东方世界。\n\n他遇到三个正带着父亲财宝赶路的男子。达格达向他们打听消息，他们说：'我们是同父同母的三兄弟，正在分配父亲的财宝。'\n'你们有什么？'达格达问。\n'一件衬衣、一根杖和一件斗篷，'他们说。\n'这些东西有什么功效？'达格达问。\n'你看到的这根大杖，'他说，'有一端光滑，一端粗糙。一端杀生，另一端使死者复生。'\"\n    ——奥斯本·伯金，\"达格达如何获得他的魔法杖\"，《纪念格特鲁德·舍珀尔·卢米斯的中世纪研究》。1927年。","current_english":"“'I am Aed Abaid of Ess Rúaid, that is, the good god of wizardry of the Tuatha\n Dé Danann, and the Rúad Rofhessa, and Eochaid Ollathair are my three names.’\n\n And thus he was, with Cermait Milbél, one of his sons, on his back, who had\n fallen in fight and combat by Lug, son of Cian, High King of Ireland. The\n Dagda betook himself to his knowledge and learning, and therefore frankincense\n and myrrh and herbs were put around the body of Cermait, and he lifted Cermait\n on his back, and bearing Cermait he searched the world, and came to the great\n eastern world.\n\n He met three men going the road and the way with their father's treasures. The\n Dagda asked news of them, and they said ‘We are three sons of one father and\n mother, and we are sharing our father's treasures.’\n ‘What have ye?’ said the Dagda.\n ‘A shirt and a staff and a cloak,’ said they.\n ‘What virtues have these?’ said the Dagda.\n ‘This great staff that thou seest,’ said he, ‘has a smooth end and a rough\n end. One end slays the living, and the other end brings the dead to life.’”\n    -Osborn Bergin, “How the Dagda Got His Magic Staff”, _Medieval Studies in\n     Memory of Gertrude Schoepperle Loomis_. 1927.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Items","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:1886","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:1646","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"7e390ca24b1448dc12899610c76eaee4e87bdeca7ecfa6b349e7d28507464baa","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:staff of necromancy","key":"staff of necromancy","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"staff of necromancy","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Items","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}

{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"巫师忽然想起了神明的话。他想起，在遍布大地的万物之中，唯有火知道他的儿子不过是一个幻影。这记忆起初使他平静，最后却折磨着他。他担心他的儿子会思索这反常的特权，并以某种方式发现自己仅是一个仿像。不是一个人，只是另一个人梦境的投射——这是何等无法比拟的屈辱，何等疯狂！\"\n    ——豪尔赫·路易斯·博尔赫斯，《环形废墟》。1940年。\n    安东尼·邦纳英译，1962年。","current_english":"“The wizard suddenly remembered the words of the god. He remembered that of all\n the creatures that people the earth, Fire was the only one who knew his son to\n be a phantom. This memory, which at first calmed him, ended by tormenting him.\n He feared lest his son should meditate on this abnormal privilege and by some\n means find out he was a mere simulacrum. Not to be a man, to be a projection\n of another man's dreams—what an incomparable humiliation, what madness!”\n    -Jorge Luis Borges, _The Circular Ruins_. 1940.\n    trans. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"它以骄傲的鳞甲为甲胄，紧紧闭合，如严密封印。鳞片片片相连，连空气也无法透入其间。它们彼此联结，互相胶结，不能分离。\"\n    ——圣经和合本，《约伯记》41:15-17。","current_english":"“His scales are his pride, shut up together as with a close seal. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"那小石子多么快乐\n独自在路上漫步徜徉，\n不在乎什么前程事业\n也不畏惧什么急难困厄——\n披着质朴的棕色外套\n那外套是路过的宇宙所披上，\n像太阳一样独立不倚\n独自相伴或独自发光，\n以漫不经心的淳朴\n践行着绝对的谕旨——\"\n    ——艾米莉·狄金森，\"那小石子多么快乐\"。约1865年。","current_english":"“How happy is the little Stone\n That rambles in the Road alone,\n And doesn't care about Careers\n And Exigencies never fears —\n Whose Coat of elemental Brown\n A passing Universe put on,\n And independent as the Sun\n Associates or glows alone,\n Fulfilling absolute Decree\n In casual simplicity —”\n    -Emily Dickinson, “How happy is the little Stone”. ca. 1865.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt consumers","proposed_translation":"\"那小石子多么快乐\n独自在路上漫步徜徉，\n不在乎什么前程事业\n也不畏惧什么急难困厄——\n披着质朴的棕色外套\n那外套是路过的宇宙所披上，\n像太阳一样独立不倚\n或与万物为伴，或独自发光，\n以漫不经心的淳朴\n践行着绝对的谕旨——\"\n {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"据说风暴形态会带来\n风雨交加的暴烈，\n当心它的力量，当心它的力量。\n虽然它的到来为诸神带来黑夜，\n它却召唤出血红的灵。\n当心它的结局，当心它的结局。\"\n    ——布兰登·桑德森，《光辉之语》。2014年。","current_english":"“Stormform is said to cause\n A tempest of winds and showers,\n Beware its powers, beware its powers.\n Though its coming brings the gods their night,\n It obliges a bloodred spren.\n Beware its end, beware its end.”\n    -Brandon Sanderson, “Words of Radiance”. 2014.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Items","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:1949","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:1692","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"2c2309d3146818a74128044cb0660f76c6a78249ac4590079464d14bffbfc568","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:storm talisman","key":"storm talisman","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"storm talisman","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Items","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}

{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"其间一两日的活动无需赘述，因为它们不过是一些寻常的林间娱乐，如射猎与追猎之类，并无足够有趣之事以留住读者，我们便直接跳到一种在某种程度上为苏格兰所特有的活动，可称之为一种猎鲑。这种追逐，用带倒刺的长矛或一种名为'沃斯特'的长柄三叉戟来追捕和刺击鱼类，在埃斯克河口及苏格兰其他鲑鱼河流中极为盛行。\"\n    ——沃尔特·司各特爵士，《盖伊·曼纳林》，第二十六章。1815年。","current_english":"“Without noticing the occupations of an intervening day or two, which, as they\n consisted of the ordinary sylvan amusements of shooting and coursing, have\n nothing sufficiently interesting to detain the reader, we pass to one in some\n degree peculiar to Scotland, which may be called a sort of salmon-hunting.\n This chase, in which the fish is pursued and struck with barbed spears, or a\n sort of long shafted trident, called a waster, is much practised at the mouth\n of the Esk, and in the other salmon rivers of Scotland.”\n    -Sir Walter Scott, _Guy Mannering_, ch. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"巨魔王：听着，皮尔王子，听从理性吧！\n你天生就是当巨魔的料。你看，你早已\n摆出一副十足的巨魔作风了！\n而你也想变成巨魔，不是吗？\n皮尔·金特：当然。\n换一个新娘和一个殷实的王国\n我不介意牺牲点什么；\n但万事都有其自然限度。\n我确实装了条尾巴；可是，\n我可以解开我们朋友系上的结\n把它取下来。我脱掉了马裤；\n它们又旧又破；但这不妨碍我\n想穿的时候就穿上。\n我大概会发现，适应\n你们巨魔的生活方式也一样容易。\n我可以轻易发誓说母牛是少女；\n誓言不是什么难以下咽的东西。\n但知道自己永远无法重获自由——\n连作为人死去都不行——\n作为山中巨魔了此一生——\n永远回不去，正如你明白告诉我的——\n那是我绝不会屈从的事。\"\n    ——亨利克·易卜生，《培尔·金特》。1867年。","current_english":"“THE TROLL KING: Now, listen, Prince Peer, and give way to reason!\n You're cut out for a Troll. Why, look, already\n You bear yourself quite in a Troll-like fashion!\n And you want to become one, don't you?\n PEER GYNT: Of course.\n In return for a bride and a well-found kingdom\n I'm not unwilling to sacrifice something;\n But all things have their natural limit.\n I have taken a tail, it is true; but, then,\n I can undo the knots that our friend has tied\n And take the thing off. I have shed my breeches;\n They were old and patched; but that won't prevent me\n From putting them on if I have a mind to.\n I shall probably find it just as easy\n To deal with your Trollish way of living.\n I can easily swear that a cow's a maiden;\n An oath's not a difficult thing to swallow.\n But to know that one never can get one's freedom —\n Not even to die as a human being —\n To end one's days as a Troll of the mountains —\n Never go back, as you tell me plainly —\n That is a thing that I'll not submit to.”\n    -Henrik Ibsen, _Peer Gynt_ . 1867.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Items","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:2011","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:1747","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"19518c934cbd4a4566ef83fb3cd7e33aab6cb2346c869627dcc041b9a674791b","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:troll leather armour","key":"troll leather armour","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"troll leather armour","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Items","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}

{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"但这不是作为装饰贴面的魅力，不是作为润滑剂或软膏的魅力，\n而是那种辨识出自己、并在任何遇见之处走出去迎接\n他人身上那个自己的魅力。\"\n    ——迈克尔·霍夫曼，\"天啊，他会冻僵的！\"，《伦敦书评》，2022年。\n\n\"他举起手，他们都停了下来，我觉得他似乎是在说：'所有这些生命我都要给你，是的，还有更多更伟大的生命，跨越无数世代，只要你肯俯伏敬拜我！'然后一团红色的云，如血的颜色，似乎笼罩了我的眼睛，在我还没意识到自己在做什么的时候，我发现自己已经打开了窗扇，对他说：'进来吧，主与主人！'\"\n    ——布拉姆·斯托克，《德古拉》。1897年。","current_english":"“But it isn't charm as veneer, as facilitating ooze or unguent,\n but charm that identifies and goes out to meet itself in others\n wherever it may be met with.”\n    -Michael Hofmann, “Hell, he'll be frozen stiff!”, London Review of Books,\n     2022.\n\n“He held up his hand, and they all stopped, and I thought he seemed to be\n saying, ‘All these lives will I give you, ay, and many more and greater,\n through countless ages, if you will fall down and worship me!’ And then a red\n cloud, like the colour of blood, seemed to close over my eyes, and before I\n knew what I was doing, I found myself opening the sash and saying to Him,\n ‘Come in, Lord and Master!’”\n    -Bram Stoker, _Dracula_. 1897.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"Als Gregor Samsa eines Morgens aus unruhigen Träumen erwachte, fand er sich in seinem Bett zu einem ungeheueren Ungeziefer verwandelt.\"\n一天早晨，格里高尔·萨姆沙从不安的睡梦中醒来，发现自己躺在床上变成了一只巨大的甲虫。\n    ——弗朗茨·卡夫卡，《变形记》。1915年。","current_english":"“Als Gregor Samsa eines Morgens aus unruhigen Träumen erwachte, fand er sich in\n seinem Bett zu einem ungeheueren Ungeziefer verwandelt.”\n    -Franz Kafka, _Die Verwandlung_. 1915","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Items","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:2090","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:1817","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"6c4d4a117866a00538e9470731e8b9f33df3f2e562f864de1ff75a571aa2f229","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:wand of polymorph","key":"wand of polymorph","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"wand of polymorph","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Items","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}

{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"'愿上帝促成这亲吻，'马克斯说，凯蒂叹息着，\n双手紧合如祈祷的封缄，'愿上帝促成这斧头！'\"\n    ——伊莎贝拉·瓦朗西·克劳福德，\"马尔科姆的凯蒂：一个爱情故事\"，第一部分，《老斯普克斯关口、马尔科姆的凯蒂及其他诗篇》。1884年。","current_english":"“‘God speed the kiss,’ said Max, and Katie sigh'd,\n With pray'rful palms close seal'd, ‘God speed the axe!’”\n    -Isabella Valancey Crawford, “Malcolm's Katie: A Love Story”, Part I, _Old\n     Spookses' Pass, Malcolm's Katie and Other Poems_. 1884.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Items","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:2108","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:1835","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"5270d7b5de525eb2242cd8a4c552b1ecf5b09ba6f6fbc38c5660c2cff3a74b44","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:war axe","key":"war axe","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":"\"'愿上帝助这一吻成真，'马克斯说，凯蒂叹息着，\n双手紧合如祈祷的封缄，'愿上帝助这一斧成功！'\"\n    ——伊莎贝拉·瓦朗西·克劳福德，\"马尔科姆的凯蒂：一个爱情故事\"，第一部分，《老斯普克斯关口、马尔科姆的凯蒂及其他诗篇》。1884年。","reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["保留现译；会继续留下已由英文正文或当前术语直接证明的偏差。"],"resolved_key":"war axe","reviewer_rationale":"God speed 是祈愿“愿上帝助其成功”，不是“促成”斧头这一宾语；两处平行祈愿同步修复。","section":"Items","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"adjust"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"心碎，不靠棍棒\n也不靠石头——\n我曾知道一条小得\n看不见的鞭子\n抽打着那神奇的造物\n直到它倒下，\n然而那鞭子的名\n太过高贵，不该说出。\"\n    ——艾米莉·狄金森，\"心碎，不靠棍棒\"。约1865年。","current_english":"“Not with a Club, the Heart is broken\n Nor with a Stone —\n A Whip so small you could not see it\n I've known\n To lash the Magic Creature\n Till it fell,\n Yet that Whip's Name\n Too noble then to tell.”\n    -Emily Dickinson, “Not with a Club, the Heart is broken”. ca. 1865.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Items","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:2115","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:1841","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"808245fa4e3503d97f49823090a40a707fec8790957218d7442cc76b92bf7754","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:whip","key":"whip","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"whip","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Items","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}

{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"事实是，我是独一无二的。一个人能传递给别人的东西，我毫无兴趣；如同那位哲人，我认为书写这种技艺无法传达任何东西。恼人而琐碎的细枝末节在我的精神中没有立足之地——这精神只接纳一切宏大的事物。\"\n  -Jorge Luis Borges, \"阿斯忒里翁之家\". 1947.\n   trans. Antonios Sarhanis, 2008.","current_english":"“The fact is that I am unique. What a man can pass unto others does not\n interest me; like the philosopher, I think nothing is communicated by the art\n of writing. Annoying and trivial minutiae have no place in my spirit, a spirit\n which is receptive only to whatsoever is grand.”\n  -Jorge Luis Borges, “The House of Asterion”. 1947.\n   trans. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"那时统治那地方的是安泰俄斯，波塞冬之子。他惯于强迫陌生人与其角力，然后将他们杀死。赫拉克勒斯被迫与他角力，将他紧紧抱住，举到空中，折断了他的身体并将他杀死；因为安泰俄斯每当触及大地就会变得更强，因此有人说他是大地之子。\"\n    -Pseudo-Apollodorus , _Library and Epitome_, 2.5.11. ca. 150 BC.\n    trans. Sir James George Frazer, 1913.\n\n\"矿场里的工头，人称安泰俄斯，\n是个肥胖的巨人，留着浓密的黑胡子，\n他似乎真的从大地母亲那里汲取力量。\"\n    -Primo Levi, \"The Periodic Table\". 1975.","current_english":"“That country was then ruled by Antaeus, son of Poseidon, who used to kill\n strangers by forcing them to wrestle. Being forced to wrestle with him,\n Hercules hugged him, lifted him aloft, broke and killed him; for when he\n touched earth so it was that he waxed stronger, wherefore some said that he\n was a son of Earth.”\n    -Pseudo-Apollodorus , _Library and Epitome_, 2.5.11. ca. 150 BC.\n    trans. Sir James George Frazer, 1913.\n\n“The head foreman in the mine, called Antaeus,\n was an obese giant with a thick black beard\n who actually seemed to draw his strength from Mother Earth.”\n    -Primo Levi, “The Periodic Table”. 1975.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:2147","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:1870","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"237414e0583a4ca950fe9ab10f7c32efe85e996880a99cd4f491574fdbe85dcf","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:antaeus","key":"antaeus","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"antaeus","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"至于我自己，我另有营生：我撮合荒唐的婚姻；我让白发老翁娶少女，让主人娶仆役，让略有薄产的姑娘嫁给身无分文的多情郎。是我将奢侈、放荡、赌博和化学引入这个世界。我是第一本烹饪书的作者，是节庆、舞蹈、音乐、戏剧和最新时尚的发明者；总而言之，我就是阿斯摩蒂斯，绰号'双拐魔鬼'。\"\n    -Alain René Le Sage, _Asmodeus: Or, The Devil on Two Sticks_. 1707.","current_english":"“For myself, I have other occupations:  I make absurd matches; I marry\n greybeards with minors, masters with servants, girls with small fortunes with\n tender lovers who have none. It is I who introduced into this world luxury,\n debauchery, games of chance, and chemistry. I am the author of the first\n cookery book, the inventor of festivals, of dancing, music, plays, and of the\n newest fashions; in a word, I am ASMODEUS, surnamed The Devil on Two Sticks.”\n    -Alain René Le Sage, _Asmodeus: Or, The Devil on Two Sticks_. 1707.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:2162","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:1881","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"6cf2871e9d273453d9913362b2d90a31c8709c73e7383b62bb65368f1b4b3885","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:asmodeus","key":"asmodeus","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"asmodeus","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
