{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"从前有个长须老翁，\n他说：'果然不出我所料！\n两只猫头鹰加一只母鸡，\n四只云雀和一只鹪鹩，\n全在我的胡子里筑了巢！'\"\n    -Edward Lear, _A Book of Nonsense [No. 1]_. 1846.","current_english":"“There was an Old Man with a beard,\n Who said, ‘It is just as I feared!\n Two Owls and a Hen,\n Four Larks and a Wren,\n Have all built their nests in my beard!’”\n    -Edward Lear, _A Book of Nonsense [No. 1]_. 1846.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:2185","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:1899","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"743b4f71b707f3b4f7c0d9c62dd6eff31eedee8d2f6f25fc9b83d03761e6f7a3","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:crazy yiuf","key":"crazy yiuf","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"crazy yiuf","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"这同一件事，朱庇特更古老的名称更能说明：因为最初他被称为迪奥维斯和迪耶斯匹特，也就是'日父'；由此衍生出'神'（dei）一词，以及'dius'和'divum'，由此有'sub divo'（在天空下）和狄乌斯·菲狄乌斯。因此他的庙宇屋顶开有天窗，以便从中望见天穹（divum），也就是天空。有人认为不应在有屋顶的地方向此神发誓。埃利乌斯说狄乌斯·菲狄乌斯是迪奥维斯之子，正如希腊人称卡斯托尔为狄奥斯库波；他还认为此神就是萨宾语中的桑库斯，希腊语中的赫拉克勒斯。同样，这位迪斯帕特被称为最低者，他与大地相连，万物在那里诞生又消亡；事物消亡的终点，被称为奥库斯。\"\n    -Marcus Terentius Varro, _De Lingua Latina_, Liber V, circa 40 BC.","current_english":"“Hoc idem magis ostendit antiquius Iovis nomen: nam olim Diovis et Diespiter\n dictus, id est dies pater; a quo dei dicti qui inde, et dius et divum, unde\n sub divo, Dius Fidius. Itaque inde eius perforatum tectum, ut ea videatur\n divum, id est caelum. Quidam negant sub tecto per hunc deierare oportere.\n Aelius Dium Fidium dicebat Diovis filium, ut Graeci Dioskopon Castorem, et\n putabat hunc esse Sancum ab Sabina lingua et Herculem a Graeca. Idem hic Dis\n pater dicitur infimus, qui est coniunctus terrae, ubi omnia ut oriuntur ita\n aboriuntur; quorum quod finis ortuum, Orcus dictus.”\n    -Marcus Terentius Varro, _De Lingua Latina_, Liber V, circa 40 BC.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:2194","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:1908","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"4f7f0310854ff944fb938cad6c96850f5fd54a1231de9a23f3aa320aa04fc617","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:dispater","key":"dispater","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"dispater","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"技艺与优雅，这对孪生兄妹，正在你的指尖上欢快地舞蹈。\"\n    -Rabindranath Tagore, _Chitra_, Act I, Scene iv. 1914.","current_english":"“Skill and grace, the twin brother and sister, are dancing playfully on your\n finger tips.”\n    -Rabindranath Tagore, _Chitra_, Act I, Scene iv. 1914.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:2206","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:1913","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"7f9b316cf8a0c0a6de9871823c061ca1f3643305553f8a1753e1a7632fe0cdcd","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:dowan","key":"dowan","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"dowan","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"一对孪生子：女孩相貌平平；\n哥哥生得俊美又虚荣；\n父亲说：'让他去夸耀他的容貌，\n你只管专心读书！\n最美的美貌孕育在头脑之中。'\"\n    -Aesop & Walter Crane, _The Baby's Own Aesop_, 36: \"Brother & Sister\".\n1887.","current_english":"“Twin children: the Girl, she was plain;\n The Brother was handsome & vain;\n ‘Let him brag of his looks,’\n Father said; ‘mind your books!\n The best beauty is bred in the brain.’”\n    -Aesop & Walter Crane, _The Baby's Own Aesop_, 36: “Brother & Sister”.\n1887.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:2212","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:1918","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"124b055fafd57288456660588b3b30e966be035dc32a75e42b256b947951be64","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:duvessa","key":"duvessa","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"duvessa","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"既然我的计谋得逞，庶子埃德蒙\n必将凌驾嫡出之上。我蒸蒸日上，步步高升——\n如今，众神啊，为私生子撑腰吧！\"\n    -William Shakespeare, _King Lear_, I, ii. 1606.\n\n\"当两军列阵之际，埃德蒙提议以单挑决胜负；但克努特说，他身材矮小，与高大健壮的埃德蒙决斗，毫无胜算，于是反过来提议，不如像他们的父辈那样平分王国。\"\n    -Thomas Keightley, _The History of England_. 1839.","current_english":"“And my invention thrive, Edmund the base\n Shall top the legitimate. I grow; I prosper.—\n Now, gods, stand up for bastards!”\n    -William Shakespeare, _King Lear_, I, ii. 1606.\n\n“When the forces stood in array Edmund proposed to decide their claims by\n single combat; but Canute saying that he, a man of small stature, would have\n little chance against the tall athletic Edmund, proposed, on the contrary, for\n them to divide the realm as their fathers had done.”\n    -Thomas Keightley, _The History of England_. 1839.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:2222","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:1928","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"f0d557f6b6525410bee4e685c99ba93c35ede905c27333dd5b6f480e064c8444","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:edmund","key":"edmund","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"edmund","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"我坚决反对决斗。我认为这既不明智，又很危险。而且，还有罪。如果有人向我挑战，我会友善而宽容地握住他的手，带他到一个安静隐蔽的地方，然后杀了他。\"\n    -Mark Twain, _Autobiography of Mark Twain_. 1924.","current_english":"“I thoroughly disapprove of duels. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"克律萨俄耳娶了光荣的俄刻阿诺斯之女卡利洛厄，生下了三首的格律翁。但格律翁被赫拉克勒斯以巨大的力量杀死在海环绕的厄律忒亚岛，就在他自己的蹒跚牛群旁。那一天，赫拉克勒斯驱赶着那些宽额的牛群前往神圣的梯林斯，渡过了俄刻阿诺斯的洪流，并在传说中俄刻阿诺斯彼岸那幽暗的草原上杀了奥尔索斯和牧牛人欧律提翁。\"\n    -Hesiod, _Theogony_, circa 700 BCE.","current_english":"“Khrysaor, married to Kallirhoe, daughter of glorious Okeanos, was father to\n the triple-headed Geryon, but Geryon was killed by the great strength of\n Herakles at sea-circled Erytheis beside his own shambling cattle on that day\n when Herakles drove those broad-faced cattle toward holy Tiryns, when he\n crossed the stream of Okeanos and had killed Orthos and the oxherd Eurytion\n out in the gloomy meadow beyond fabulous Okeanos.”\n    -Hesiod, _Theogony_, circa 700 BCE.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"然后我望向更远处，越过沙丘苍白的轮廓，我看见了一座黄金金字塔，那是胡夫建造的奇迹。它如金色的奇观向我致敬，如金色的造物向我致意，如金色的神迹，我将永远铭记。\"\n    -Robert Hichens, _The Spell of Egypt_. 1910.","current_english":"“And then I looked farther, beyond the pallid line of the sands, and I saw a\n Pyramid of gold, the wonder Khufu had built. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"他承担的第二件功业是杀死勒尔奈亚的许德拉。从她那单一体躯中长出百条脖颈，每条颈上各生一颗蛇头。每当一颗头被砍下，断处便生出两颗来；因此这怪物被视为不可战胜——这并非没有道理，因为被制服的那一部分反而送来了双倍的助力。面对如此棘手之物，赫拉克勒斯想出了一个巧妙的计策，命令伊俄拉俄斯用燃烧的火把灼烧被砍断的部位，以阻止鲜血流淌。\"\n    -Diodorus Siculus, _Bibliotheca Historica_. [4. 11. 5.]. c. 100.","current_english":"“The second Labour which he undertook was the slaying of the Lernaian Hydra,\n springing from whose single body were fashioned a hundred necks, each bearing\n the head of a serpent. And when one head was cut off, the place where it was\n severed put forth two others; for this reason it was considered to be\n invincible, and with good reason, since the part of it which was subdued sent\n forth a two-fold assistance in its place. Against a thing so difficult to\n manage as this Herakles devised an ingenious scheme and commanded Iolaos to\n sear with a burning brand the part which had been severed, in order to check\n the flow of the blood.”\n    -Diodorus Siculus, _Bibliotheca Historica_. [4. 11. 5.]. c. 100.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:2287","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:1978","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"f0f73f8e4b7b2b2be08dec011cac07c8c9fdef469c6fa5c06c15ded1363ca49a","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:lernaean hydra","key":"lernaean hydra","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"lernaean hydra","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"猫：但谁能说清一件东西的价值呢。\n女孩：大概是自由市场吧。\n猫：一颗_灵魂_标什么_价_？\n女孩：时薪七块两毛五。\"\n    -Dorothy Gambrell, _Cat and Girl_, 2010.","current_english":"“Cat:  But who can say what a thing is worth.\n Girl: The free market, supposedly.\n Cat:  What _price_ do we put on a _soul_?\n Girl: $7.25 an hour.”\n    -Dorothy Gambrell, _Cat and Girl_, 2010.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:2300","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:1983","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"8c9dc1f7a7d78febcee2fea37043288f04b4fff21883dbdf9857146bada2c74a","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:lodul","key":"lodul","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"lodul","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"今夜，幻象之主从你们中间经过，玛拉，梦者中的强者，为恶的强者。他遇到了另一个能以不同方式运用梦境之质的人。他与法相遇——法能将梦者逐出梦境。他们交战了，玛拉主已不复存在。他们为何交战，死神对幻术师？你说，他们的方式深不可测，因为那是神祇的方式。这不是答案。\"\n    -Roger Zelazny, \"Lord of Light\". 1967.\n\n\"若人唯求欢愉，\n放纵感官，饮食无度，\n怠惰，懦弱，玛拉必将倾覆他，\n正如狂风摧折弱树。\"\n    -The Buddha, _Dhammapada_, 1:7.\n     trans. F. Max Muller\n","current_english":"“This night the Lord of Illusion passed among you, Mara, mighty among dreamers,\n mighty for ill. He did come upon another who may work with the stuff of dreams\n in a different way. He did meet with Dharma, who may expel a dreamer from his\n dream. They did struggle, and the Lord Mara is no more. Why did they struggle,\n deathgod against illusionist? You say their ways are incomprehensible, being\n the ways of gods. This is not the answer.”\n    -Roger Zelazny, “Lord of Light”. 1967.\n\n“He who lives looking for pleasures only,\n his senses uncontrolled, immoderate in his food,\n idle, and weak, Mara will certainly overthrow him,\n as the wind throws down a weak tree.”\n    -The Buddha, _Dhammapada_, 1:7.\n     trans. F. Max Muller\n","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:2308","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:1991","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"76072ad12755c04d0e6816e8c6e347d7062d1483de42b50bfa0c687f53d6dd01","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:mara","key":"mara","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"mara","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"'抓贼！抓贼！'这喊声中有一种魔力。店主离开柜台，车夫抛下货车；屠夫扔下托盘，面包师丢下篮子；送奶工撇下奶桶，跑腿的小厮丢开包裹；学童扔掉弹珠，铺路工抛开铁镐；孩子扔下球拍。他们一路狂奔，乱作一团，横冲直撞，跌跌撞撞：拉扯着，吼叫着，尖声喊叫着，拐弯时撞倒行人，惊起群狗，吓坏家禽：于是整条街、整个广场、整片院落，都回荡着这喊声。\"\n    -Charles Dickens, _Oliver Twist_. 1838.","current_english":"“‘Stop thief! Stop thief!’ There is a magic in the sound. The tradesman leaves\n his counter, and the car-man his waggon; the butcher throws down his tray; the\n baker his basket; the milkman his pail; the errand-boy his parcels; the\n school-boy his marbles; the paviour his pickaxe; the child his battledore.\n Away they run, pell-mell, helter-skelter, slap-dash: tearing, yelling,\n screaming, knocking down the passengers as they turn the corners, rousing up\n the dogs, and astonishing the fowls: and streets, squares, and courts, re-echo\n with the sound.”\n    -Charles Dickens, _Oliver Twist_. 1838.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:2326","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:2004","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"64f03668fbac752c86a735590e917571be58a3cefb7183e11de9361ca2de1c4d","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:maurice","key":"maurice","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"maurice","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}

{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"看来，猫与所有其他种类的走兽一样，确实有某种理性与语言，使它们能够彼此理解。但说到这只格里马尔金：我宁愿视其为巫婆或女巫，而非一只猫。因为女巫常常化作那副模样，由此便产生了那句既真实又普遍的谚语：猫有九条命。也就是说，一个女巫可以九次化为猫形。\"\n    -William Baldwin, \"Beware the Cat\", 1584","current_english":"“It dooth appéere that there is in Cats as in all other kindes of beasts, a\n certaine reason and language wherby they vnderstand one another. But as\n touching this Grimmalkin: I take rather to be an Hagat or a VVitch then a Cat.\n For witches haue gone often in that likenes, And therof hath come the prouerb\n as trew as common, that a Cat hath nine liues, that is to say, a witch may\n take on her a Cats body nine times.”\n    -William Baldwin, “Beware the Cat”, 1584","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:2353","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:2024","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"04307919de15b4238b0cfaf910f9a85cdea518b25f01c9cae82f49a34f710ca5","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:natasha","key":"natasha","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"natasha","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}

{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"公主！最年轻的公主！\n为我开门！\n你难道忘了，昨日\n在清凉的喷泉边你对我说的话？\n公主！最年轻的公主！\n为我开门！\"\n    -Brothers Grimm (Margaret Hunt), _The Frog King, or Iron Henry_","current_english":"“Princess! youngest princess!\n Open the door for me!\n Dost thou not know what thou saidst to me\n\n Yesterday by the cool waters of the fountain?\n Princess, youngest princess!\n Open the door for me!”\n    -Brothers Grimm (Margaret Hunt), _The Frog King, or Iron Henry_","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:2392","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:2051","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"21bcd4dc02af028302aa715be3d267ceafa232df36416962e1ad6c44af21a5c2","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:prince ribbit","key":"prince ribbit","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"prince ribbit","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"但西格蒙德转过身来，说：'你怎么了，孩子？\n难道我们的岁月永远不能欢乐，我们的劳苦永远不能终结？'\n\n辛菲奥特利说：'我看见了，看哪，杯中乃是死亡。'\n\n于是歌声与竖琴的铮铮之音绕梁而上；\n西格蒙德因酒意与经年劳顿而恍惚；\n人群的喧哗与欢腾，在他听来犹如荒野林莽的声响；\n而布兰斯托克的繁花枝干，恍若四周摇曳的野树；\n\n于是他说：'看得好，我的养子；那就用嘴唇将它滤去吧。'\"\n    -William Morris, _The Story of Sigurd the Volsung and the Fall of the\nNiblungs_. 1891.","current_english":"“But Sigmund turned him about, and he said: ‘What aileth thee, son?\n Shall our life-days never be merry, and our labour never be done?’\n\n But Sinfiotli said: ‘I have looked, and lo, there is death in the cup.’\n\n And the song, and the tinkling of harp-strings to the roof-tree winded up;\n And Sigmund was dreamy with wine and the wearing of many a year;\n And the noise and the glee of the people as the sound of the wild woods were\n And the blossoming boughs of the Branstock were the wild trees waving about;\n\n So he said: ‘Well seen, my fosterling; let the lip then strain it out.’”\n    -William Morris, _The Story of Sigurd the Volsung and the Fall of the\nNiblungs_. 1891.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:2403","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:2061","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"40391412d1ad6779235d2d522bc2596393d9d8183f1e4f928d67b85fd4aae04d","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:sigmund","key":"sigmund","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"sigmund","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"他说，我们的母亲提亚马特对我们怀了憎恨，\n她倾尽全力的狂怒，满腔怒火。\n所有的神祇都转向了她，\n与你们所造之众一道，随她而行。\n他们结为一体，在提亚马特身侧前进；\n他们狂怒不休，日夜谋划祸端。\n他们为战斗准备，咆哮沸腾；\n他们纠集兵力，正挑起战争。\n提亚马特，创造万物的她，\n更造了无敌的兵器；她产下了巨蛇怪物，\n齿牙锋利，毒牙无情；\n她以毒液代替鲜血，充塞它们的躯体。\n凶猛的巨蛇怪物，她以恐怖包裹之，\n以辉煌装饰之，使它们体型巍然。\n谁见了它们，恐惧便攫住谁，\n它们昂起身躯，谁也无法抵御它们的攻击。\"\n    -Enuma Elish, Third Tablet. circa 668 BCE.","current_english":"“He saith that Tiamat our mother hath conceived a hatred for us,\n With all her force she rageth, full of wrath.\n All the gods have turned to her,\n With those, whom ye created, they go at her side.\n They are banded together, and at the side of Tiamat they advance;\n They are furious, they devise mischief without resting night and day.\n They prepare for battle, fuming and raging;\n They have joined their forces and are making war.\n Tiamat who formed all things,\n Made in addition weapons invincible; she spawned monster-serpents,\n Sharp of tooth, and merciless of fang;\n With poison, instead of blood, she filled their bodies.\n Fierce monster-vipers she clothed with terror,\n With splendor she decked them, she made them of lofty stature.\n Whoever beheld them, terror overcame him,\n Their bodies reared up and none could withstand their attack.”\n    -Enuma Elish, Third Tablet. circa 668 BCE.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:2419","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:2077","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"6204f50a26cf2583fd8f14075e9c0ccddc607fd87da72c9061e73d58f945fc41","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:tiamat","key":"tiamat","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"tiamat","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\" _乌鲁格_，翻滚而下，因崩裂而坠落。如山侧滚落的泥土，堆积之石之类。如瀑布之水般落下。以填土填入坑洼。在路上铺设砾石或材料。山体滑坡。\n\n_Ururugan_，显然是'Urug'的复数形式。指带一群人去从事某项工作。发动战争，以军队攻击。以人数优势围攻，仿佛蜂拥而至、成团坠落。\"\n   -Jonathan Rigg, A dictionary of the Sunda language of Java. 1862.","current_english":"“_Urug_, tumbled down, fallen by crumbling down. Slipped down, as earth from a\n hill-side, stones which have been piled up or the like. To fall as water at a\n cascade. To fill up a hollow by putting earth into it. To lay gravel or\n materials on a road. A landslip.\n\n _Ururugan_, apparently a plural form of 'Urug'. To go with a number of men to\n any work. To make war, to attack with an army. To set upon in numbers, as it\n were to tumble upon in masses.”\n   -Jonathan Rigg, A dictionary of the Sunda language of Java. 1862.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:2439","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:2097","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"94d487f8ca699c453d3270e84ea43dcae243e57e84eafbaf9d1782dba0e29509","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:urug","key":"urug","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"urug","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"男人身旁站着一个女人——勉强算是。她对人类性别的模仿只是表面的，一种礼节。同样，她穿的那身宽松垂坠的衣袍也并非布料。她不过是将自己那坚硬物质的一部分塑造成了符合那些脆弱的、终有一死的造物的偏好——在她当下与之交往的这群生灵面前。从远处看，这幻象足以让她蒙混过关，看起来就像一个静立不动的女人……\n\n当她转向那男人时——缓慢地；噬石者在地下很慢，除了例外之时——这动作将她从精巧的美推向了某种截然不同的东西。男人已经习惯了，但即便如此，他也不看她。他不想让厌恶毁掉这一刻。\"\n    -N.K. Jemisin, \"The Fifth Season\". 2015.\n","current_english":"“Beside the man stands a woman - of sorts. Her emulation of the human gender is\n only superficial, a courtesy. Likewise the loose drapelike dress she wears is\n not cloth. She has simply shaped a portion of her stiff substance to suit the\n preferences of the fragile, mortal creatures among whom she currently moves.\n From a distance the illusion would work to pass her off as a woman standing\n still...\n\n When she turns to the man - slowly; stone eaters are slow aboveground, except\n when they aren't - this movement pushes her beyond artful beauty into something\n altogether different. The man has grown used to it, but even so, he does not\n look at her. He does not want revulsion to spoil the moment.”\n    -N.K. Jemisin, “The Fifth Season”. 2015.\n","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:2451","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:2104","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"2c45eadc73ae12a5950eaa4d204a6f088a6d801e42ed55293b9c9c8e2ee0449e","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:vv","key":"vv","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":"\"男人身旁站着一个女人——勉强算是。她对人类性别的模仿只是表面的，一种礼节。同样，她穿的那身宽松垂坠的衣袍也并非布料。她不过是将自己那坚硬物质的一部分塑造成了符合那些脆弱的、终有一死的造物的偏好——在她当下与之交往的这群生灵面前。从远处看，这幻象足以让她蒙混过关，看起来就像一个静立不动的女人……\n\n当她转向那男人时——缓慢地；噬石者在地面上很慢，除了例外之时——这动作将她从精巧的美推向了某种截然不同的东西。男人已经习惯了，但即便如此，他也不看她。他不想让厌恶毁掉这一刻。\"\n    -N.K. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"我看见了那个可怜虫——那个我亲手创造的悲惨怪物。他撩起了床帘；他的眼睛，如果那也能叫作眼睛的话，死死地盯着我。他的下颌张开着，嘴里嘟囔着一些含混不清的声音，一抹笑纹爬上了他的脸颊。\"\n    -Mary Shelley, _Frankenstein_. 1818.","current_english":"“I beheld the wretch — the miserable monster whom I had\n created. He held up the curtain of the bed; and his eyes, if\n eyes they may be called, were fixed on me. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"珀琉斯在远处看见他被那可怕的伤口撕裂，便喊道：'接受这献给死者的祭品吧，至少，克朗托尔，最亲爱的少年！'他用有力的臂膀，竭尽全力，掷出那杆梣木长矛，直取德摩勒翁。长矛击穿了肋骨，矛杆在骨中颤抖不休。半人马拔出了矛杆，却不见矛头（他艰难地试图够到它），但那矛头卡在了他的肺中。痛苦反而增强了他的意志：他负伤而立，猛冲向敌人，用蹄子将那英雄踏倒在地。珀琉斯以头盔和盾牌承受着震耳的重击，护住上臂，稳住紧握的兵器，一剑刺穿那条手臂，贯穿了那双形的胸膛。\"\n    -Ovid, _Metamorphoses_, XII, 330. 8 AD.","current_english":"“When Peleus, some distance away, saw him torn apart by the frightful wound he\n shouted: ‘Accept this tribute to the dead, at least, Crantor, dearest of\n youths’, and with his powerful arm, he hurled his ash spear, at full strength,\n at Demoleon. It ruptured the ribcage, and stuck quivering in the bone. The\n centaur pulled out the shaft minus its head (he tried with difficulty to reach\n that also) but the head was caught in his lung. The pain itself strengthened\n his will: wounded, he reared up at his enemy and beat the hero down with his\n hooves. Peleus received the resounding blows on helmet and shield, and\n defending his upper arms, and controlling the weapon he held out, with one\n blow through the arm he pierced the bi-formed breast.”\n    -Ovid, _Metamorphoses_, XII, 330. 8 AD.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Monster glyphs","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:2539","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:2179","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"2ec262cce9bab047251d7187360b15b1766966dde9cae7998de6465fb5c8f1b1","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:__c_suffix","key":"__c_suffix","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"__c_suffix","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Monster glyphs","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}

{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"另一方面，据说孔子对弟子说：'吾知鸟能飞，鱼能游，兽能走。走者可以为罔，游者可以为纶，飞者可以为矰。至于龙，吾不知其乘风云而上天。今日见老子，其犹龙邪？'\"\n    -Life of Confucius（《史记·老子韩非列传》）\n\n\"此龙有两扇狂怒之翼\n   各在双肩之上；\n尾巴上长着连枷般的毒刺，\n   使他愈发大胆张狂。\n他有长长的利爪，口中\n   长着四十四颗铁牙；\n一身坚韧如牛皮般的厚皮，\n   将他周身环绕覆裹。\"\n\n    -\"An Excellent Ballad of a most dreadful Combat, fought between Moore of\nMoore-Hall, and the Dragon of Wantley\", retold by Ambrose Philips, _A\nCollection of Old Ballads. Corrected from the Best and Most Ancient Copies\nExtant. With Introductions Historical, Critical, Or Humorous_. 1723.","current_english":"“On the other hand, Confucius is made to say to his disciples, ‘I know how\n birds can fly, how fishes can swim, and how animals can run. But the runner\n may be snared, the swimmer may be hooked, and the flyer may be shot by the\n arrow. But there is the dragon. I cannot tell how he mounts on the wind\n through the clouds, and rises to heaven. Today I have seen Lao-tsze, and can\n only compare him to the dragon.’”\n    -Life of Confucius\n\n“This Dragon had Two furious Wings\n   Each one upon each Shoulder;\n With a Sting in his Tail as long as a Flail,\n   Which made him bolder and bolder.\n He had long Claws, and in his Jaws\n   Four and forty Teeth of Iron;\n With a Hide as tough, as any Buff,\n   Which did him round environ.”\n\n    -“An Excellent Ballad of a most dreadful Combat, fought between Moore of\nMoore-Hall, and the Dragon of Wantley”, retold by Ambrose Philips, _A\nCollection of Old Ballads. Corrected from the Best and Most Ancient Copies\nExtant. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"后者住在乡下，他的屋前有一棵橡树，树中有一个蛇窝。他的仆人杀死了那些蛇，但墨兰普斯捡来柴火，焚化了那些爬虫的尸体，并抚养了幼蛇。幼蛇长大后，在他睡着时分立在他两肩旁边，用舌头涤净了他的双耳。他惊恐地跳了起来，却听懂了空中飞鸟的话语，并根据从它们那里得知的消息，向人们预言将要发生的事情。\"\n    -Pseudo-Apollodorus , _Library and Epitome_, 1.9.11. circa 150 BC.\n    trans. Sir James George Frazer, 1913.\n\n\"一条蛇，带着罕见的斑驳，\n巡行于我的房间地板，\n模样如同之前的蠕虫，\n却环环萦绕着力量。\"\n    -Emily Dickinson, \"In Winter In My Room\". circa 1860.","current_english":"“The latter lived in the country, and before his house there was an oak, in\n which there was a lair of snakes. His servants killed the snakes, but Melampus\n gathered wood and burnt the reptiles, and reared the young ones. And when the\n young were full grown, they stood beside him at each of his shoulders as he\n slept, and they purged his ears with their tongues. He started up in a great\n fright, but understood the voices of the birds flying overhead, and from what\n he learned from them he foretold to men what should come to pass.”\n    -Pseudo-Apollodorus , _Library and Epitome_, 1.9.11. circa 150 BC.\n    trans. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"我是你唯一的朋友\n我不是你唯一的朋友\n但我是个微微发光的朋友\n但其实我不是你真正的朋友\n但我是\"\n    -They Might Be Giants, \"Birdhouse in Your Soul\". 1989.","current_english":"“I'm your only friend\n I'm not your only friend\n But I'm a little glowing friend\n But really I'm not actually your friend\n But I am”\n    -They Might Be Giants, “Birdhouse in Your Soul”. 1989.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Non-unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:2700","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:2318","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"dfe414e73fc3470b24b05579b7a72c56307bce44858f23e776e1c22da316ad06","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:battlesphere","key":"battlesphere","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"battlesphere","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Non-unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"贝斯·肯瓦尔（Bès Kěmwar），'蛆灵'，是马来西亚贾胡特人所知的众多_bès_或疾病精灵之一。作为一种多价精灵，它被描绘为蛆虫或毛虫，其变体以毛发、刺、翅膀及其他细节加以区分。它以稻米、蔬菜和其他作物为食。可导致骨骼、关节和肌肉的疼痛。若其毛虫的毛发落入水中，饮者将引发咳嗽和咽喉出血。蛆灵也居住在腐烂的树干中并以此为食。任何靠近这棵倒树的人都会被精灵咬伤腿部，引起红肿、瘙痒及肿胀。所有脚指甲都会脱落，但肿胀将在两三个月内消退。\"\n    - _A Book of Creatures_, 2020.","current_english":"“The Bès Kěmwar, “maggot spirit”, is one of the many _bès_ or disease spirits\n known to the Jah Hut of Malaysia. A polyvalent spirit, it is depicted as a\n maggot or caterpillar, with variants distinguished by hair, thorns, wings, and\n other details. It eats rice, vegetables, and other crops. It is responsible for\n aches in bones, joints, and muscles. If its caterpillar hair falls into water\n and that water is drunk, it causes coughing and bleeding in the throat. Maggot\n spirits are also known to live in rotten tree trunks and feed in them. Anyone\n who approaches the fallen tree will be bitten in the leg by the spirit, causing\n redness, swelling, and itching. All the toenails fall off but the swelling will\n be gone by two or three months.”\n    - _A Book of Creatures_, 2020.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Non-unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:2709","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:2327","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"0c51efac5cfb2bbe072cf34803e3e196e5a72c26166a6c4aa6b4b31b67e9464b","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:bes kemwar","key":"bes kemwar","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"bes kemwar","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Non-unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"他看每一丛灌木都像博加特（妖怪）。\"\n    -John Ray, _A Compleat Collection of English Proverbs_. 1768.\n\n\"一个博加特侵入了某位安静守法、勤劳耕作之农夫的家；无人知晓它凭什么借口或何种权威。一旦占据那屋子，它便以一种极不友善、专横霸道的方式，与合法的凡人居住者争夺居住权。它尤其对孩童怀有极大敌意。对于父母而言，没有什么比子女遭受虐待更令人痛彻心扉的了，因此那位父亲，尤其是他的好妻子，日日夜夜被这恶毒而隐形的博加特之恶意所折磨。\"\n    -C.J.T., _Folk-lore and Legends: English_  1890.","current_english":"“He thinks every bush a boggart.”\n    -John Ray, _A Compleat Collection of English Proverbs_. 1768.\n\n“A BOGGART intruded himself, upon what pretext or by what authority is unknown,\n into the house of a quiet, inoffensive, and laborious farmer; and, when once\n it had taken possession it disputed the right of domicile with the legal\n mortal tenant, in a very unneighbourly and arbitrary manner. In particular, it\n seemed to have a great aversion to children. As there is no point on which a\n parent feels more acutely than that of the maltreatment of his offspring, the\n feelings of the father and more particularly of his good dame, were daily, ay,\n and nightly, harrowed up by the malice of this malignant and invisible\n boggart.”\n    -C.J.T., _Folk-lore and Legends: English_  1890.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Non-unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:2725","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:2334","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"dfbc30e2739a7e0edea100f3c40c8ab3366574b03642bfa1a5b8ae951e403bd1","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:boggart","key":"boggart","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"boggart","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Non-unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}

{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"幸福是一只蝴蝶，你若追逐它，它永远在你够不到的地方；但你若静静坐下，它也许就会落在你身上。\"\n    -Nathaniel Hawthorne","current_english":"“Happiness is a butterfly, which when pursued, is always just beyond your\n grasp, but which, if you will sit down quietly, may alight upon you.”\n    -Nathaniel Hawthorne","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Non-unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:2765","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:2364","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"a8435d9b3685bda7381120bcc5192e7058ecd8b56c208624ef572a48157b4721","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:butterfly","key":"butterfly","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"butterfly","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Non-unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"我们就叫他卡科德蒙，带着他那黑猫吉布，他的苏库芭，他的魔鬼之种，他的弗莱格松后裔——我敢说，那可是用科塞特斯河的泡沫喂养出来的。\"\n    -John Fletcher, _The Knight of Malta_. 1647.","current_english":"“We'll call him Cacodemon, with his black Gib there, his Succuba, his Devil's\n Seed, his Spawn of Phlegethon, that o’ my Consience was bred o’ the Spume of\n Cocytus.”\n    -John Fletcher, _The Knight of Malta_. 1647.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；专名例外复用 Issue #3、QUOTE_NAME_EXCEPTIONS 与 docs/decisions.md，未重新裁定。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Non-unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:2771","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:2369","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"8324f20eae1d979bbe2d2c098c5da33437576b3c9253255dd6c73dec9687158e","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:cacodemon","key":"cacodemon","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":"The word is a character's proper name in the cited play.","producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"cacodemon","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Non-unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"于是他渡海登岛，携着阿纳克西乌斯的两位兄弟；在那里，他找到了那位被弃的骑士，身穿他自己的制服，色如悲伤本身在至暗的镜中照见自身时那般漆黑：他的饰物亦同此色，却铸成乌鸦的形状，仿佛在张口等待腐肉；唯有他的缰绳是蛇，它们彼此精巧地盘绕，蛇头汇聚于马衔的两颊与饰钉之处，仿佛要咬那马，而那马在嚼咬衔铁时，又仿佛要回咬它们；白色的泡沫便是这场毒烈的搏斗所生。他的纹章乃是一只卡托布莱帕，此兽在月亮（它与月亮有种天然的共感）失去光芒时，便长久地僵卧如死。铭词意为：月亮并非缺少光芒，而是那可怜的野兽缺少了月光。\"\n    -Sir Philip Sidney","current_english":"“So passed he over into the island, taking with him the two brothers of\n Anaxius; where he found the forsaken knight attired in his own livery, as\n black as sorrow itself could see itself in the blackest glass: his ornaments\n of the same hue, but formd into the figures of ravens which seemed to gape for\n carrion: only his reins were snakes, which finely wrapping themselves one\n within the other, their heads came together to the cheeks and bosses of the\n bit, where they might seem to bite at the horse, and the horse, as he champed\n the bit, to bite at them, and that the white foam was engendered by the\n poisonous fury of the combat. His impresa was a Catoblepta, which so long lies\n dead as the moon (whereto it hath so natural a sympathy) wants her light. The\n word signified, that the moon wanted not the light, but the poor beast wanted\n the moon's light.”\n    -Sir Philip Sidney","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Non-unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:2778","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:2374","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"11f7d474485aed7a13f7335cdfab63cd41bd54575214847209cf004ed72320d7","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:catoblepas","key":"catoblepas","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"catoblepas","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Non-unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"耶和华的荣耀从基路伯那里上升，停在门坎以上；殿内满了云彩，院宇也被耶和华荣耀的光辉充满。\n\n基路伯翅膀的响声听到外院，好像全能神说话的声音。\"\n    -WEB Bible, Ezekiel 10:4-5（和合本）","current_english":"“The glory of Yahweh mounted up from the cherub, and stood over the threshold\n of the house; and the house was filled with the cloud, and the court was full\n of the brightness of Yahweh's glory.\n\n The sound of the wings of the cherubim was heard even to the outer court, as\n the voice of God Almighty when he speaks.”\n    -WEB Bible, Ezekiel 10:4-5","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；专名例外复用 Issue #3、QUOTE_NAME_EXCEPTIONS 与 docs/decisions.md，未重新裁定。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Non-unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:2794","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:2379","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"0ebac1d31e4dff1e4c1f26679c67221a11c4271ddcc705144ef71b2c93fe001d","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:cherub","key":"cherub","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":"The Biblical quotation uses the established religious name 基路伯.","producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"cherub","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Non-unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"其最基本的形式是一种夜鸟，一种猫头鹰（_Strix rufipes_），在夜间以无声的翅膀飞行，宣告疾病、死亡或其他不幸之事。当听到它的嘶哑叫声时，人们应当向空中撒灰并念诵几句祈祷，以期驱散它的邪恶。\n\n据说琼琼也是一种_calcu_，即伪装的邪恶巫师。它形似一颗人头，长着一对巨大的、翅膀般的耳朵，使其能够飞行。夜间，这些巫师的头颅从身体上分离，四处飞行作恶，对大多数人而言不可见，而它们那不祥的_tué, tué, tué_叫声宣告着不幸。它们拥有与巫师相同的能力，且据说会吸食沉睡者的鲜血。\"\n    - _A Book of Creatures_, 2015.","current_english":"“In its simplest form it is a bird of the night, an owl (_Strix rufipes_) that\n flies on silent wings during the night to announce illness, death, or some\n other unwelcome event. When its croaking is heard it is advised to throw ash\n into the air and utter a few prayers in the hopes of turning away its evil.\n\n The chonchón is also said to be a _calcu_ or evil sorcerer in disguise. It\n resemble a human head, with oversized wing-like ears that allow it to fly.\n During the night, these sorcerers’ heads detach themselves from their bodies\n and fly around to cause mischief, invisible to most, their ominous _tué, tué,\n tué_ call announcing misfortune. They have the same powers as sorcerers do,\n and have been known to suck the blood of sleepers.”\n    - _A Book of Creatures_, 2015.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Non-unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:2804","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:2386","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"c526b90929ddcb04ebf99ed04c46140d9358366b2560212e72cf11b7ce6bd19a","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:chonchon","key":"chonchon","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"chonchon","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Non-unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"魔鬼有时也偷人类的孩子；他常常在婴儿出生后头六周内将其掳走，并用小鬼取而代之。\"\n    -Martin Luther","current_english":"“The Devil, too, sometimes steals human children; it is not infrequent for him\n to carry away infants within the first six weeks after birth, and to\n substitute in their place imps.”\n    -Martin Luther","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Non-unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:2821","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:2395","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"bfe1de4410f9f04008ae669a6be01ed2957278b7375ef34c982c7a80d0275b23","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:crimson imp","key":"crimson imp","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"crimson imp","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Non-unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"小小鳄鱼怎么样\n把他闪亮的尾巴来改良，\n把尼罗河水来浇漾，\n浇在片片金鳞上！\n\n看他笑得多么欢畅，\n爪子张得多齐整，\n欢迎小鱼游进来，\n那微笑的嘴巴多温存！\"\n    -Lewis Carroll（赵元任译）, _Alice's Adventures in Wonderland_, 1865.","current_english":"“How doth the little crocodile\n  Improve his shining tail,\n And pour the waters of the Nile\n  On every golden scale!\n\n How cheerfully he seems to grin,\n  How neatly spreads his claws,\n And welcomes little fishes in\n  With gently smiling jaws!”\n    -Lewis Carroll, _Alice's Adventures in Wonderland_, 1865.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Non-unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:2828","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:2400","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"10ebf0a5afe9e9b29d3f70a84a23c082de5b19f227d8d53780fb4cd895a9a980","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:crocodile","key":"crocodile","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"crocodile","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Non-unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"好朋友，看在耶稣的份上，\n莫要挖掘此处封存的尘土。\n容我安息者，必蒙赐福；\n动我骸骨者，必受咒诅。\"\n    -Epitaph on William Shakespeare's grave","current_english":"“Good friend for Jesus’ sake forbear,\n To dig the dust enclosed here.\n Blessed be the man that spares these stones,\n And cursed be he that moves my bones.”\n    -Epitaph on William Shakespeare's grave","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Non-unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:2841","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:2413","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"d55fd86c3800337b005afdcc383c33282b6832d2427eada1901f426e7cddeaa3","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:curse skull","key":"curse skull","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"curse skull","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Non-unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}

{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"作为形式固有含义的一个例证，让我们考察一种特定的垂直石碑形式，它被不同地称为石碑、方尖碑、立石和纪念柱。历史上，这些碑柱一直被普遍用于纪念被尊崇的现象。因此，当一群人希望铭记某个重要的关系、事件或人物时，他们会为此选定一个地点，并通常以经久耐用、昂然向上的垂直形式或一组这样的形式来标记它。在自然语言中，一块垂直的石头意味着：我们（在地上）与一个理想（在天上）之间昂然向上的连接器；我们为这被尊崇的现象而自豪地'站立起来'。这块标记是对其所占据空间的象征性栖居。其规模和工艺是将大量劳动和资源奉献给一段记忆的牺牲行为。它（因为我们把它留给了后人，且它是由耐久材料制成的）是一个强有力的提示，希望未来的人们也能向这被铭记的现象致以敬意。当我们使用这种特定的垂直标记的物理形式时，其作为荣耀象征的历史用途，以及其在自然语言中的含义，很可能发送出一条信息：这是一个荣耀之地，一个关于'善'的地方——无论是在我们的文化中，还是在未来观察者的文化中。\"\n    -\"Expert Judgment on Markers to Deter Inadvertent Human Intrusion into the\n      Waste Isolation Pilot Plant\", Sandia National Laboratories report. 1993","current_english":"“As one example of the meanings inherent in a form, let us examine a particular\n form of vertical stone marker, variously called stele, obelisk, standing-stone,\n and memorial column. These have been historically and commonly used to\n commemorate honored phenomena. So, when a people wish to remember an important\n relationship, event or personage, a location is dedicated to it and often\n marked with an enduring and aspiring vertical form or sets of them. In natural\n language, a vertical stone means: an aspiring connector between us (on earth)\n and an ideal (up there); that we ‘stand up’ with pride about this honored\n phenomenon. The marker is a symbolic inhabitation of the place it occupies. Its\n size and workmanship is a sacrifice of much work and resources to a memory. It\n is a strong suggestion (because we left it to them and it is of durable\n material) that future people also give honor to the memorialized phenomenon.\n When we use this particular physical form of the vertical marker, both its\n historic use as an honorific and its meanings in natural language may well send\n a message that this is an honored place, a place about a ‘good’ both in our\n culture and in the culture of future observers.”\n    -“Expert Judgment on Markers to Deter Inadvertent Human Intrusion into the\n      Waste Isolation Pilot Plant”, Sandia National Laboratories report. 1993","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Non-unique {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"站在雨果身旁，咬住他喉咙的，是一个肮脏的东西，一头巨大的黑色野兽，形如猎犬，却比凡人肉眼见过的任何猎犬都要庞大。就在他们眼睁睁看着的时候，那东西将雨果·巴斯克维尔的喉咙撕开了……\"\n    -Arthur Conan Doyle, _The Hound of the Baskervilles_. 1902.","current_english":"“Standing over Hugo, and plucking at his throat, there stood a foul thing, a\n great, black beast, shaped like a hound, yet larger than any hound that ever\n mortal eye has rested upon. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"当戴胜鸟回到所罗门那里（向他报告了消息），他回应道（对示巴人）：'你们拿钱财来给我吗？真主赐予我的，远胜过他赐予你们的。你们才是应为这样的恩赐而欢喜的人。'（他对戴胜鸟说，）'回到他们那里去，（让他们知道）我们将以他们无法想象的兵力来到他们那里。我们将驱逐他们，使他们受辱蒙羞。'他说：'诸位长老啊，你们谁能在她来归顺之前，把她的宝座搬到我跟前来？'精灵中的一个伊弗利特说：'在你从座位上起身之前，我就能把它搬来。我有足够的力量做到此事。'\"\n    -The Quran, Sura 27 Al-Naml","current_english":"“When the hoopoe returned to Solomon (he told him the news), and he responded\n (to Sheba's people): “Are you giving me money? What GOD has given me is far\n better than what He has given you. You are the ones to rejoice in such gifts.”\n (To the hoopoe, he said), “Go back to them (and let them know that) we will\n come to them with forces they cannot imagine. We will evict them, humiliated\n and debased.” He said, “O you elders, which of you can bring me her mansion,\n before they arrive here as submitters?” One afrit from the jinns said, “I can\n bring it to you before you stand up. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"睡意刚将他攫住，消解了所有悲愁——\n清爽的睡意如迷雾般围绕他倾泻而下——\n他那强健的躯体因直冲赫克托耳、\n奋力杀到狂风呼啸的特洛伊城墙边而疲惫不堪——\n就在此刻，殒命的帕特罗克洛斯的魂影飘然而至……\n他形貌如生，每一处特征分毫不差，\n同样的高大身躯，同样美好的眼睛和嗓音，\n身披的衣袍正是生前所穿。\n魂影悬浮在他头顶，开口说道：\n'你在沉睡吗，阿喀琉斯？你已将我遗忘，我的朋友。\n生前你从不怠慢我，如今只在死后。\n快将我埋葬——让我通过哈迪斯之门。\n那些灵魂全将我远远挡开，\n那些燃尽的、没有呼吸的死者的幽冥，\n永不让我渡过冥河，与它们共处……\n它们任我徘徊游荡，被遗弃、迷失\n在有深深重门的死亡之殿。'\"\n    -Homer, _The Iliad_, XXIII, 72-88.\n     trans. Robert Fagles, 1990.","current_english":"“No sooner had sleep caught him, dissolving all his grief\n as mists of refreshing slumber poured around him there—\n his powerful frame was bone-weary from charging Hector\n straight and hard to the walls of windswept Troy—\n than the ghost of stricken Patroclus drifted up...\n He was like the man to the life, every feature,\n the same tall build and the fine eyes and voice\n and the very robes that used to clothe his body.\n Hovering at his head the phantom rose and spoke:\n ‘Sleeping, Achilles? You’ve forgotten me, my friend.\n You never neglected me in life, only now in death.\n Bury me, quickly—let me pass the Gates of Hades.\n They hold me off at a distance, all the souls,\n the shades of the burnt-out, breathless dead,\n never to let me cross the river, mingle with them...\n They leave me to wander up and down, abandoned, lost\n at the House of Death with the all-embracing gates.’”\n    -Homer, _The Iliad_, XXIII, 72-88.\n     trans. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"于是国王来到盲人们所在之处，走近他们说道：\n'你们现在知道大象是什么样了吗？'\n'当然，尊主：我们现在知道大象是什么样了。'\n'那么告诉我，盲人们啊，大象是什么样？'\n那些摸过大象头的盲人说：'尊主啊，大象像一个巨大的圆罐。'\n那些摸过耳朵的说：'它像一个簸谷的竹篮。'\n那些摸过象牙的说：'它像一个犁铧。'\n那些摸过象鼻的说：'它像一把犁。'\n那些摸过身躯的说：'它像一座谷仓。'\n那些摸过脚的说：'它像一根柱子。'\n那些摸过背的说：'它像一个石臼。'\n那些摸过尾巴的说：'它像一根杵。'\n那些摸过尾梢毛的说：'它像一把扫帚。'\n于是他们纷纷举起拳头互相争吵，声称：'大象就是这样，大象不是这样，大象不像那样，它就像这样。'\"\n国王，比丘们啊，感到十分欣慰。\n    -_Udāna_, VI \"Jaccandhavagga\". ca. 5th cent. B.C.\n     trans. Dawsonne Melanchthon Strong, 1902.","current_english":"“And the King went to where the blind men were, and drawing near said to them:\n ‘Do you now know what an elephant is like?’\n ‘Assuredly, Lord: we now know what an elephant is like.’\n ‘Tell me then, O blind men, what an elephant is like.’\n And those blind men, O Bhikkhus, who had felt the head of the elephant, said:\n ‘An elephant, Sir, is like a large round jar.\n Those who had felt its ears, said: 'it is like a winnowing basket.’\n Those who had felt its tusks, said: ‘it is like a plough-share.’\n Those who had felt its trunk, said: ‘it is like a plough.’\n Those who had felt its body, said: ‘it is like a granary.’\n Those who had felt its feet, said: ‘it is like a pillar.’\n Those who had felt its back, said: ‘it is like a mortar.’\n Those who had felt its tail, said: ‘it is a like a pestle.’\n Those who had felt the tuft of its tail, said: ‘it is like a broom.’\n And they all fought amongst themselves with their fists, declaring, ‘such is\n an elephant, such is not elephant, an elephant is not like that, it is like\n this.’”\n And the King, O Bhikkhus, was highly delighted.\n    -_Udāna_, VI “Jaccandhavagga”. ca. 5th cent. B.C.\n     trans. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"看哪！经线已绷紧，\n为战士的陨落；\n看，织机中的纬线，\n已被鲜血浸湿；\n如今，战斗的预感，\n在友人迅捷的手指下，\n我们的灰色织物\n因战争的警讯而增长，\n我们的经线血红，\n我们的纬线尸蓝。\n\n这幅织物是以\n人类的内脏织就；\n这经线沉重地压着\n被杀戮者的头颅；\n沾满鲜血的长矛\n充当我们的纺锤，\n我们的织机镶着铁，\n箭矢是我们的纱轴；\n以刀剑为梭子，\n我们编织这战争之纬；\n如此我们纺织，怪诞的姐妹，\n我们得胜的织品。\n\n如今战争之胜者走来\n轮到她来编织，\n如今挥剑者踏入，\n如今疾击者，如今风暴；\n当她们加速梭子时\n矛头将如何闪耀！\n盾牌碎裂，盔甲吞噬者\n在铠甲上狠狠撕咬！\"\n    -_The Story of Burnt Njal_, trans. George Dasent. 1861","current_english":"“SEE! warp is stretched\n For warrior’s fall;\n Lo, weft in loom,\n ’Tis wet with blood;\n Now, fight foreboding,\n ’Neath friends’ swift fingers\n Our gray woof waxeth\n With war’s alarms,\n Our warp blood-red,\n Our weft corse-blue.\n\n This woof is y-woven\n With entrails of men;\n This warp is hard weighted\n With heads of the slain;\n Spears blood-besprinkled\n For spindles we use,\n Our loom iron-bound,\n And arrows our reels;\n With swords for our shuttles\n This war-woof we work;\n So weave we, weird sisters,\n Our war-winning woof.\n\n Now war-winner walketh\n To weave in her turn,\n Now Sword-swinger steppeth,\n Now Swift-stroke, now Storm;\n When they speed the shuttle\n How spear-heads shall flash!\n Shields crash, and helm-gnawer\n On harness bite hard!”\n    -_The Story of Burnt Njal_, trans. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"但他刚在藏身之处躲了不久，那可怕的厄丁便走了进来；他才进门，就听到他喊道：\n'四处嗅啊四处探，\n我闻到了凡人气味，\n管他是活还是死，\n今夜他心脏要给我的面包当馅料。'\"\n    -Joseph Jacobs, _The Red Ettin_","current_english":"“But he had not been long in his hiding-hole, before the awful Ettin came in;\n and no sooner was he in, than he was heard crying:\n ‘Snouk but and snouk ben,\n  I find the smell of an earthly man,\n  Be he living, or be he dead,\n  His heart this night shall kitchen my bread.’”\n    -Joseph Jacobs, _The Red Ettin_","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；专名例外复用 Issue #3、QUOTE_NAME_EXCEPTIONS 与 docs/decisions.md，未重新裁定。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Non-unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:3024","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:2557","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"5984503eaf25dd957d2486d7e6f41f115b80558e4baffddb1e6773b59b6df590","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:ettin","key":"ettin","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":"Red Ettin is the proper name of a figure in the cited ballad.","producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"ettin","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Non-unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}

{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"这颗行星产生了闪烁的、宝石般的、爬来爬去的螃蟹，沃贡人把它们吃掉，用铁锤砸开它们的壳；高大挺拔的树木，有着令人叹为观止的纤细和色彩，沃贡人把它们砍倒，用来烧螃蟹肉；优雅的、瞪羚般的动物，长着丝缎般的皮毛和水汪汪的眼睛，沃贡人把它们捉来当椅子坐。\"\n    -Douglas Adams, _The Hitch-hiker's Guide to the Galaxy_. 1979.","current_english":"“The planet brought forth scintillating jewelled scuttling crabs, which the\n Vogons ate, smashing their shells with iron mallets; {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"在沙漠中\n我见到了一个生灵，赤裸，兽性，\n蹲坐在地上，\n双手捧着自己的心脏，\n一口一口地吃着。\n我说：'好吃吗，朋友？'\n'很苦——苦极了'，他答道，\n'但我喜欢它\n正因为它苦，\n也因为它是我的心。'\"\n    -Stephen Crane, _The Black Riders and Other Lines_. 1895.","current_english":"“In the desert\n I saw a creature, naked, bestial,\n Who, squatting upon the ground,\n Held his heart in his hands,\n And ate of it.\n I said, ‘Is it good, friend?’\n ‘It is bitter — bitter’, he answered,\n ‘But I like it\n Because it is bitter,\n And because it is my heart.’”\n    -Stephen Crane, _The Black Riders and Other Lines_. 1895.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Non-unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:3088","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:2612","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"ae6be57f687d8ee03e20f698790bccf40aff9806799af65f93542ddf32fea25a","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:ghoul","key":"ghoul","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"ghoul","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Non-unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"你所见的眼睛\n并非因你看见它才是眼睛，\n它之所以是眼睛，是因为它在看着你。\"\n    -Antonio Machado, Campos de Castilla. 1912-1917.","current_english":"“El ojo que ves\n no es ojo porque tú lo veas,\n es ojo porque te ve.”\n    -Antonio Machado, Campos de Castilla. 1912-1917.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Non-unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:3102","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:2626","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"01b7b06112edfd0478ead7bb55af39d327e97b9cc924f0cad0d75c03bf57cfe5","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:glass eye","key":"glass eye","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"glass eye","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Non-unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"于是他悄悄降下，向努斯示意。但那些格诺尔早已透过他们在树干上凿出的狡诈孔洞监视着他，于是那诡异的寂静，仿佛带着几分优雅地，被通克被他们从身后擒住时发出的急促尖叫所打破——尖叫声越来越快，越来越快，直到语无伦次。至于他们把他带到了哪里，这不是一个好问的问题，而他们对他做了什么，我也不会说。\"\n    -Lord Dunsany, \"How Nuth Would Have Practised His Art Upon the Gnoles\".\n1912.","current_english":"“Then he descended softly and beckoned to Nuth. But the gnoles had watched him\n through knavish holes that they bore in trunks of the trees, and the unearthly\n silence gave way, as it were with a grace, to the rapid screams of Tonker as\n they picked him up from behind — screams that came faster and faster until\n they were incoherent. And where they took him it is not good to ask, and what\n they did with him I shall not say.”\n    -Lord Dunsany, “How Nuth Would Have Practised His Art Upon the Gnoles”.\n1912.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Non-unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:3109","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:2633","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"3e2f71463791658f86594aba9722c7c61b0c947e9ba53563152cd11ee817190c","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:gnoll","key":"gnoll","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"gnoll","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Non-unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
