{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"你不会戏剧性地降临，带着巨龙\n它们后腿直立，夹在我生命之间\n把我屠戮后摔在货车旁边\"\n    -Philip Larkin, \"To Failure\". 1949.","current_english":"“You do not come dramatically, with dragons\n That rear up with my life between their paws\n And dash me butchered down beside the wagons”\n    -Philip Larkin, “To Failure”. 1949.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Non-unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:3130","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:2649","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"9f446f59582ded24b1eac4dcae37859510d49045bb3abdc3c97cad2a11c58aca","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:golden dragon","key":"golden dragon","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":"\"你不会戏剧性地降临，带着巨龙\n它们昂然立起，双爪挟持着我的性命，\n把遭到屠戮的我摔在货车旁边\"\n    -Philip Larkin, \"To Failure\". 1949.","reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["保留现译；会继续留下已由英文正文或当前术语直接证明的偏差。"],"resolved_key":"golden dragon","reviewer_rationale":"rear up with my life between their paws 描写龙昂然立起、双爪挟持说话者性命；原译“夹在我生命之间”颠倒关系。","section":"Non-unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"adjust"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"没有贪恋，没有嫉妒燃烧\n在你明亮的金色眼睛里，\n那平静而无辜的目光，\n投向天空下的一切万物。\"\n    -Hannah Flagg Gould, _The Youth's Coronal_","current_english":"“No coveting nor envy burns\n   In thy bright golden eye,\n That calm and innocently turns\n   On all below the sky.”\n    -Hannah Flagg Gould, _The Youth's Coronal_","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；专名例外复用 Issue #3、QUOTE_NAME_EXCEPTIONS 与 docs/decisions.md，未重新裁定。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Non-unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:3137","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:2656","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"a9d2cc5d3d5eee38a169faf727c8ff6d4b72579ae1499d1c115a1a63ba197ca7","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:golden eye","key":"golden eye","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":"The words are an ordinary phrase describing a golden eye.","producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"golden eye","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Non-unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"'听着，雷布_戈仑_，'老人说，摇晃着手指。'注意听我说——你明白吗？'\n'明白……'\n'你要是想留下来，就得照古姆拜纳先生说的做。'\n'照-古姆拜纳-先生-说的-做……'\n'这才是我要听的戈仑说话的样子。马尔卡，把钱包里的小镜子给我。看，你看见你的脸了吗？你看见额头上写的是什么了吗？你要是不照古姆拜纳先生说的做，他就会擦掉写的东西，你就活不成了。'\n'活-不成……'\n'这就对了。现在，听着。门廊下面你会找到一台割草机。把它拿来。去割草坪。然后回来。去吧。'\"\n    -Avram Davidson, \"The Golem\", 1955.","current_english":"“‘Listen Reb _Golem_,’ the old man said, wagging his finger. ‘Pay attention to\n  what I say—you understand?’\n ‘Understand...’\n ‘If you want to stay here, you got to do like Mr. Gumbeiner says.’\n ‘Do-like-Mr.-Gumbeiner-says...’\n ‘_That's_ the way I like to hear a golem talk. Malka, give here the mirror\n  from the pocketbook. Look, you see your face? You see on the forehead, what's\n  written? If you don't do like Mr. Gumbeiner says, he'll wipe out what's\n  written and you'll be no more alive.’\n ‘No-more-alive...’\n ‘_That's_ right. Now, listen. Under the porch you'll find a lawnmower. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"她们是鸟身、少女面孔的东西；她们的排泄物令人作呕，她们的双手是利爪，她们的面孔因永不满足的饥饿而枯槁憔悴。\"\n    -Virgil, Aeneid 3\n\n\"菲纽斯还未来得及拿起第一口食物，她们便如旋风或闪电一般，毫无预兆地从云中坠落，发出沙哑的叫声宣告着对食物的渴望。年轻的贵族们看见她们来了，发出了警报。然而他们还未来得及行动，哈尔庇厄们便已吞尽了整餐食物，再度展开翅膀，远远地飞到了海上。她们留下的一切，只是一股无法忍受的恶臭。\"\n    -Apollonius Rhodius, Argonautica 2. 179 — 434","current_english":"“Bird-bodied, girl-faced things they are; abominable their droppings, their\n hands are talons, their faces haggard with hunger insatiable.”\n    -Virgil, Aeneid 3\n\n“And Phineus had scarcely taken the first morsel up when, with as little\n warning as a whirlwind or a lightning flash, they dropped from the clouds\n proclaiming their desire for food with raucous cries. The young lords saw them\n coming and raised the alarm. Yet they had hardly done so before the Harpyiai\n had devoured the whole meal and were on the wing once more, far out at sea.\n All they left was an intolerable stench.”\n    -Apollonius Rhodius, Argonautica 2. 179 — 434","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；专名例外复用 Issue #3、QUOTE_NAME_EXCEPTIONS 与 docs/decisions.md，未重新裁定。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Non-unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:3172","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:2686","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"f7ad6ceb302c4a76a6ab752d5caa534117fcf8062e186e5d7881a8dc3f794e1a","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:harpy","key":"harpy","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":"Harpyiai is a classical proper name in the cited work.","producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"harpy","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Non-unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"在她腰间\n一群地狱犬的嚎叫从不停止地吠着，\n张着宽阔的刻耳柏洛斯般的巨口，震响着\n一阵可怖的狂鸣；然而，它们高兴时，若有什么扰了\n它们的喧嚣，便会爬进她的子宫，\n蜷伏在那里；但在那里，看不见的地方，\n却仍然吠着，嚎叫着。\"\n    -John Milton, _Paradise Lost_, Book II, 1667.","current_english":"“About her middle round\n A cry of Hell-hounds never-ceasing barked\n With wide Cerberean mouths full loud, and rung\n A hideous peal; yet, when they list, would creep,\n If aught disturbed their noise, into her womb,\n And kennel there; yet there still barked and howled\n Within unseen.”\n    -John Milton, _Paradise Lost_, Book II, 1667.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Non-unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:3186","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:2694","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"c080f0ee2c4913e8dcf2155d78186fceca354c755061ad3567066f4e28245b71","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:hell hound","key":"hell hound","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"hell hound","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Non-unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}

{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"从前，当梵授王在波罗奈为王时，菩萨转生为一只鬣蜥。长大后，他住在大河岸边的一个大洞穴里，有成百上千只鬣蜥追随他。菩萨有一个儿子，一只年轻的鬣蜥，他与一只变色龙交情甚好，常常拥抱亲近。有人将这种亲密关系禀告了鬣蜥王，他便召来自己的小儿子，说这样的友谊是不适当的，因为变色龙是低等生物，如果继续这种亲密关系，灾祸将降临在整个鬣蜥族群身上。他命令儿子不要再与变色龙来往。但儿子仍继续这种亲密。\"\n    -_Khuddaka Nikāya_, Jātaka 141 \"Godha-jātaka\". ca. 4th cent. B.C.\n     trans. Robert Chalmers, 1895.","current_english":"“Once on a time when Brahmadatta was reigning in Benares, the Bodhisatta was\n born an iguana. When he grew up he dwelt in a big burrow in the river bank\n with a following of many hundreds of other iguanas. Now the Bodhisatta had a\n son, a young iguana, who was great friends with a chameleon, whom he used to\n clip and embrace. This intimacy being reported to the iguana king, he sent for\n his young son and said that such friendship was misplaced, for chameleons were\n low creatures, and that if the intimacy was persisted in, calamity would\n befall the whole of the tribe of iguanas. And he enjoined his son to have no\n more to do with the chameleon. But the son continued in his intimacy.”\n    -_Khuddaka Nikāya_, Jātaka 141 “Godha-jātaka”. ca. 4th cent. B.C.\n     trans. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"我们三个面色惨白地坐着，仿佛刚刚目睹了一场肮脏而恶毒的谋杀。至少如果我们看的是谋杀，那个凶手也不会像眼下这样面无表情地直视着我们的眼睛。或许是那种冷酷而不为所动的傲慢感让我们如此不安。但无论我们试图将什么恶毒的情感强加到那只蜥蜴身上，我们都知道那根本不是蜥蜴的情感，而只是我们自己的。蜥蜴只不过在以简单直接的蜥蜴方式做它蜥蜴该做的事。它对那种恐惧、那种愧疚、那种羞耻、那种丑陋——我们这些唯一懂得愧疚和羞耻的动物试图强加给它的东西——一无所知。于是这一切都反弹回到我们身上，仿佛反射在它那只毫不闪烁、毫无兴趣的眼睛的镜面中。\"\n    -Douglas Adams, \"Last Chance to See\". 1990.","current_english":"“The three of us were sitting ashen faced as if we had just witnessed a foul\n and malignant murder. At least if we had been watching a murder the murderer\n wouldn't have been looking us impassively in the eye as he did it. Maybe it\n was the feeling of cold unflinching arrogance that so disturbed us. But\n whatever malign emotions we tried to pin on to the lizard, we knew that they\n weren't the lizard's emotions at all, only ours. The lizard was simply going\n about its lizardly business in a simple, straightforward lizardly way. It\n didn't know anything about the horror, the guilt, the shame, the ugliness that\n we, uniquely guilty and ashamed animals, were trying to foist on it. So we got\n it all straight back at us, as if reflected in the mirror of its single\n unwavering and disinterested eye.”\n    -Douglas Adams, “Last Chance to See”. 1990.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Non-unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:3280","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:2768","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"4d50fcaf1a4db2aa65911eb5e3288e991319f8ffe387be25d9c9a77ed8b5dd6e","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:komodo dragon","key":"komodo dragon","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":"\"我们三个面色惨白地坐着，仿佛刚刚目睹了一场肮脏而恶毒的谋杀。至少如果我们看的是谋杀，那个凶手也不会像眼下这样面无表情地直视着我们的眼睛。或许是那种冷酷而不为所动的傲慢感让我们如此不安。但无论我们试图将什么恶毒的情感强加到那只蜥蜴身上，我们都知道那根本不是蜥蜴的情感，而只是我们自己的。蜥蜴只不过在以简单直接的蜥蜴方式做它蜥蜴该做的事。它对那种恐惧、那种愧疚、那种羞耻、那种丑陋——我们这些唯一懂得愧疚和羞耻的动物试图强加给它的东西——一无所知。于是这一切都反弹回到我们身上，仿佛反射在它那只目光坚定而超然的眼睛的镜面中。\"\n    -Douglas Adams, \"Last Chance to See\". 1990.","reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["保留现译；会继续留下已由英文正文或当前术语直接证明的偏差。"],"resolved_key":"komodo dragon","reviewer_rationale":"unwavering and disinterested 描写目光坚定、超然无私，并非眼睛“不闪烁、没兴趣”；恢复形容词语义。","section":"Non-unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"adjust"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"……克拉肯，亦称蟹鱼，据挪威的引水人说，并没有那么大，因为头和尾巴加在一起，也不比我们的厄兰岛宽多少（即不足16公里）……它栖息在海底，周身始终环绕着无数小鱼，这些小鱼既是它的食物，也反过来受它喂养：因为它的进食——如果我记得E. 蓬托皮丹的记载没错的话——持续时间不超过三个月，之后又需要三个月来消化。它的排泄物随后滋养了一大群小鱼，因此渔夫们会探测它的栖息之处……渐渐地，克拉肯升到海面，当它在十到十二英寻深时，船只最好撤离其附近，因为不久之后它便会破水而出，像一座浮动的岛屿，从它那可怕的鼻孔中喷出水流，掀起环状的巨浪，能够波及数英里之远。谁能怀疑这就是约伯记中的利维坦呢？\"\n    -Jacob Wallenberg, \"Min son på galejan\". 1781.","current_english":"“... Kraken, also called the Crab-fish, which [according to the pilots of\n Norway] is not that huge, for heads and tails counted, he is no larger than\n our Öland is wide [i.e. less than 16 km] ... He stays at the sea floor,\n constantly surrounded by innumerable small fishes, who serve as his food and\n are fed by him in return: for his meal, if I remember correctly what E.\n Pontoppidan writes, lasts no longer than three months, and another three are\n then needed to digest it. His excrements nurture in the following an army of\n lesser fish, and for this reason, fishermen plumb after his resting place ...\n Gradually, Kraken ascends to the surface, and when he is at ten to twelve\n fathoms, the boats had better move out of his vicinity, as he will shortly\n thereafter burst up, like a floating island, spurting water from his dreadful\n nostrils and making ring waves around him, which can reach many miles. Could\n one doubt that this is the Leviathan of Job?”\n    -Jacob Wallenberg, “Min son på galejan”. 1781.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；专名例外复用 Issue #3、QUOTE_NAME_EXCEPTIONS 与 docs/decisions.md，未重新裁定。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Non-unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:3295","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:2773","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"7e34eacdbd62d8ec334616c2d632a5ad754784f58d1c5e14c707f95f6ff0122c","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:kraken","key":"kraken","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":"The Norse creature is referred to by its established proper name 克拉肯.","producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"kraken","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Non-unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}

{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"当然，若我们将龙或林德虫想象为这样一种怪兽——它长长的脖颈末端长着鹰头、狮头或海豚头；宽阔的背上长着狮鹫或纳希翼；在层层盘卷的尾巴上长着带倒钩的毒刺；口吐火焰；爱上少女并将她们掳走；时而化作这种形态，时而变作那种；盘踞在辛苦积攒的财宝之上——简而言之，若将其想象为一头拥有寓言赋予它的一切特征的怪兽，那么相信龙和林德虫便是疯狂的。但若我们仅仅将它视为一种可怕的、如今已从我们大陆上灭绝的怪兽，那么对它存在的信念便毫无可笑之处。\"\n    -Leopold Ziegelhauſer, _Schattenbilder der Vorzeit: Ein Kranz von\n     Geschichten, Sagen, Legenden, Märchen, Skizzen und Heldenmahlen, Aus allen\n     Gegenden Deutschlands und des österreichischen Kaiserstaates_. 1844.","current_english":"“Freilich verbürgt uns keine Silbe die Existenz von solcherlei Thieren, wenn\n wir uns den Drachen oder Lindwurm als ein Ungeheuer vorstellen, dessen langer\n Hals in einen Adler-, Löwen- oder Delphinenkopf endigt; das auf dem breiten\n Rücken Greifs- oder Nachisittige trägt; und am vielfach\n gerollten Schweif einen Stachel mit Widerhaken hat; Feuer speit; sich in\n Mädchen verliebt und diese entführt; bald diese bald jene Gestalt annimmt; auf\n sauer erworbenen Schatzen ruht — kurz, als ein Ungeheuer, das alle\n Eigenschaften besitzt, welche die Fabel ihm andichtet; dann wäre es Wahnsinn,\n an Drachen und Lindwürmer glauben zu wollen. Nehmen wir aber dafür bloß ein\n furchtbares Ungeheuer überhaupt, welches nun aus unserem Welttheile vertilgt\n ist, so hat der Glaube daran nichts Lächerliches.”\n    -Leopold Ziegelhauſer, _Schattenbilder der Vorzeit: Ein Kranz von\n     Geschichten, Sagen, Legenden, Märchen, Skizzen und Heldenmahlen, Aus allen\n     Gegenden Deutschlands und des österreichischen Kaiserstaates_. 1844.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；专名例外复用 Issue #3、QUOTE_NAME_EXCEPTIONS 与 docs/decisions.md，未重新裁定。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Non-unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:3316","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:2782","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"f4f94fae50532b85249eae1a6a6aac46869abd78775f8c5c2ea8c05e885404f4","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:lindwurm","key":"lindwurm","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":"The quotation preserves the Germanic creature's proper name.","producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"lindwurm","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Non-unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"她在暮光中行走，脚步声无声，\n她的脚在露水覆盖的地上不留痕迹。\n她轻轻招手，低唤你的名字——\n但那些跟她而去的人从此不再一样。\"\n    -Magic: the Gathering","current_english":"“She walks in the twilight, her steps make no sound,\n  Her feet leave no tracks on the dew-covered ground.\n Her hand gently beckons, she whispers your name—\n  But those who go with her are never the same.”\n    -Magic: the Gathering","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Non-unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:3333","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:2789","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"c2d2db8ac8fbaf7e2bb639171afde47ee388137ea95d21e423fbdc3cb044e8d4","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:lost soul","key":"lost soul","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":"\"她在暮光中行走，脚步悄无声息，\n她的脚在露水覆盖的地上不留痕迹。\n她轻轻招手，低唤你的名字——\n但那些跟她而去的人从此不再一样。\"\n    -Magic: the Gathering","reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["保留现译；会继续留下已由英文正文或当前术语直接证明的偏差。"],"resolved_key":"lost soul","reviewer_rationale":"steps make no sound 应译为脚步悄无声息；原译“脚步声无声”成分重复。","section":"Non-unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"adjust"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"克特西亚斯记载说，在埃塞俄比亚还有一种野兽，他称之为曼提科拉，生有三排牙齿，合拢时彼此相错如同梳齿；长着人的脸和耳朵，眼睛赤红；肤色血红；身躯似狮；尾巴上长着一根蝎子般的毒刺；其声音如同笛与号同时吹奏；它极为敏捷，而一切肉食中它最渴望人肉。\"\n    -Pliny the Elder, _Natural History_, Book 8, Chapter XXI","current_english":"“Ctesias writeth, that in Aethiopia likewise there is a beast which he calleth\n Mantichora, having three rankes of teeth, which when they meet togither are\n let in one within another like the teeth of combes: with the face and eares of\n a man, with red eyes; of colour sanguine, bodied like a lyon, and having a\n taile armed with a sting like a scorpion: his voice resembleth the noise of a\n flute and trumpet sounded together: very swift he is, and mans flesh of all\n others hee most desireth.”\n    -Pliny the Elder, _Natural History_, Book 8, Chapter XXI","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Non-unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:3341","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:2797","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"d04a623583fd14251ab58fa4ecd6386e4920a24743647bea6f884bf6c60ab545","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:manticore","key":"manticore","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"manticore","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Non-unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"天神来了，带来了黑夜，渴望着爱情，他躺在大地身上，将自己完全铺展在她身上。于是那儿子从埋伏之处伸出了左手，右手拿起那把带有锯齿的巨大的长镰刀，迅速割下了他父亲的下体，将它们扔在身后。它们没有徒然地从他手中落下；大地承接了所有喷涌而出的血滴，随着季节流转，她生下了强大的厄里倪厄斯，和穿着闪亮铠甲、手持长矛的巨大巨人，以及那些在整个无边大地上被称作墨利埃的宁芙们。\"\n    -Hesiod, _Theogony_, 182-187, trans. H.G. Evelyn-White. 1920","current_english":"“And Heaven came, bringing on night and longing for love, and he lay about Earth\n spreading himself full upon her. Then the son from his ambush stretched forth\n his left hand and in his right took the great long sickle with jagged teeth,\n and swiftly lopped off his own father's members and cast them away to fall\n behind him. And not vainly did they fall from his hand; for all the bloody\n drops that gushed forth Earth received, and as the seasons moved round she bare\n the strong Erinyes and the great Giants with gleaming armour, holding long\n spears in their hands, and the Nymphs whom they call Meliae all over the\n boundless earth.”\n    -Hesiod, _Theogony_, 182-187, trans. H.G. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"终于我的思绪渐渐复苏，\n当我发现自己已不再是原来模样；\n首先我感到这海绿色的胡须开始生长，\n这两鬓也获得了这宽大的殊荣；\n我长长的发绺在波浪中飘拂，\n我宽阔的肩膀划开柔软的深海；\n我鱼形的尾巴，我蔚蓝的双臂，\n每一部分都已神圣地改变，我都看在眼里。\"\n    -Ovid, _Metamorphoses_, XIII, 546-7. 8 A.D.\n     trans. Garth, Dryden, et al.","current_english":"“Again at length my thought reviving came,\n When I no longer found my self the same;\n Then first this sea-green beard I felt to grow,\n And these large honours on my spreading brow;\n My long-descending locks the billows sweep,\n And my broad shoulders cleave the yielding deep;\n My fishy tail, my arms of azure hue,\n And ev'ry part divinely chang'd, I view.”\n    -Ovid, _Metamorphoses_, XIII, 546-7. 8 A.D.\n     trans. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"主啊，主啊！我想，溺水是多么痛苦啊！\n耳中听到的是多么可怕的水声！\n眼前看到的是多么丑陋的死亡景象！\n我想我看见了上千艘可怕的沉船，\n上万个被鱼啃食的人，\n大块的金锭，巨大的铁锚，成堆的珍珠，\n无价的宝石，无法估量的珠玉，\n全都散落在海底：\n有些躺在死人头骨里；而在那些\n曾是眼睛栖居的窟窿中，\n仿佛是对眼睛的嘲弄，嵌着反光的宝石。\"\n    -William Shakespeare, _King Richard III_, I, iv. 1597","current_english":"“Lord, Lord! methought, what pain it was to drown!\n What dreadful noise of waters in mine ears!\n What ugly sights of death within mine eyes!\n Methought I saw a thousand fearful wrecks,\n Ten thousand men that fishes gnawed upon,\n Wedges of gold, great anchors, heaps of pearl,\n Inestimable stones, unvalued jewels,\n All scattered in the bottom of the sea:\n Some lay in dead men’s skulls; and in those holes\n Where eyes did once inhabit, there were crept,\n As ’t were in scorn of eyes, reflecting gems.”\n    -William Shakespeare, _King Richard III_, I, iv. 1597","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Non-unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:3378","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:2820","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"9a9c8c815a43116b2441c0187466b80e14a87a073ebf72f83bdd8369be169dac","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:merfolk aquamancer","key":"merfolk aquamancer","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"merfolk aquamancer","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Non-unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"于是美人鱼从海中升起，\n手中拿着梳子和镜子：\n'敬你们一杯，我快乐的年轻人们，\n因为你们再也见不到陆地了。'\"\n    -\"Sir Patrick Spens\", Scottish folk song, version 58J in Francis James\n     Child, _The English and Scottish Popular Ballads_. 1898.","current_english":"“Then up it raise the mermaiden,\n Wi the comb an glass in her hand:\n ‘Here's a health to you, my merrie young men,\n For you never will see dry land.’”\n    -“Sir Patrick Spens”, Scottish folk song, version 58J in Francis James\n     Child, _The English and Scottish Popular Ballads_. 1898.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Non-unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:3393","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:2835","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"96ca1a184e0ec445eb9d8b81c52e38c4428785d315ae2162d2180d381d1832bc","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:merfolk avatar","key":"merfolk avatar","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"merfolk avatar","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Non-unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"该死的！让尼普顿劈死你，温斯洛！听啊！听啊，特里同，听啊！咆哮吧，叫我们的父海王从深渊中升起，满怀着狂暴的愤怒！黑色的巨浪翻腾着盐沫，用辛辣的黏液窒息这张年轻的嘴，噎死你，塞满你的器官直到你发青浮肿，被舱底污水和盐水填满，再也不能尖叫——只等他头戴蛤壳冠，拖着黏滑蜿蜒的触手尾和冒着热气的胡须，举起他那可怕的生着鱼鳍的手臂，他那珊瑚齿的三叉戟在风暴中发出女妖般的尖啸，径直刺穿你的喉咙，将你爆开——不再是一团鼓胀的水泡，而是一层爆碎的血色薄膜——一滩虚无，供哈尔庇厄和死去水手的幽灵啄食、抓挠、吞食，最终被那可畏的帝王本人的无尽海水舔食吞咽——从此被所有人遗忘，被所有时间遗忘，被任何神祇或魔鬼遗忘，甚至被大海遗忘，因为温斯洛的任何部分、任何碎片，你灵魂的任何碎屑都不再是温斯洛，而是此刻已然成为大海！\"\n    -Thomas Wake, _The Lighthouse_. 2019","current_english":"“Damn ye! Let Neptune strike ye dead, Winslow! HAAARK! Hark Triton, hark!\n Bellow, bid our father the Sea King rise from the depths full foul in his fury!\n Black waves teeming with salt-foam to smother this young mouth with pungent\n slime, to choke ye, engorging your organs ‘til ye turn blue and bloated with\n bilge and brine and can scream no more -- only when he, crowned in cockle\n shells with slitherin’ tentacled tail and steaming beard take up his fell\n be-finnéd arm, his coral-tine trident screeches banshee-like in the tempest and\n plunges right through yer gullet, bursting ye -- a bulging bladder no more, but\n a blasted bloody film now -- a nothing for the harpies and the souls of dead\n sailors to peck and claw and feed upon, only to be lapped up and swallowed by\n the infinite waters of the dread emperor himself -- forgotten to any man, to\n any time, forgotten to any god or devil, forgotten even to the sea, for any\n stuff or part of Winslow, even any scantling of your soul is Winslow no more,\n but is now itself the sea!”\n    -Thomas Wake, _The Lighthouse_. 2019","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"向前划行，进入一道直立声音的传及范围，\n塞壬们很快便察觉了我们，毫无声息，\n她们调谐了那魔力强大的甜美音调，\n口中吟唱出塞壬的歌谣：\n'来吧，你，值得全世界赞美之人，\n你如此高高地擢升了希腊的荣耀，\n尤利西斯！停下你的船，聆听那支歌，\n从未有人经过却不让它弯转了自己的耳朵，\n都沉醉离去，从我们这里获得的教益\n比从前任何人听闻的都更多。\n因为我们知晓 在广阔的特洛伊土地上\n所发生的一切；知晓在那些\n由诸神命定的崇高事件中，\n希腊人和特洛伊人双方所承受的一切。\n我们也知晓大地上所能展现的一切，\n来让一种对功绩的认知臻于完满。'\"\n    -Homer, _The Odyssey_, XII, 268-82.\n     trans. George Chapman, 1857.","current_english":"“Row'd on, in reach of an erected voice,\n The Sirens soon took note, without our noise,\n Tuned those sweet accents that made charms so strong,\n And these learn'd numbers made the Sirens' song:\n ‘Come here, thou worthy of a world of praise,\n That dost so high the Grecian glory raise,\n Ulysses! stay thy ship, and that song hear\n That none past ever but it bent his ear,\n But left him ravish'd, and instructed more\n By us, than any ever heard before.\n For we know all things whatsoever were\n In wide Troy labour'd; whatsoever there\n The Grecians and the Trojans both sustain'd\n By those high issues that the Gods ordain'd.\n And whatsoever all the earth can show\n T' inform a knowledge of desert, we know.’”\n    -Homer, _The Odyssey_, XII, 268-82.\n     trans. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"我看见埃及和埃及人——我看见金字塔和方尖碑；\n我看见刻在沙石板、或花岗岩块上的\n凿刻的历史、歌谣、哲思；\n我在孟菲斯看见木乃伊坑，里面有木乃伊，涂抹了防腐香料，\n裹着亚麻布，在那里躺了许多世纪；\n我望着倒下的底比斯人，圆睁的眼球，歪斜的脖颈，\n双手交叉在胸前。\"\n    -Walt Whitman, \"Salut au Monde\"","current_english":"“I see Egypt and the Egyptians — I see the pyramids and obelisks;\n I look on chisel'd histories, songs, philosophies, cut in slabs\n     of {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"在天神、阿修罗和天仙之中，可爱的女士啊，纳迦被赋予了巨大的能量。他们拥有极快的速度，又被赋予了极佳的芬芳。他们值得被崇拜。他们能够赐予恩惠。确实，我们也值得被别人追随左右。我告诉你，女士啊，人类是无法看见我们的。\"\n    -Mahābhārata, Santi Parva, Mokshadharma Parva, section CCCLX. ca. 500 B.C.\n     trans. Kisari Mohan Ganguli, 1883.","current_english":"“Amongst the deities and Asuras and celestial Rishis, O amiable lady, the Nagas\n are endued with great energy. Possessed of great speed, they are endued again\n with excellent fragrance. They deserve to be worshipped. They are capable of\n granting boons. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"然后，格得双臂之间那片灰黄的椭圆变得明亮起来。它扩大、延展，成了大地与黑夜的黑暗中一道裂口，撕裂了世界的织体。从中迸射出一道可怕的光芒。而通过那道明亮的、畸形的裂缝，有什么东西攀爬了出来，像一团黑色的阴影凝块，迅捷而丑恶，径直扑向格得的面孔。\"\n    -Ursula K. Le Guin, _A Wizard Of Earthsea_. 1968.","current_english":"“Then the sallow oval between Ged's arms grew bright. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"即使此刻，他那可怖力量的强度仍能被感知到\n削弱着身体，使心灵哀伤\n最终他们将化为空洞的、无意识的幽灵\n被剥夺了意志和灵魂\n只有他们对自由的渴望\n赋予他们复仇的饥渴……\"\n    -Rush, \"The Necromancer\". 1975.","current_english":"“Even now the intensity of his dread power can be felt\n Weakening the body and saddening the heart\n Ultimately they will become empty, mindless spectres\n Stripped of will and soul\n Only their thirst for freedom\n Gives them hunger for vengeance...”\n    -Rush, “The Necromancer”. 1975.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；专名例外复用 Issue #3、QUOTE_NAME_EXCEPTIONS 与 docs/decisions.md，未重新裁定。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Non-unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:3511","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:2929","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"9c1b95bc045ea75371d48caab7018d4c504fafaa52ef6823aa98b189a2cc16c4","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:necromancer","key":"necromancer","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":"The occurrence is only in a cited song title.","producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"necromancer","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Non-unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}

{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"如果突变因此受自然选择的影响，这就意味着任何特定的突变必然朝着对相应物种有利的方向进展；而实际上，化石动物中已知的许多突变例子，显然都是由于这种变化带来的优势所致。然而，我必须在此补充说明，并非所有的突变（就古生物学的意义而言）都归因于自然选择，许多突变并不意味着实际的改良。\"\n    -_Proceedings of the American Philosophical Society_, Volume XXV, no. 150.\n     1896.","current_english":"“If thus mutation is influenced by natural selection, it implies, that any\n particular mutation must advance in a direction advantageous for the\n respective species, and, indeed, many examples of mutation known among fossil\n animals are apparently due to the advantage produced by the change. I must add\n here, however that probably not all mutations (in a palaeontological meaning)\n are due to natural selection, but that many do not imply an actual\n improvement.”\n    -_Proceedings of the American Philosophical Society_, Volume XXV, no. 150.\n     1896.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Non-unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:3527","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:2944","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"f8214dd9e6887992e38d4d7f0a7aab829f4a6f2252af22f09382e8fbbea63f5a","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:neqoxec","key":"neqoxec","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"neqoxec","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Non-unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}

{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"在他们短暂的相处中，斯洛思罗普形成的印象是这只章鱼\n 精神健康状况不佳——尽管他有什么比较的依据呢？但这种\n 软体动物的每一个动作中都有一种疯狂的亢奋，就像当我们\n 对噪音和自己的笨拙格外敏感、不希望东西掉落时，那些\n 无生命的物体偏偏从桌上掉下来——一种'砰！哈哈你听到\n 了吗？又来了，砰！'的感觉——斯洛思罗普终于像掷铁饼\n 一样用尽全身力气把螃蟹抛向大海，得以脱身；而那只章鱼\n 则急切地溅起水花、咕噜作响，紧追不舍，随即消失不见。\"\n    -托马斯·品钦，《万有引力之虹》。1973年。","current_english":"“In their brief time together Slothrop forms the impression that this octopus\n is not in good mental health, though where's his basis for comparing? But\n there is a mad exhuberance, as with inanimate objects which fall off of tables\n when we are sensitive to noise and our own clumsiness and don't want them to\n fall, a sort of wham! ha-ha you hear that? here it is again, WHAM! in the\n cephalopod's every movement, which Slothrop is glad to get away from as he\n finally scales the crab like a discus, with all his strength, out to sea, and\n the octopus, with an eager splash and gurgle, strikes out in pursuit, and is\n presently gone.”\n    -Thomas Pynchon, _Gravity's Rainbow_. 1973.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Non-unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:3545","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:2956","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"94a50c77bdb95595032e55608cd91f28e3bf835ee4f5c5f653ca949ea77483ac","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:octopode","key":"octopode","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"octopode","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Non-unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"小公主在摇篮中沉睡，漂浮在水面上，最终被冲上了一片\n 美丽国度的海岸。然而，那里几乎无人居住，因为食人魔\n 拉瓦乔和他的妻子图尔芒蒂娜搬到那里去了——他们把\n 所有人都吃掉了。食人魔是可怕的生物。一旦尝过生人肉，\n 他们几乎就不愿再吃别的东西了；而图尔芒蒂娜总有办法\n 让人送上门来，因为她有一半仙女的血液。\"\n    -玛丽-卡特琳·勒朱梅尔·德·巴纳维尔，奥努瓦男爵夫人，\n    《橙子树与蜜蜂》。1697年。\n\n\"不。层次。洋葱有层次。食人魔也有层次。洋葱有层次。\n 懂了吗？我们都有层次。\"\n    -史莱克。2001年。","current_english":"“The little princess, asleep in her cradle, floated on the water, and at last\n she was cast up on the shore of a beautiful country, where, however, very few\n people dwelt since the ogre Ravagio and his wife Tourmentine had gone to live\n there-for they ate up everybody. Ogres are terrible people. When once they\n have tasted raw human flesh they will hardly eat anything else, and\n Tourmentine always knew how to make some body come their way, for she was half\n a fairy.”\n    -Marie-Catherine Le Jumel de Barneville, Baronne d'Aulnoy, “'Orangier et\nl'Abeille”. 1697.\n\n“NO. Layers. Onions have layers. Ogres have layers. Onions have layers. You get\n it? We both have layers.”\n    -Shrek. 2001.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Non-unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:3558","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:2968","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"9ad29d8e6dbb324fa125ce583299e207f269991a4870ce3432b55e2dfaa222f9","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:ogre","key":"ogre","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"ogre","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Non-unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}

{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"碳酸是构成植物生长起点的三种材料之一，另外两种是水\n 和硝酸。这种酸由碳和氧按一比二的比例化合而成。它是\n 一种无色气体，具有酸味和酸臭；可溶于水；比空气重\n 一半，可以像液体一样从一个容器倒入另一个容器；一百\n 份水可溶解一百零六份这种气体，植物的根系正是由此\n 获得所需的供应。\"\n    -亨利·斯图尔特，《农作物的栽培：农业科学手册与美国\n     农民实用指南》，第十章。1887年。","current_english":"“Carbonic acid is one of the three materials which together form the starting\n point of vegetable growth; the others being water and nitric acid. This acid is\n formed of carbon and oxygen in the proportion of one part of the former to\n two of the latter chemically combined. It is a colorless gas, having an acid\n taste and smell; is soluble in water; weighs one-half more than air and can be\n poured from one vessel to another, as a liquid may be; 100 parts of water\n dissolve 106 parts of this gas, and it is from this source that the roots of\n plants derive the needed supplies of it.”\n    -Henry Stuart, _The Culture of Farm Crops: A Manual of the Science of\n    Agriculture, and a Hand-book of Practice for American Farmers_, ch X. 1887.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Non-unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:3578","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:2987","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"6fe94d7949bee29a435fd8e2c62fc22c2e85b58efef0bfb30c27d9d2e05c51ae","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:oklob plant","key":"oklob plant","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"oklob plant","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Non-unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}

{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"鬼神话的流行与经久不衰，无疑部分基于这些生物一贯的\n 恶魔特征——这些特征历经岁月而相对恒定：它们是从佛教\n 地狱深渊中涌现的可怕超自然存在，来恐吓邪恶的凡人；\n 它们怪异而凶残的举止和外形令人瞬间生畏；而鬼在日本\n 社会历史与文化档案中的无所不在，则直接归因于鬼的故事\n 所产生的道德、社会和宗教教化作用。\"\n    -诺里科·T·赖德，《日本恶魔传说：从古至今的鬼》，\n     第一章，2010年。","current_english":"“The popularity and longevity of the oni myth is no doubt partially based on\n the beings’ conventional demonic accoutrements, which have remained\n relatively constant through the ages: they are dreadful super-natural\n beings emerging from the abyss of Buddhist hell to terrify wicked mortals;\n their grotesque and savage demeanor and form instill instant fear; and the\n oni’s omnipresence in the socio-historical and cultural archive of Japan is\n directly attributable to the moral, social, and religious edification that\n stories about oni engender.”\n    -Noriko T. Reider, _Japanese Demon Lore: Oni from Ancient Times to the\n     Present_, ch. 1, 2010.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Non-unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:3595","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:3002","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"fd0f8adb3e4820b71dd3671413b556438ca0ec54ba9d40ea5523de60f7fc1979","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:oni incarcerator","key":"oni incarcerator","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"oni incarcerator","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Non-unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"我观看活物的时候，见活物的脸旁，各有一轮在地上。\n 轮的形状和构造，好像苍翠的水苍玉。四轮都是一个样式，\n 形状和构造，好像轮中套轮。轮行走的时候，向四方都能\n 直行，并不掉转。至于轮辋，高而可畏；四个轮辋周围\n 满有眼睛。\"\n    -以西结书 1:15-18（和合本）","current_english":"“As I looked at the living creatures, I saw a wheel on the ground beside\n each creature with its four faces. This was the appearance and structure\n of the wheels: They sparkled like chrysolite, and all four looked alike.\n Each appeared to be made like a wheel intersecting a wheel. As they\n moved, they would go in any one of the four directions the creatures faced;\n the wheels did not turn about as the creatures went. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"与此同时，奉至上大能的谕令，\n 插翅使者们以威严的仪式\n 和响彻全军的号角之声宣告\n 即刻在撒旦及其群臣的巍峨都城——\n 万魔殿——召开庄严会议……\"\n    -约翰·弥尔顿，《失乐园》，第一卷，1667年。","current_english":"“Mean while the winged heralds by command\n Of Sovran power, with awful Ceremony\n And Trumpets sound throughout the Host proclaim\n A solemn Councel forthwith to be held\n At Pandaemonium, the high Capital\n Of Satan and his Peers...”\n    -John Milton, _Paradise Lost_, Book I, 1667.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Non-unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:3647","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:3050","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"9b6dd5e6ccba71a0c786e09f57c779f645585c4d74d85092921cb585864405ec","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:pandemonium lord","key":"pandemonium lord","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"pandemonium lord","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Non-unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}

{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"北极熊是一种力量和凶猛都极为惊人的动物。巴伦支在寻找\n 通往中国的东北航道的航行中，在新地岛上亲历了这些动物\n 的残暴：它们袭击他的船员，用嘴叼住他们，轻而易举地将\n 他们拖走，在同伴的注视下将他们吞噬。据说它们会攻击并\n 试图登上远离海岸的武装船只，有时极难将其击退。\"\n    -乔治·肖，《普通动物学，或称系统自然史》，第一卷，\n     第二部分。1800年。","current_english":"“The Polar Bear is an animal of tremendous strength and fierceness. Barentz, in\n his voyage in search of a north-east passage to China, had proofs of the\n ferocity of these animals, in the island of Nova Zembla, where they attacked\n his seamen, seizing them in their mouths; carrying them off with the utmost\n ease, and devouring them in the sight of their comrades. It is said that they\n will attack and attempt to board armed vessels, at a great distance from\n shore, and have sometimes been with much difficulty repelled.”\n    -George Shaw, _General Zoology, or, Systematic Natural History_, vol. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"我呼唤普罗透斯，\n 执掌钥匙的沧海之主，\n 首生之神，他揭示了\n 万物的起源，\n 将物质变换为\n 千姿百态。\n 受万有敬仰，他通晓智慧，\n 知道现在之事，\n 过去之事，\n 以及未来之事。\n 他掌管一切，\n 其变化远超\n 栖居于白雪皑皑的奥林匹斯、\n 翱翔于天空、\n 跨越大地与海洋的\n 所有不朽者，\n 因为菲西斯最先\n 将万物置于普罗透斯之中。\n 父神啊，在神圣天命的随侍下，\n 请降临于秘仪的信徒，\n 为勤劳富足的一生\n 带来善终。\"\n    -《俄耳甫斯赞美诗》，第二十五首。\n     阿波斯托洛斯·N·阿萨纳萨基斯与本杰明·M·沃尔科夫英译，2013年。","current_english":"“I call upon Proteus,\n key-holding master of the sea,\n first-born, who showed\n the beginnings of all nature,\n changing matter\n into a great variety of forms.\n Honored by all, he is wise,\n and he knows what is now,\n what was before,\n and what will be in the future.\n He has all at his disposal,\n transformed far beyond\n all other immortals\n who dwell on snowy Olympos\n and fly through the air\n and over land and sea,\n for Physis was the first to place\n everything in Proteus.\n Father, attended by holy providence,\n visit the mystic initiates,\n and bring a good end to a life\n of industry and prosperity.”\n    -_The Orphic Hymns_, XXV.\n     trans. Apostolos N. Athanassakis and Benjamin M. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"毗耶娑说：'离般度族安睡之地不远，有一个名叫希丁巴的\n 罗刹栖居在娑罗树上。他力大无比，勇猛非凡，是个残忍的\n 食人者，因牙齿尖长而面目狰狞。此刻他饥肠辘辘，渴望\n 人肉。他腿长腹大，须发皆为赤红。双肩宽阔如树干；双耳\n 如箭簇，容貌可怖。'\"\n    -《摩诃婆罗多》，初篇，希丁巴伏诛篇，第一百五十四章。\n     约公元前500年。基萨里·莫汉·甘古利英译，1883年。","current_english":"“Vaisampayana said, 'Not far from the place where the Pandavas were asleep, a\n Rakshasa by name Hidimva dwelt on the Sala tree. Of long\n shanks and a large belly, his locks and beard were both red in hue. His\n shoulders were broad like the neck of a tree; his ears were like unto arrows,\n and his features were frightful.”\n    -_Mahābhārata_, Adi Parva, Hidimva-vadha Parva, section CLIV. ca. 500 B.C.\n     trans. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"我们的时光都到了。\n 方才还在，而今已逝。\n 四季不畏惧死神，\n 风、太阳和雨也一样。\n 我们可以像它们那样。\n 来吧宝贝，别畏惧死神。\"\n    -巴克·达摩，Blue Öyster Cult，《（别畏惧）死神》。1976年。","current_english":"“All our times have come.\n Here but now they're gone.\n Seasons don't fear the reaper,\n Nor do the wind, the sun or the rain.\n We can be like they are.\n Come on baby, don't fear the reaper.”\n\n    -Buck Dharma, Blue Öyster Cult, “(Don't Fear) The Reaper”. 1976.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；专名例外复用 Issue #3、QUOTE_NAME_EXCEPTIONS 与 docs/decisions.md，未重新裁定。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Non-unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:3747","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:3145","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"cb81063b2309708d82e308379a2fd8ba9a028a9e2e2502ec7de9d5d5824f92e7","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:reaper","key":"reaper","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":"The quotation personifies Death as the Grim Reaper.","producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"reaper","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Non-unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}

{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"例如：沙罗曼达形似蜥蜴，遍体斑点如星，从不外出，\n 只在暴雨中现身；因为晴朗天气里是见不到它的。其性\n 至寒，若触火，则火即灭，犹如投冰入火。沙罗曼达口中\n 吐出一种如奶的毒液，一旦触及男女身体任何裸露之处，\n 毛发尽落；被触之处肤色变为白斑。\"\n    -盖乌斯·普林尼乌斯·塞昆杜斯，《自然史》，第十卷，\n     第六十七章。公元79年。菲莱蒙·霍兰德英译，1601年。","current_english":"“As for example: the Salamander made in fashion of a Lizard, marked with spots\n like to stars, never comes abroad and sheweth it selfe but in great showers;\n for in faire weather he is not seene. He is of so cold a complexion, that if\n hee do but touch the fire, hee wil quench it as presently, as if yce were put\n into it. The Salamander casteth up at the mouth a certaine venomous matter\n like unto milke, let it but once touch any bare part of a man or womans bodie,\n all the haire will fall off: and the part so touched will change the colour of\n the skin to the white morphew.”\n    -Gaius Plinius Secundus, _Naturalis Historia_, Book X, ch. LXVII. 79 A.D.\n     trans. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"当乌西雅王崩的那年，我见主坐在高高的宝座上。他的衣裳\n 垂下，遮满圣殿。\n\n 其上有撒拉弗侍立，各有六个翅膀：用两个翅膀遮脸，两个\n 翅膀遮脚，两个翅膀飞翔。\n\n 彼此呼喊说：圣哉！圣哉！圣哉！万军之耶和华，他的荣光\n 充满全地！\"\n    -和合本圣经，以赛亚书 6:1-3。","current_english":"“In the year that king Uzziah died I saw also the LORD sitting upon a throne,\n high and lifted up, and his train filled the temple.\n\n Above it stood the seraphims: each one had six wings; {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"于是那古老的海神从深处\n 爬出，找到了他的肥牛犊，\n 审视、清点，却从未接近\n 我们设下的计谋，只当那是五头牛犊。\n 他用睡眠舒缓了自己的额角；\n 我们高喊可怖的呼号，一拥而上，\n 奋力用双手从四面八方抱住他；\n 于是那古老的变形者开始施展他所有的形态：\n 首先是一头鬃毛雄壮的狮子，\n 接着是一条龙，然后是一头斑豹，\n 然后是一头巨野猪，又骤然化作\n 一片流水。最后他变成一棵树，\n 树冠卷曲，直冲云霄。\"\n    -荷马，《奥德赛》，第四卷，602-614行。\n     乔治·查普曼英译，1857年。","current_english":"“And then th' old Sea-God crept\n From forth the deeps, and found his fat calves there,\n Survey'd, and number'd, and came never near\n The craft we used, but told us five for calves.\n His temples then dis-eased with sleep he salves;\n And in rush'd we, with an abhorred cry,\n Cast all our hands about him manfully;\n And then th' old Forger all his forms began:\n First was a lion with a mighty mane,\n Then next a dragon, a pied panther then,\n A vast boar next, and suddenly did strain\n All into water. Last he was a tree,\n Curl'd all at top, and shot up to the sky.”\n    -Homer, _The Odyssey_, IV, 602-14.\n     trans. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"有一种寂静，那里从未有过声响，\n 有一种寂静，那里不可能有声响，——\n 在冰冷的坟墓中，在深深的、深深的海底，\n 或在没有生命的辽阔荒漠里。\"\n    -托马斯·胡德，《寂静》。19世纪初。","current_english":"“There is a silence where hath been no sound,\n There is a silence where no sound may be,—\n In the cold grave, under the deep, deep sea,\n Or in the wide desert where no life is found.”\n    -Thomas Hood, “Silence”. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"我看到一张嘴在嘲讽。熔化的红铁般的笑容从上面掠过。它\n 的笑声满是钉子叮当碰撞。那是孩子梦中才会出现的嘴。\n 一只拳头击中了那张嘴：由电动的腕和肩驱动的炮铜指节。\n 那是孩子梦中才会出现的手臂。\n 拳头一次又一次、一遍又一遍地击打着那张嘴。嘴里流出\n 熔化的铁血，发出钉子叮当碰撞的笑声。\n 而我看到，拳头捶打得越狠，那张嘴笑得越响。拳头不停地\n 捶打着，捶打着，嘴巴回应着。\"\n    -卡尔·桑德堡，《滴水兽》，《玉米剥皮者》。1918年。","current_english":"“I saw a mouth jeering. A smile of melted red iron ran over it. Its laugh was\n full of nails rattling. It was a child's dream of a mouth.\n A fist hit the mouth: knuckles of gun-metal driven by an electric wrist and\n shoulder. It was a child's dream of an arm.\n The fist hit the mouth over and over, again and again. The mouth bled melted\n iron, and laughed its laughter of nails rattling.\n And I saw the more the fist pounded the more the mouth laughed. The fist is\n pounding and pounding, and the mouth answering.”\n    -Carl Sandburg, “Gargoyle”, _Cornhusker_. 1918.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Non-unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:3886","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:3275","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"684b0eb466ab5de032e7547203d75568ddb06a23c970a25bde101a056248ba00","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:sixfirhy","key":"sixfirhy","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"sixfirhy","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Non-unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"说吧！说吧！你这可怕的来客！\n 你胸中空空，\n 却仍披着粗陋的战甲，\n 前来惊吓我！\n 未曾裹以东方香膏，\n 却伸出你无肉的掌心，\n 仿佛在乞求施舍，\n 你为何萦绕我不散？\"\n    -亨利·沃兹沃思·朗费罗，《披甲骷髅》。1841年。","current_english":"“Speak! speak! thou fearful guest!\n Who, with thy hollow breast\n Still in rude armor drest,\n Comest to daunt me!\n Wrapt not in Eastern balms,\n But with thy fleshless palms\n Stretched, as if asking alms,\n Why dost thou haunt me?”\n    -Henry Wadsworth Longfellow, “The Skeleton in Armor”. 1841.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Non-unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:3898","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:3287","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"6e6db5f3ba79aa5619477d7f2ef401e26dfec05c29750c61e232122cd4dd9087","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:skeletal warrior","key":"skeletal warrior","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"skeletal warrior","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Non-unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"愿上帝拯救我们脱离那\n 端坐于宴席上的骷髅！\"\n    -詹姆斯·杰弗里·罗奇，《宴席上的骷髅》。1890年。","current_english":"“God save us from the skeleton\n Who sitteth at the feast!”\n    -James Jeffrey Roche, _The Skeleton at the Feast_. 1890.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Non-unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:3910","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:3299","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"386a551f47a7da21d9dc677ef1dd936d3a19eb78d6eb23d985270353658aa2eb","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:skeleton","key":"skeleton","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"skeleton","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Non-unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"她的亲生母亲在生命的最后几年里，一直怀着一种可怕的\n 猜疑：电正无形地从房子的每个角落滴漏下来。\"\n    -詹姆斯·瑟伯，《我的人生与艰难岁月》。1934年。","current_english":"“Her own mother lived the latter years of her life in the horrible suspicion\n that electricity was dripping invisibly all over the house.”\n    -James Thurber, _My Life and Hard Times_. 1934.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Non-unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:3916","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:3305","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"089d30baa28c429313453dbe1d476624122e7cceadd5f8de33e242c8b3b99076","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:sky beast","key":"sky beast","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"sky beast","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Non-unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"深渊本身腐烂了：哦基督！\n 这竟会发生！\n 是啊，黏滑的东西用腿爬行\n 在黏滑的海面上。\"\n    -塞缪尔·泰勒·柯勒律治，《古舟子咏》。1798年。","current_english":"“The very deep did rot: O Christ!\n That ever this should be!\n Yea, slimy things did crawl with legs\n Upon the slimy sea.”\n    -Samuel Tayor Coleridge, “The Rime of the Ancient Mariner”. 1798.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Non-unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:3922","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:3311","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"bb234154e9d1a39e64d490fd227e64ac4748e2fbc92f4d9aea8379a522fd0171","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:slime creature","key":"slime creature","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"slime creature","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Non-unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"不——根本不是那样。它无处不在——一种凝胶——一种黏液，\n 却有着形态，千种超出一切记忆的恐怖形态。那里有眼睛——\n 还有一种污迹。那是深渊——大漩涡——终极的憎恶。\n 卡特，那是不可名状之物！\"\n    -H.P.洛夫克拉夫特，《不可名状》。1925年。","current_english":"“No — it wasn't that way at all. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"走了一阵，他们来到了一个村庄。'好吧，'鳄龟说，\n '朋友们，天亮时分，我们就发起进攻。我先去那个地方。'\n 战团的首领对他们说道。\n\n '好，'另一个小家伙说，'你是决定我们该做什么的人，'\n 他们对那只鳄龟说道。'好吧，'鳄龟说，'现在我要\n 告诉你们我该做什么。'他这样说道。'现在，我要开始\n 朝这个村子走了。等我杀死酋长的女儿时，天正好破晓，\n 与此同时，明日来临的方向天空会染红。'嗬，那边，我们\n 的同伴杀了她！'——你们心里要这样想。那时就是你们发出\n 巨大声响的时刻，你们要不停地呐喊。现在就是你们去攻打\n 这个村子的时候。'他如此对他那些年轻人说道。\n\n '好的！'其他小家伙们说道。\"\n    -《鳄龟出征记》，《美国民族学学会出版物，第九卷：\n     基卡普故事集》。1915年。\n     杜鲁门·迈克尔逊英译。","current_english":"“After a while they came to a village. ”Now then,“ said Snapping Turtle, ”in\n the morning at daylight, my friends, we will make on attack. I myself will\n first go to the place,“ the leader of the war party said to them.\n\n ”Good,“ said the other little one, ”thou art the one who sees to it what we\n shall do,“ they said to that Snapping Turtle. ”Now then,“ said Snapping\n Turtle, ”verily I am now going to tell you what I shall do.“ Thus he spoke.\n ”Now is the time I shall begin to walk toward this village. Verily at the time\n I shall kill the daughter of the chief will be when the light of day is\n breaking, and at the same instant the sky will glow with red in the direction\n whence the morrow comes. ‘Ho, there, our comrade has killed her!’ will thus be\n the thought in your hearts. Then is the time when you want to make a great\n noise, when you shall whoop all keep it up. Now is the time that you go to\n attack this village.“ Thus he spoke to those his young men.\n\n ”All right!“ said the other little fellows.”\n    -“When Snapping Turtle went to War”, _Publications of the American\n     Ethnological Society, Volume IX: Kickapoo Tales_. 1915.\n     trans. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"在任何组合中，固体叙述着由虚空生成的异常，或者说，\n 虚空对固体的感染（当虚空抵达固体时，它作用为一种缠绕\n 的瘟疫，一种盘卷翻腾的传染病，而非一种消除过程或固体\n 湮灭剂）；在组合中，没有纯粹的固体，只有被玷污的、\n 染病和被摧残的固体。\n ［……］\n 固体能够启动其构造和组合活动（生存、发展等过程）的\n 唯一方式，就是让虚空进入。固体的动态特征只有在被虚空\n 吞噬、缠绕和搅乱时才能被激活。固体别无选择。\"\n    -雷扎·内加雷斯塔尼，《旋风学：与匿名物质的共谋》。2008年。","current_english":"“In any composition, the solid narrates the anomalies generated by the void, or\n the infection of the void through the solid (where the void comes to the solid,\n it works as a convoluting plague, a coiling swirling epidemic rather than a\n nullifying process or a solid-annihilating agent); in a composition there is no\n pure solid but a defiled one, a diseased and deflowered solid.\n [...]\n The only way that the solid can initialize its architectonic and compositional\n activities (processes for survival, development, etc.) is by letting the void\n in. The dynamic traits of solid can only be actuated when solid is eaten,\n convoluted and messed up by the void. There is no other option for solid.”\n    -Reza Negarestani, _Cyclonopedia: Complicity with Anonymous Materials_. 2008","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Non-unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:3981","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:3368","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"3e8325b46fb389da747aef0e2f53e4a9e6b2fef5c62b4db94a25186406acb9df","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:spatial maelstrom","key":"spatial maelstrom","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"spatial maelstrom","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Non-unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}

{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"俄狄浦斯说：'怯懦的罪恶与耻辱离我很远，我的勇气不\n 知畏惧为何物［……］斯芬克斯，用晦暗的韵律编织着谜语，\n 我并未逃遁；我直面那凶残女预言家的血盆大口和散落白骨\n 染白的土地。当她从高耸的悬崖上，已经在她猎物上方盘旋，\n 张开羽翼，像一头野蛮的狮子般甩动尾巴，激起她威胁的\n 怒火时，我问出了她的谜语。随即传来一阵恐怖的声音；\n 她的下颚嘎吱作响，她的利爪迫不及待，垂涎我的内脏，\n 撕扯着岩石。那命运之神错综复杂、诡计交织的言辞，\n 那有翼野兽的可怕谜语，我解开了。'\"\n    -塞内加，《俄狄浦斯》，公元1世纪。\n     弗兰克·贾斯特斯·米勒英译，1917年。","current_english":"“Oedipus speaks: 'Far from me is the crime and shame of cowardice, and my\n valour knows not dastard fears[...] The Sphinx, weaving her words in darkling\n measures, I fled not; I faced the bloody jaws of the fell prophetess and the\n ground white with scattered bones. And when from a lofty cliff, already\n hovering over her prey, she prepared her pinions and, lashing her tail like\n a savage lion, stirred up her threatening wrath, I asked her riddle.\n Thereupon came a sound of dread; her jaws crashed, and her talons, brooking\n no delay, eager for my vitals, tore at the rocks. The lot's intricate,\n guile-entangled words, the grim riddle of the winged beast, I solved.'”\n    -Seneca, _Oedipus_, 1st cent. A.D.\n     trans. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"'我真的相信你说的话，'骑士答道；'因为，我刚刚与那个\n 巨人经历了一场我平生所经历的最为激烈、最为固执的战斗：\n 反手一击，砰的一声，他的头落到了地上；鲜血四溅，如同\n 溪水般流淌在田野上。'\"\n    -米格尔·德·塞万提斯·萨维德拉，《拉曼查的奇情异想的\n     绅士堂吉诃德》，第四卷第十章。1605年。\n     卡洛斯·富恩特斯英译，1997年。","current_english":"“I really believe what you say, answered the knight; for, I have been engaged\n with the giant, in the most obstinate and outrageous combat that I believe I\n shall ever fight in all the days of my life: with one backstroke, slam went\n his head to the ground; and discharged such a quantity of blood, that it ran\n like rills of water, along the field.”\n    -Miguel de Cervantes Saavedra, _The Ingenious Gentleman Don Quixote of La\n     Mancha_, IV, 10. 1605.\n     trans. {"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"沼泽带着它的秘密是甜美的，\n 直到我们遇见一条蛇；\n 那时我们便叹息着想念房屋，\n 于是拔腿而逃\n\n 以那种只有童年才懂的\n 迷人的飞奔。\n 蛇是夏日的背叛，\n 狡诈随着它的行踪而至。\"\n    -艾米莉·狄金森，《蛇》。约1865年。","current_english":"“Sweet is the swamp with its secrets,\n Until we meet a snake;\n 'T is then we sigh for houses,\n And our departure take\n\n At that enthralling gallop\n That only childhood knows.\n A snake is summer's treason,\n And guile is where it goes.”\n    -Emily Dickinson, “A Snake”. ca. 1865.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Non-unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:4064","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:3449","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"e669a7dcb37eee13f93967cafe5df7025c68843589b18586d98b77388feca48e","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:swamp drake","key":"swamp drake","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"swamp drake","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Non-unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"alias_target":null,"confidence":"high","current_chinese":"\"那愚人，如我所想，在深渊边缘，\n 俯身在沼泽或泥淖之旁，\n 正如我一样，终将欢喜，\n 因为死亡之声必是祂真正的声音。\"\n    -阿瑟·爱德华·韦特，《万物的终结》。1906年。","current_english":"“The fool, as I think, at the chasm's brink,\n Prone by the swamp or the marsh's side,\n Did, even as I, in the end rejoice,\n Since the voice of death must be His true voice.”\n    -Arthur Edward Waite, “At The End of Things”. 1906.","decision_authority":"Issue #72 逐项人工审阅；术语以当前 docs/glossary.md 与 ZH source.txt 为准。","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"Non-unique monsters","display_context":"QuotesDB 直接引文；由帮助/描述界面的物品、法术、能力、怪物或地形详情按英文 canonical key 查询并显示。","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/quotes.txt:4077","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/quotes.txt:3462","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@getQuoteString","docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e"],"fact_sha256":"64309c782c654168357bf49bcc4875005fc9799db4cb9045a31dacb8af471cb8","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"quotes:swamp worm","key":"swamp worm","lifecycle":"direct-quote","literary_exception_reason":null,"producer_consumer":"TextDB(\"quotes\", \"descript/\") → locale overlay descript/zh/quotes.txt → getQuoteString(lowercase key, localized lookup with EN fallback, alias resolution) → quote pane consumers","proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"当该 EN/ZH block、canonical key、别名目标、QuotesDB 加载/查询消费者、Issue #3 专名裁定或 docs/glossary.md 权威变化时重审。","rejected_alternatives":["仅因偏好不同改换文学译本、书名或标点；缺乏可证明的语义收益。"],"resolved_key":"swamp worm","reviewer_rationale":"逐句核对完整英文、当前中文、署名与上下文后，核心命题、专名指代和语气均无可证明偏差；保持现译。","section":"Non-unique monsters","suggestions":[],"terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
